1a1(1): (zhan nas ma bsam mi dge ba'i yon tan ma yin par gsungs so//
1b1(1): @#//rgya gar skad du/Ar+ya lang+ka a ba tA ra ma hA yA na sU tra/bod skad du/'phags pa lang+kar gshegs pa'i theg pa chen po'i mdo/bam po dang po/sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' thams cad la phyag
1b2(1): 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na/bcom ldan 'das lang+ka'i grong rdal na/rgya mtsho'i rim la ya'i rtse mo rin po che'i rigs dang /me tog sna tshogs kyis brgyan pa la dge slong gi dge 'dun chen po dang /byang chub
1b3(1): sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po dang /sangs rgyas kyi zhing sna tshogs pa nas 'dus pa ting nge 'dzin dang /dbang dang /stobs dang /mngon par shes pa du mas rnam par rol pa/byang chub sems dpa' blo gros chen po
1b4(1): la sogs pa dag sangs rgyas thams cad kyi phyag gi dbang bskur bas dbang bskur ba bdag gi sems snang ba'i spyod yul yongs su shes pa'i don la mkhas pa/sems can gyi sems sna tshogs dang /gzugs dang tshul
1b5(1): dang /'dul ba rnam pa tha dad pa'i cha byad 'chang ba/chos lnga dang rang bzhin dang /rnam par shes pa dang /bdag med pa gnyis khong du chud par rtogs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' dag gi tshogs chen po dang thabs cig tu
1b6(1): bzhugs te/yang de na bcom ldan 'das rgya mtsho'i klu'i rgyal po'i gnas nas zhag bdun lon te byung nas brgya byin dang /tshangs pa dang /klu'i bu mo bye ba du mas bsu ba dang lang+ka'i rim la yal gzigs te/lang+ka'i grong rdal gyi
1b7(1): rim la ya'i rtse mo 'dir sngon gyi de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sang+yas rnams kyis kyang 'phags pa so so rang gi sa rig pa'i ye shes/rtog ge'i lta ba mu stegs can dang /nyan thos dang 
1b8(1): rang sangs rgyas dang /'phags pa'i yul ma yin pa de dag gis bsgoms pa'i chos bstan te/ngas kyang de nyid du gnod sbyin gyi bdag po 'bod sgrogs kyi phyir de nyid bsnyad cing chos bstan to snyam nas 'dzum pa
1b9(1): mdzad do//de nas gnod sbyin gyi bdag po 'bad sgrogs de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin gyi rlabs kyis bcom ldan 'das rgya mtsho 'i klu'i rgyal po'i gnas nasabyung te/brgya byin dang /tshangs pa nga /klu'i bu mo brgya stong 
2a1(1): @#//du mas bskor cing mdun du bdar nas rgya mtsho'i rlabs la gzigs te/kun gzhi rnam par shes pa'i rgya mtsho la 'jug pa'i rnam par shes pa yul gyi rlung gis bskyod cing de dag gi sems 'byung bar gzigs te/
2a2(1): ched du brjod pa ched du brjod pa mdzad do zhes thos nas/bdag song la bcom ldan 'das la gsol ba btab ste lang+kar gshegs par bya'o//de ni bdag dang lha dang mi rnams la yun ring po'i don dang /phan pa dang bde bar 'gyur ro snyam ste/
2a3(1): de nas gnod sbyin gyi bdag po 'bod sgrogs 'khor dang bcas pa me tog gi gzhal med khang du zhugs nas/bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der song ste phyin pa dang /'khor dang bcas te gzhal med khang nas babs nas rnga dang sil snyan
2a4(1): rnams rab tu bsgrags te/bcom ldan 'das la lan gsum bskor ba byas nas pi bang gi khog pa in+da ni la la bai DU r+ya dang /mus ras spras pa/gos ngur ka pri yang ku 'dra ba rin thang med pas ched du sbyangs te/'khor rnams
2a5(1): rab tu dga' bar byas nas/glu'i dbyangs/sha rdza dang /ri Sha b+ha dang /gAn d+har dang /d+he bi ta dang /ni ShA dang /ma d+h+ya ma dang /ke shi ka dang /glu'i dbyangs mang por sbyar ba la sogs pa dang ldan zhing /rgyud rjes su mthun
2a6(1): pa dang pi bang gi nang du chud par byas te tshigs su bcad pa'i dbyangs kyis glu blangs pa/sems kyi rang bzhin tshul nyid chos kyi gter//bdag med lta dang rnam bral dri ma med//so so rang rig shes pa ston pa yi//chos tshul
2a7(1): mgon po 'dir yang bshad du gsol//dge ba'i chos kyis yang dag bsags pa'i sku//bde gshegs 'phrul pas sprul pa ston pa ste//so so rang rig rtogs pa'i chos dgyes pa//thub pa deng ni lang+kar gshegs pa'i dus//lang+ka
2a8(1): 'di na sngon gyi rgyal ba dang //de yi sras rnams gzugs mang 'chang ba bzhugs//mgon po 'dir ni chos rab bshad du gsol//gzugs mang ldan pa'i gnod sbyin kun kyang nyan//de ltar lang+ka'i bdag po 'bod sgrogs kyis toT
2a9(1): ka'i rgyud du glu blangs nas yang tshigs su bcad pa'i dbyang glu blangs pa//bcom ldan 'das ni dgung bdun du//chu srin gnas kyi rgya mtsho la//rgya mtsho'i dbang po'i gnas nas ni//slad du byung ste 'gram na bzhugs//
2b1(1): sangs rgyas bzhugs ma thag tu yang //ne tsos ra nas brgyan pa'i//gnod sbyin rnam pa mang po dang //'bod sgrogs lha mo lhan cig tu//mgon pa gang na bzhugs pa yi//lam der 'phrul gyis song nas ni//me tog bzhon las
2b2(1): babs nas kyang //de bzhin gshegs la mchod phyag 'tshal//rgyal ba'i dbang pos byin brlabs nas//de la mngon par mi bsnyed pa//bdag ni 'bod sgrogs mgrin bcu ste//srin po'i dbang po 'dir mchis so//grong rdal 'di
2b3(1): nasu gnas dang //bdag gi lang+ka rjes su gzung //rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas snga mas kyang //so so rang rig spyod yul ni//rtse mo rin chen brgyan pa yi//grong rdal dbus su rab tu bshad//bcom ldan gyis kyang de nyid ni//
2b4(1): rtse mo rin chen brgyan pa la//rgyal ba'i sras kyis yongs bskor cing //rdul med chos ni bshad du gsol//gang rnams lang+kar gnas pa dang //bdag cag de ring nyan par 'tshal//bshad pa'i tshul las rnam grol ba//so
2b5(1): so rang rig spyod yul te//lang+kar gshegs pa'i mdo sde ni//sangs rgyas snga mas gsungs pa'o//rgyal ba'i sras kyis yong bskor cing //sngon gyi sangs rgyas dag gis kyang //mdo sde 'di ni bshad par dran//bcom
2b6(1): ldan 'das kyis bshad du gsol//ma 'ongs dus na 'byung ba yi//sangs rgyas sangs rgyas sras dag kyang //'phrul gyi tshul te 'di nyid ni//rtse mo rin chen brgyad pa la//gnod sbyin rnams la thugs brtse phyir//'dren pa
2b7(1): rnams la ston par 'gyur//'phrul gyi lang+ka'i grong rdal ni//mdangs dga' rin chen sna tshogs dang //thel sdings rdo steng dga' ba dang //rin chen dra ba'i bla bres brgyan//rnal 'byor nyes dang rnam bral ba//so so rang
2b8(1): rig sems pa yi//bcom ldan 'di na gnod sbyin dag/sangs rgyas snga mas mdzad sang ba//theg chen tshul la dad pa yi//mngon par zhen cing gzhan 'dzud pa//gnod sbyin mo dang gnod sbyin bu//theg chen rtogs
2b9(1): par 'tshal ba mchis//lang+ka ma la ya yi rim//ston pa sangs rgyas gshegs su gsol//bum pa rnal sogs pa rnams//srin po grong rdal gnas pa dang //theg pa che la rton pa rnams//so so rang rig nyan par 'tshal//
2b10(1): sangs rgyas rnams la bgyi ba bgyis//de ltar na yang shin tu bgyid//bdag la rjes su thugs brtse'i phyir//lang+kar sras dang bcas te gshegs//khyim dang lha mo tshogs rnams dang //se mo do yang sna tshogs dang //
3a1(1): @#//mdangs dga' a sho ka yi tshul//thub pa chen po bzhes su gsol//bdag ni rgyal sras de dag dang //sangs rgyas rnams kyi bka' bzhin bgyid//bdag gis me dbul gcig ma mchis//
3a2(1): thub pa chen po thugs brtser dgongs//de yi tshig de gsan pa na//srid gsum dbang pos gsungs pa ni//gnod sbyin dbang po 'das pa yi//mgon po (rin )chen ri bo la//so so rang rig chos rnams bstan//khyod nyid la yang thug+sa
3a3(1): brtser dgongs//rin chen rnam brgyan ri bo la//ma 'ongs rnams kyang gsung bar 'gyur//'di ni rnal 'byor ldan pa dag/mthong ba'i chos la spyod pa'i gnas//nga dang sras rnams dag gis kyang //gnod sbyin dbang po
3a4(1): brtser dgongs rigs//bcom ldan gnang nas mi gsung par//zhi ba'i blo yis rnam par bzhugs//'bod sgrogs kyis na phul ba yi//me tog bzhon par bzhugs par gyur//de nyid du ni 'bod sgrogs
3a5(1): dang  ༐ 'jigs med rgyal sras gzhan yang ste//lha mos gzhan dag rol mo yis//rab tu mchod cing grong du gshegs//dga' ba'i grong rdal der gshegs nas//mchod pa rab tu yang brnyes so//'bod
3a6(1): sgrogs la sogs gnod sbyin gyi//sde dang gnod sbyin mos kyang mchod//gnod sbyin bu mo gnod sbyin bus//rin po che yi dra bas mchod//'bod sgrogs kyis kyang sangs rgyas kyi//rin chen brgyan pa'i se mo do//
3a7(1): rgyal ba'i sras dang rgyal ba yi//stod kyi yan lag rnams la btags//bcom ldan rgyal sras mkhas bcas pa//mchod pa phul ba bzhes nas ni//so so rang rig spyod yul gyi//chos ni rnam par bstan pa
3a8(1): mdzad||gnod sbyin sde dang 'bod sgrogs kyis//smra ba rnams kyi mchog mchod de//blo gros chen po rab mchod cing //gsol ba yang dang yang 'debs pa//khyod ni sangs rgyas thams cad la//rang rig spyod
3a9(1): yul 'dri ba yin//'di na bdag dang gnod sbyin dang //rgyal ba'i sras rnams nyan pa mchis//khyod la rgyal sras mkhas pa dang //gnod sbyin rnams kyang gsol ba 'debs//smra ba'i nang na smra chen khyod//
3b1(1): rnal 'byor can gyi'ang rnal 'byor zhugs//khyod la gus par gsol 'debs kyis//'jigs pa med pas tshul dris shig/rang rgyal nyan thos rnams dang ni//mu stegs nyes las rnam par grol//so so rang 
3b2(1): rig chos nyid gtsang //sangs rgyas sa ni thob byed pa'o//rtse mo rin chen brgyan pa dang //gzhan yang lha rdzas rnams kyi ni//rin chen bye bas brgyan pa dag/bcom ldan 'das kyis der sprul te//mgon pos 
3b3(1): ri bo re re la//nyid kyi sku ni rab tu bstan//'bod sgrogs gnod sbyin de dag na//re re la ni rnam par gnas//thams cad 'khor ni ma bri bar//re re na yang shin tu snang //de na zhing rnams thams cad dang //
3b4(1): gang yang de na 'dren pa dang //de na srin po'i dbang po rnams//gang dag lang+kar gnas pa'ang snang //de dang 'gran pa'i lang+ka dang //a sho ka (ya)tsal gzhan dang //nags tshal bzang po de dag kyang //rgyal ba y+si ni mngon 
3b5(1): par sprul//mgon po ri rnams re re la//blo gros chen pos bskul ba dang //so so rang rig brjod pa yis//chos ni gnod sbyin rnams la bstan//stong phrag brgya ba'i mdo sde dag/ri bo la ni ma lus bshad//
3b6(1): ston pa dang ni rgyal sras rnams//de nyid du yang mi snang gyur//gnod sgrin 'bod sgrogs bdag gis lus//khyim na gnas par mthong ba dang //bsams pa 'di go dzri zhig snyam//gang gis bshad de 
3b7(1): su yis thos//ci zhig mthong ge gang gis mthong //grong khyer de dag gang du song //sangs rgyas de dag zhing de rnams//rin chen nags bzang de dag gi/'di na sgyu ma'ang rmi lam yod//dri za'i grong 
3b8(1): zhes bsgrags pa'am//rab rib yin nam smig rgyu 'am//rmi lam mo sham bu byung ngam//bdag gis 'dir ni gang mthong ba//mgal me bskor btam du ba 'am//'on te sems kyi spyod yul la//
3b9(1): chos rnams kyi ni chos nyid dam//lta ba med cing blta ba'ang med//smra med smra bar bya med na//rang gi rtogs pas myos pa yi//byis pa rnams kyis mi shes so//gzhan du rnam par rtog pa 'di//
4a1(1): @#//sangs rgyas dang ni chos ltar gnas//ji ltar mthong bzhin su lta ba//de dag 'dren pa mi mthong ngo //rnam par rtog pa mi 'jug cing //gang tshe sangs rgyas mi mthong la//'jug med
4a2(1): sangs rgyas yin par ni//gal te mthong na de sangs rgyas//lang+ka'i bdag po gnas shin tu gyur pa dang /rang gi sems snang ba tsam du khong du chud pa rab tu rtogs ma thag tu rnam par rtog pa'i rgyu ba la gnas pa/sngon gyi dge ba'i
4a3(1): rtsa ba'i gtsug lag thams cad la mkhas pa'i blo de bzhin nyid ji lta ba bzhin du mthong ba/gzhan gyi dring mi 'jog pa/bdag gi blo rnam par dpyod pa la mkhas pa/rtog ge ba'i lta ba dang bral bar lta ba/gzhan
4a4(1): gyi dring la mi 'jog pa/rnal 'byor chen po'i rnal 'byor can/gzugs sna tshogs 'chang ba chen po thabs la mkhas pa khong du chud par rtogs pa/gong nas gong du sa thams cad kyi rang gi mtshan nyid rtogs
4a5(1): par bya ba la mkhas pa/sems dang yid dang rnam par shes pa'i rang bzhin dben pa la dga' ba/rgyud gsum chad par lta ba rgyu dang mu stegs can thams cad kyi rgyu dang bral ba'i blor gyur pas de bzhin gshegs
4a6(1): ba'i snying po//sangs rgyas kyi sa la nang du snyoms par zhugs pa la gnas shing sangs rgyas kyi blos nam mkha' dang nang nas sgra thos pa lang+ka'i bdag po legs so legs so//yang lang+ka'i bdag po khyod ni legs
4a7(1): so||de ltar rnal 'byor can rnams kyi bslab par bya'o//ji ltar khyod kyis mthong ba de bzhin nyid du de bzhin gshegs pa rnams dang /chos rnams lta bar bya'o/rnam pa gzhan bltas na chad pa la gnas par 'gyur
4a8(1): ro||khyod kyi sems dang yid dang /yid kyi rnam par shes pa dang bral zhing nang du spyod pas/chos thams cad rnam par bsgom par bya ste/phyi rol gyi don du lta ba la mngon par chags pas ni ma yin no//khyod
4a9(1): kyis nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas dang /mu stegs can gyi mngon par rtogs pa dang /tshig gi don gyi spyod yul du lhung ba'i lta ba dang /ting nge 'dzin du ma yin/gtam dang sgrung la dga' bar ma yin/rig byed
4a10(1): kyi rang bzhin tu lta bar ma yin/rgyal po'i dbang gis dregs pa la lhung bar ma yin no//bsam gtan drug la sogs pa la bsam gtan pang ma yin te/lang+ka'i bdag po 'di ni rgyal ba'i sras rnal 'byor chen po'i
4b1(1): rnal 'byor dang ldan pa/pha rol rgol ba rab tu 'jom pa/lta ba ngan pa sel ba/bdag tu lta ba rnam par bzlog pa la mkhas pa/blo zhib cing rnam par shes pa/shin tu 'gyur ba la mkhas pa/theg pa chen po la spyod
4b2(1): pa rnams kyi dam tshig go//de bzhin gshegs pa'i bdag gi so so rang gi sa la 'jug pa khong du chud par bya ba'i phyir khyod kyi sa brtson par gyis shig/de ltar byas nas lang+ka'i bdag po gang khyod kyis ting nge 'dzin la
4b3(1): mkhas pa'i snyoms par 'jug pas yongs su bzung ba'i lam 'di ni phyir zhing gong nas gong du rnam par spyod pa yin te/ji ltar byis pa mu stegs can gyi rnal 'byor la rnal 'byor can bdag tu 'dzin par snang ba'i mtshan nyid
4b4(1): la mngon par chags pa 'byung ba dang /yon tan dang rdzas kyi rjes su spyod pa/ma rig pa'i rkyen du lta ba la mngon par zhen pa/stobs skye bas g.yengs pa dang /rnam par rtog pa la mngon par chags pa/mtshan nyid du lhung ba'i
4b5(1): bsam pas brtags pa/nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas dang /mu stegs can rjesu 'jug pa'i sgo'i spyod yul ni ma yin no//lang+ka'i bdag po bdag gi so so rang gis rig pa rtogs par byed pa'i theg pa chen po khong du chud pa 'di
4b6(1): ni gzugs sna tshogs rab tu 'thob par byed pa'o//bye brag gi srid par skye ba yongasu 'thob par yang 'gyur ro//lang+ka'i bdag po theg pa chen po'i rnal 'byor la 'jug pa 'di ni khebs pa'i g.yogs pa rnam pa shes pa sna tshogs kyi
4b7(1): rlabs zlog par byed pa yin te/mu stegs can gyi rnal 'byor gyi gnas su lhung ba ni ma yin no//lang+ka'i dag po mu stegs can gyi rnal 'byo ni mu stegs can rnams kyi bdag tu mngon par zhen pa las byung ba'o//rnam par shes
4b8(1): pa'i rang bzhin gnyis kyi don la chags par lta bas mu stegs can rnams kyi 'di rnal 'byor 'di'ang rnal 'byor ma yin no//lang+ka'i ba dag po de legs so//de bzhin gshegs pa mthong zhing ji ltar rjes su bsams pa'i don de nyid
4b9(1): rjesu soms shig dang de nyid ni de bzhin gshegs pa mthong ba yin no//de nas de'i skabs su 'bod sgrogs 'di snyam du sems te/yang 'di ltar bdag gis bcom ldan 'das rnal 'byor thams cad la mnga' mdzad pa/mu stegs
4b10(1): can gyi rnal 'byor rnam par zlog pa/so so rang gis rig pa'i spyod yul ston pa/'phrul pas sprul pa dang bral ba'i thugs su chud pa'i blo la gang gi phyir rnal 'byor can rnams rnal 'byor mngon par rtogs pa'i dus na/ting nge 'dzin gyi
5a1(1): @#//bde ba dang /snyoms par 'jugs pa rnams khong du chud par 'gyur te/de khong du chud pa'i phyir 'dis khong du chud do zhes rnal 'byor can rnams kyi rnal 'byor gyi sgrar gdags te/de'i phyir bdag gis
5a2(1): thugs rje can nyon mongs pa 'bar ba'i rgyu dang /rnam par rtog pa zad par byed pa/rgyal ba'i sras rnams kyis yongs su bskor ba/sems can thams cad kyi sems dang bsam pa'i rjesu zhugs pa/thams cad du khyab pa/thams cad mkhyen
5a3(1): pa la byed pa'i mtshan nyid rnam par log pa'i rdzu 'phrul gyis de ltar blta'o//de mthong bas ni khong du ma chud pa khong du chud par bya'o//rnam par mi rtog pa la spyod pa bde ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang /snyoms par 'jug pa la gnas pa de bzhin
5a4(1): gshegs pa'i thugs su chud pa'i sa thob par byed pa bdag gis khong du chud par yang rnam par 'phel bar 'gyur ro snyamo//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de'i tshe lang+ga'i bdag po ma skyes pa'i chos la bzod pa thob par thugs chud nas mgrin bcu la
5a5(1): thugs brtse ba'i phyir rin po che mang pos brgyan pa/rin po che 'dra bas bres pa'i bdag gi ri'i rtse mo la mdzes de lta bu nyid du bdag nyid bstan nas/yang lang+ga'i bdag po mgrin bcus mthong ba dang /myong ba'i mdzes pa dang /rtse mo la de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcoM
5a6(1): pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sad+yas mtshan rab sum cu rtsa gnyis kyis brgyan pa'i sku dang /ri bo re re la bdag gi lus kyang de bzhin gshegs pa dgra 'acom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'is sad+yas rnams kyi spyan snga na 'dug pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa blo gros chen po
5a7(1): dang thabs cig tu/so so rang gis rig pa'i spyod yul gyi gtam mdzad pa dang /gnod sbyin gyis bskor cing bshed pa brjod pa'i gtam gsung ba dang /mgon po dang bcas pa'i zhing de dag kyang mthong bar gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de'i tshe
5a8(1): sad+yas kyi sha'i spyan gyis 'khor la gzigs nas seng ge'i rgyal po ltar rnam par bsgyings te/bzhad mo cher bzhad nas/mdzod spu dang /rtsib logs dang /brla dang /sked pa'i phyogs dang /dpal gyi be'u dang /spu'i khung bu thams cad nas ni 'od zer rab tu
5a9(1): 'byin|dus kyi mtha'i me bzhin du ni 'bar/gzi brjid ni 'ja' lta bu/'od kyi dkyil 'khor ni nyi ma shar ba 'dra bar shin tu 'bar/brgya byin dang /tshangs pa dang /'jig rten skyong ba rnams kyis ni nam mkha'i dbyings nas lta zhing ri rab rtse mo la 'gran pa'i zom la
5a10(1): bzhugs nas bzhad mo cher bzhad do//de nas byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor de dang /brgya byin dang /tshangs ba la sogs pa de dag 'di snyam du sems te/rgyu gang dang rkyen gang gis de ltar bac+doMs chos thams cad la mnga' mdzad pa sngon du 'dzum pas bzhad mo
5a11(1): cher bzhad cing rang gi sku las 'od zer rab tu 'byin/phyung nas kyang bdag gi so so rang gi rig pa'i 'phags pa'i ye shes mthong ba'i spyod yul ting nge 'dzin gyi bde ba la dgongs pa la rab tu bzhag ste/gzigs mo med par seng ge'i lta ba'i phyogasu gzigs nas
5b1(1): 'bod sgrogs kyi rnal 'byor rtogs pa rgyu ba la rjesu dgongs shing cang mi gsung bar 'gyur snyamo//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po blo gros chen po sngar gsol ba btab pas 'bod sgrogs la snying brtse ba dang /byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor de dag gi sems dang /
5b2(1): bsam pa dang /rnam par spyod pa'ang shes shing ma 'ongs pa'i skye bo'i tshogs la'ang bltas na bshad par brjod pa la mngon par dga' ba'i sems can rnams dang /sgra ji bzhin du mngon par zhen pa rnams dang /nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas dang /mu stegs can
5b3(1): gyi rnal 'byor gyi stobs la mngon par zhen pa thaMd 'di ltar bac+doMs de bzhin gshegs pa rnaM par shes pa'i yul las phyir log pa rnams kyang bzhad mo cher bzhado snyam du sems shing sems rnaM par myos par gyur na mi rung snyam nas/de dgi ngo mtshar rnaM par bzlog pa'i phyir/bac+doMs la zhus pa/bcoM ldan 'das rgyu gang
5b4(1): dang rkyen gang gis 'di ltar 'dzum pa mdzad/bcom ldan 'das kyis bag 'astsal pa/blo gros chen po legaso legaso//yang blo gros chen po gang khyod kyis 'jig rten gyi rang bzhin dang /'jig rten rnams kyi lta ba ngan pa la bltas te/dus gsuM gyi seMs khong du chud pas/da la dri bar brtsams pa ni blo gros chen po khyod legaso//de ltar mkhas
5b5(1): pa rnams kyi bdag dang gzhan gyi don dang /gnyis ka'i don du yongasu 'dri ba'i rang bzhin du bya'o//blo gros chen po lang+ka'i bdag po 'bod sgrogs 'dis ni sngon gyi de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sad+yas rnams la dri ba gnyis driso//de nga la yang gang nyan thos dang /rang sad+yas dang /mu stegs can gyi rnal
5b6(1): 'byor pa rnal 'byor pa rnams kyis ma myong ba'i dri ba gnyis kyi bye brag khong du chud pa'i mtshan nyid rnam par bsgom pa'i phyir dri bar 'dod do//'di ltar 'dri bar 'dod pa'i mgrin bcu 'di ni ma 'ongs pa'i rgyal ba rnams la'ang 'dri bar 'gyuro//de nas bac+doMs kyis mkhyen bzhin du/lang+ka'i bdag po la 'di skad ces bka'
5b7(1): stsal to//lang+ka'i bdag po khyod la de bzhin gshegs pa'i skabs phyes rtsod pa na rab tu g.yo ba ma sdod par ci dang ci 'dod pa dal dris shig dang /ngas dri ba de dang de'i lung bstan pas ji ltar khyod rnam par rtog pa'i gnas shin tu gyur nas/sa'i mi mthun pa'i phyogs la mkhas shing rab tu 'byed
5b8(1): pa'i blos so so rang gi rig pa'i tshul gyis mtshan nyid kyi ting nge 'dzin gyi bde bar gnas pa la rnam par dpyod cing ting nge 'dzin gyi sangs rgyas rnams kyis ni yongasu bzung /zhi ba'i bde ba la ni rnam par gnas te/nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas kyi ting nge 'dzin gyi nyes pa las 'das nas mi g.yo
5b9(1): ba dang /dge ba'i blo gros dang /chos kyi sprin gyi sa la ni rnam par gnas/chos la bdag med pa'i yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin la ni rab tu mkhas pas rin po che'i pad+mo chen po bting ba la ting nge 'dzin gyi rgyal bas dbang bskur ba thob par 'gyur zhing /de dang rjesu mthun
5b10(1): pa'i pad+mo dag la bdag gi lus rnam pa sna tshogasu byin gyi rlabs kyis byin du brlabs te/pad+mo de dag la bdag gi lus 'dug pa mthong bar 'gyur ba dang /gcig gis gdong la gcig lta bar yang byed par 'gyur ba dang /de ltar gang gi phyir bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i yul de ni mngon par sgrub pa la mkhas
6a1(1): @#//ba|gcig gis mngon par bsgrubs ba yin te/spyod pa'i sa la gnas shing thabs mkhas pas yongs su bzung ba'i mngon par bsgrub pa mngon par bsgrubs pas/bsam gyis mi khyab ba'i yul de'ang rjes su 'thob par 'gyur ba dang /
6a2(1): de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku gzugs rnam pa mang por rnam par 'gyur ba/nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas dang /mu stegs can dang /tshangs pa dang /dbang po dang /nye ba'i dbang po rnams kyis sngon ma mthong ba de'ang thob par 'gyur bar khyod kyi sems rangs par
6a3(1): bya'o//de nas lang+ka'i bdag po lha mo dang bcas pa'i tshogs kyis yongs su bskor ba/bcom ldan 'das kyis skabs phye bas rin po che'i pad+mo'i 'od zer gyis dri ma med pa'i 'od rin po che'i pad+mo dang 'dra ba'i rin po che'i rtse mo de nas langs nas me tog dang /phreng
6a4(1): ba dang /spos dang /'dug pa dang /byug pa dang gdugs dang /rgyal mtshan dang /lha'i ba dan dang /se mo do dang /do shal dang /cod pan dang /thor tshugs rnam pa du ma rnam pa tha dad pa sna tshogs dang /sngon ma mthong ma thos pa'i rgyan gyi khyad par rnam par
6a5(1): 'phags pa gzhan dag dang /lha dang klu dang gnod sbyin dang /srin po dang /dri za dang mi 'am ci dang /lto 'phye chen po dang /mi las lhag pa las kyang 'das pa'i rnga dang /sil snyan rnams dang /'dod pa'i khams su gtogs pa'i rol mo'i cha byad
6a6(1): thams cad kyang rnam par sbrul/gang yang sangs rgyas kyi zhing gzhan gyi rol mo'i khyad par mthong ba de dag kyang mngon par sprul/bcom ldan 'das dang byang chub sems dpa' rnams la rin po che'i dra bas ni mngon par bkab/
6a7(1): gos sna tshogs kyi lha'i ba dan ni rab tu bsgreng /ta la bdun tsam du ni nam mkha' la ni mngon par 'phags/mchod pa'i sprin chen po'i char ni mngon par phab/rnga dang sil snyan rnams kyi sgra ni rab tu phyung ste/
6a8(1): nam mkha' de las babs nas nyi ma dang /glog gi 'od lta bu rin po che'i pad+ma chen pos brgyan pa'i rin po che'i rtse mo gnyis pa la 'dug go/'dug nas zhe sar 'dzum pa sngon du byas te/bcom ldan 'das kyis go skabs phye
6a9(1): mas|bcom ldan 'das la dri ba gnyis dris so//sngon gyi de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams la'ang bdag gis zhus nas/de rnams kyis kyang lan glon te/
6b1(1): bcom ldan 'das la'ang da zhu'o//bstan pa brjod pa las 'di ni sangs rgyas rnams dang khyod kyis kyang gdon mi 'tshal bar bshad bar 'gyur te/bcom ldan 'das chos gnyis po 'di ni 'phral bas sprul pa
6b2(1): rnams kyis bstan pa lags te/ngo bo'i de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyis ni ma gsungs so//bcom ldan 'das ngo bo'i de bzhin gshegs rnams ni ting nge 'dzin gyi sgo'i spyod yul ston te/tshig gi spyod yul
6b3(1): gyi rnam par rtog par lhung ni mi ston na/bcom ldan 'das chos la mnga' mdzad pa de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas nyid kyis chos gnyis po de legs par bdag la bshad du gsol/bcom ldan
6b4(1): 'das rgyal ba'i sras 'di rnams kyang nyan/bdag kyang 'tshal bar 'gyur ro//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/lang+ka'i bdag po chos gnyis po de smros shig/srin po'i dbang po lus la cod pan dang /dpung rgyan dang /se mo do dang /rdo rje mchog gi
6b5(1): phreng ba thogs shing rgyan bzang pos lus brgyan pas gsol pa/chos nyid kyang spang bar bgyi na/chos ma mchis par lta ci smos/bcom ldan 'das chos gnyis po de ji ltar spang bar 'gyur/chos mas chos ma mchis pa rnams ni gang lags/
6b6(1): ji ltar na gnyis spang ba'i chos can rnam par rtog pa'i mtshan nyid du lhung ba rnam par rtog pa'i rang bzhin du 'byung ba dang /'byung ba las gyur pa ma lags pa/kun gzhi rnam par shes pa yongs su ma rig pas khyad par gyi mtshan nyid ma
6b7(1): mchis pa/sgra shad 'dzings pa'i rang bzhin du gnas pa/shes pa ma dag cing zad par 'gyur ba'i yul can/de lta bu'i rang bzhin de dag ji ltar spang bar 'gyur//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/lang+ka'i bdag po 'di ltar
6b8(1): bum pa la sogs pa 'chag pa'i rang bzhin 'jig pa'i chos can rnams byis pa rnams kyi rnam par rtog pa'i spyod yul la tha dad pa'i bye brag tu snang ba/de bzhin du 'di la'ang ci phyir mi gzung /byis pa rnams kyi so so'i rnam par rtog pa nye
6b9(1): bar bzung na/chos dang chos med pa tha dad pa'i bye brag kyang yod do//'phags pa'i ye shes khong du chud par bya ba'i phyir mthong bas ni ma yin no//lang+ka'i bdag po bum pa la sogs pa'i dngos po mtshan nyid sna tshogs su lhung ba'i rnams byis
6b10(1): pa dag gi yin te/'phags pa rnams kyi ma yin pa lta zhog la/rang bzhin gcig cing 'bar ba gcig las byung ba'i rab tu 'bar ba khyim dang gnas dang skyed mos tshal dang /khang pa la 'dug pa shing gi dbang gis 'od ring ba dang
7a1(1): @#//thung ba'i khyad par dang tha dad pa'i bye brag tu snang ba de bzhin du 'di la'ang chos dang /chos med pa tha dad pa'i bye brag yod par ci'i phyir mi gzung /me lce'i rgyud gcig tu gyur pa'i 'od 'phro ba tha dad pa'i bye brag tu sna ba 'ba' 
7a2(1): zhig tu'ang ma zad de/lang+ka'i bdag po sa bon gcig las byung yang myu gu dang /ljang bu dang /sogs ma dang /tshigs dang /lo ma dang /yal ga dang /me tog dang /'bras bu dang /gra ma'i bye brag yod pa de bzhin du phyi nang gi chos skye ba'i chos
7a3(1): can|ma rig pa las rab tu byung ba/phung po dang /khams dang /skye mched du gtogs pa thams cad dang /khams gsum du skyes pa'i chos thams cad la bde ba'i dbyibs dang /brjod pa dang 'gro ba'i khyad par dag kyang snang ste/
7a4(1): mtshan nyid gcig pa'i rnam par shes pa yul la mngon par 'dzin par rab tu 'jug pa'i khyad par rab dang 'bring dang tha ma'i bye brag dang /rnam par byang ba dang /dge ba dang /mi dge bar yang snang ngo //lang+ka'i bdag po chos de dag gi tha dadu
7a5(1): bye brag tu'ang ma zad de/rnal 'byor can rnal 'byor bsgom pa rnams kyi rnal 'byor gyi lam la'ang so so rang gis rig pa'i mtshan nyid kyi khyad par snang na/so so'i rnam par rtog pa las byung ba'i chos dang /chos med pa'i khyad par lta ci'i phyir med de yod do//
7a6(1): lang+ka'i bdag po rnam par rtog pa'i mtshan nyid kyi phyir chos dang /chos med pa tha dad pa'i bye brag yod do//lang+ka'i bdag po de la chos rnams gang zhe na/'di lta ste/mu stegs can dang /nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas dang /byis pa rnams kyi rnam par rtog pas
7a7(1): rnam par brtags pa rgyu las yon tan dang rdzas kyis gzhi btsugs pa ni chos rnams zhes bya'o//de dag ni spang bar bya ba btang bar bya ba ste/mtshan nyid du so sor rnam par brtags par mi bya'o//rang gi sems snang ba'i chos rnams la mngon par zhen pa'i
7a8(1): phyir rdza ma la sogs pa'i chos byis pas kun brtags pa lusu ma red pa'i rnams ni med pa yin te/de lta bur lhag mthong gis rab tu mthong na spangs par 'gyuro//de la chos med pa rnams gang zhe na/gang de dag bdag nyid du ma red pa yin te/mtshan nyid kyi
7a9(1): rnaM par rtog pa rgyu ba med pa'i chos don gyi rgyu las byung ba de dag yang dag pa dang /yang dag pa ma yin par 'jug pa med par mthong nas/de'i phyir chos med pa spong bar 'gyur ro//yang bdag nyid du ma red pa'i chos rnams gang zhe na/'di lta ste/ri bong dang /
7a10(1): rnga mo dang /bong bu dang /rta'i rwa dang mo sham gyi bu la sogs pa'i chos rnams te/bdag nyid du ma red pa'i phyir de dag ni mtshan nyid du brtag par mi bya'i/gzhan du na tha snyad kyi don du brjod de/bum pa la sogs pa ltar mngon par chags pas ni ma yin no//ji ltar
7b1(1): de dag rnam par shes pas gzung du med pa'i phyir spang bar bya ba de bzhin du rnam par rtog pa'i dngos po rnams kyang spang bar bya ba yin te/de'i phyir chos dang /chos med pa spang ba yino//lang+ka'i bdag po khyod kyis chos dang /chos med pa gnyis ji lta bur spang bar bya zhes smras pa de ni 'di ltar
7b2(1): bshad do//gang yang lang+ka'i bdag po khyod gyis sngon gyi de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams la'ang bdag gis dris reg de dag gis kyang lan glon zhes smras pa'ang /lang+ka'i bdag po sngon zhes bya ba 'di ni rnam par rtog pa'i tshig bla dgas te/
7b3(1): 'das pa la'ang 'di ltar 'das pa zhes rnam par rtog go/de bzhin du ma 'ongs pa dang /da ltar yang rtog ste/de bzhin gshegs pa rnams ni de'i chos nyid kyis rnam par mi rtog pa rnam par rtog pa'i spros pa thams cad las 'das pa ste/gzugs kyi ngo bo nyid la brtags pa ltar ni ma yin
7b4(1): gyi/gzhan du na ye shes thugs su chud pa bde ba'i don du mtshan ma med pa la spyod pa'i shes rab kyis rnam par bsgomo//de'i phyir de bzhin gshegs pa rnams na ye shes kyi bdag nyid ye shes kyi sku ste/rtag par mi bya'o//gang gis mi brtag ce na/yid kyis bdag dang /srog dang /gang 
7b5(1): zag tu mi rtag ji ltar mi rtag ce na/yul gyi don gyi rgyu las byung ba yid kyi rnam par shes pas ji ltar gzugs kyi mtshan nyid dang /dbyibs dang rnam pa bzhin du mi rtago/de bas na khyod kyis brtag par bya ba dang /rnaM par rtog pa dang bral bar bya'o//lang+ka'i bdag po 'di ltar yang sems
7b6(1): can gyi spyod pa rnams ni brtsig pa'i ri mo'i gzugs dang 'dra ste/lang+ka'i bdag po chos thams cad yod pa ma yin pa'i phyir 'jig rten gnas pa ni g.yo ba med pa ste/las dang byed pa dang bral ba'o//'di la gang yang nyan pa'am/mnyan par bya ba med do//
7b7(1): lang+ka'i bdag po 'jig rten gnas pa ni sprul pa dang mtshungs na/mu stegs can byis pa'i rnal 'byor pa rnams rnam par mi sgom mo//lang+ka'i bdag po gang gis de ltar mthong ba des ni yang dag par mthong ba ste/rnam pa gzhan du mthong na rnam par rtog pa
7b8(1): la spyod pa'o//de ltar rnam par rtog pa dang bcas pa rnams ni rnam pa gnyisu 'dzin te/'di lta ste me long gi nang na yod pa'i bdag gi gzugs kyi gzugs brnyan dang /chu la bdag gi gzugs kyi grib ma dang /zla ba'i 'od dang 
7b9(1): khyim na me mar 'bar ba la bdag gi lus kyi grib ma dang /brag ca 'am/gzhan yang bdag gi rnam par rtog pa'i gzung ba rab tu bzung ste/chos dang chos med par rnam par rtog cing /chos
7b10(1): dang chos med pa spong ba la mi spyod de spyod do//rnam par rtog go/rgyas par byed do//rab tu zhi ba mi slob bo//'di ni rtse gcig pa'i tshig bla dgas te//'di ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying po bdag gi 'phags pa so so rang 
8a1(1): //gi ye shes kyi spyod yul du 'jug pa'i phyir mchog gi ting nge 'dzind skye ba'o////'bod 'grogs kyis gsol pa btab pa'i le'u ste dang po'o//////////nas
8a2(1): zhing thams cad du rgyu ba'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po blo gros chen po /byang chub sems dpa' blo gros dang //lhan cig tu sangs rgyas kyi mthus stan las langs te /bla gos phrag pa gcig tu bzar nas /pus mo
8a3(1): g.yas pa'i lhar ma la btsugs te /bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der logs su thal mo sbyard te phyag 'tshal nas /bcom ldan 'das la tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag gis mngon par bstod do//'jig rten skye 'jig ma mchis te /
8a4(1): nam mkha'i me tog rab tu 'dra //khyod kyi shes rab thugs rje'i sa//khyod kyis yod dang myed ma dmyigs //chos rnams thams cad sgyu ma bzhin //sems dang rnam par shes (brda )spangs //khyod kyis shes rab thugs rje'i
8a5(1): khyod kyis yod dang myed pa dmyigs //'jig rten rtag dang chad spangs pa //'khor zug rmi lam 'dra ba ste //khyod kyis shes rab thugs rje'is///khyod kyis yod dang myed ma dmyigs //chos dang gang zag bdag myed dang
8a6(1): //khyod kyi nyan mongs shes pa rnams //khyod kyi shes rab thugs rje dang //mtshan ma myed pas rab tu dag //sgyu ma rmi lam rang bzhin gyi //chos kyis sku la ci skad bstod //dngos po rang bzhin myed rnams kyis //
8a7(1): ma skyes de ni bstod pa lags //dbang po don dang rnam bral zhing//snang myed de'i gzigs pa pas //gal te bstod dam smad kyang rung //thub pa de la ci skad brjod //mya ngan myi 'da' 'da' la myin //khyod la mya ngan 'da' myi gnas//
8a8(1): blo dang rig par bgyi dang bral //yod dang myed pa'i phyogs kyang spangs//de ltar skye 'jig spangs pI //thub pa zhi ba sus mthong pa//de dag 'di dang slad mar yang //chags myed nyer len ma mchis 'gyur //de nas byang chub sems dpa'
8a9(1): sems dpa' chen po blo gros chen pos //bcom ldan 'das la tshul dang 'dra pa'i tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag gis mngon par bstod de /bdag gi mying dang rigs bcom ldan 'das la gsold pa //bcom ldan bdag ni blo can te //theg chen
8a10(1): khong du chud par rtogs//dri pa brgya po rtsa brgyad pa //gsung pa'i mchog la zhu ba bgyi //de'i tshig de gsang pa dang //sangs rgyas 'jig rten 'khyend pa'i mchog//thams cad 'khor la rnam gzigs nas //bde gshegs sras la gsungs
8b1(1): pa ni //nga la dris shing rgyal sras rnams//glo gros chen po khyod kyang dris //so so rang rig spyod yul ni //nga yis khyod la bshad par bya//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis skabs phyed pas//byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen
8b2(1): po blo gros chen pos//bcom ldan 'das kyis zhabs gnyis la gtugs te//bcom ldan 'das la zhu ba zhus pa //ci ltar rtog ge'i rnam dag 'gyur //rtog ge ci'i slad du 'byung //ci lta bur na nor par 'da' //ci'i slad du 'anor pa 'byung
8b3(1): ci'i slad du sruld dang ni//khyod kyi mtshan dang mu stegs kyang //snang myed rim pa ci'i slad du //khyod kyi rgyal sras gang lags gsungs //grol pa gang tu mchi' bar 'gyur //bcings pa su lags gang gis 'grol//bsam gtan pa'i yul
8b4(1): de gang //ci ltar theg pa gsum po lags //rkyend kyis skye ba de dag ci//'bras bu ci zhig rgyu ni gang //gnyis kyi mtha' brjod ci slad du //ci ltar bur yang 'byung ba lags //gzugs myed snyoms par 'jug pa dang //'gog pa'i de yang ci lta bu //
8b5(1): 'du shes 'gog pa ci lta bu //ci slad ci ltar sa dbang 'gyur //'gro ba dang ni lus 'dzind kyang //ci'i slad du bya ba 'byung //ci ltar snang ba rnam par sgom ci ltar sa la 'jug bar 'gyur //srid gsum chom nas swe su //gnas ni gang lags lusi ni //
8b6(1): gnas shing 'gro ba gang zhig tu //rgyal ba'i sras su ci ltar 'gyur //dbang dang ting nge 'dzind rnams dang //mngon par shes pa ci ltar thob //ci ltar sems ni mnyam bar gzhog//rgyal ba khyu mchog bdag la gsungs //yid dang rnam par
8b7(1): shes pa dang //kun gzhi de yang ci ltar bur //snang la ci ltar 'jug pa lags //ci ltar snang la slad du ldog //rigs dang rigs myed ci ltar cis //sems tsam du yang ci ltar 'gyur //mtshan rnams rnam par dgod pa dang //bdag
8b8(1): myed pa 'ang ci ltar 'gyur //sems can ma mchis ci lta bu //kun rdzob bshad pa ci lta bu //ci ltar rtag dang chad pa'i//lta ba la rab tu 'byung myi 'gyur //ci ltar khyod dang mu stegs su //mtshan nyid rnams kyis 'gal myi 'gyur //sde ba
8b9(1): rnams kyang ci ltar bur //ma 'ongs dus na 'byung ba gsungs //ci slad ci ltar stong ba nyid //ci ltar khyod kyis skad cig 'jig //snying po ci ltar 'byung pa legs//ci ltar 'jig rten byed ma mchis //ci slad 'jig rten smig rgyu 'dra //
8b10(1): dri za 'dra dang smig rgyu dang //chu'i zla ba 'dra pa yang //ci slad pa yang bdag la gsungs//byang chub yan lag ci ltar mchis //byang chub phyogs rnams ci las byung //yul blug pa dang khrag khrug pa dang //ci ltar 'byung bar lta bar 'gyur
9a1(1): @#//ma skyes pa dang ma 'gags pa//ji ltar nam mkha'i me tog 'dra//'jig rten ji ltar thugs su chud//yi ge med par ji ltar gsung //ci yis rnam par mi rtog 'gyur//ji ltar nam mkha' 'dra ba lags//de bzhin nyid ni rnam pa du//sems
9a2(1): dang pha rol phyin pa du//gang gis sa yi rim par gyur//snang med rgyu ba gang zhag lags//gang gis bdag med rnam pa gnyis//shes bya ji ltar rnam par bdag/mgon po ye shes rnam pa dang //tshul khrims sems can 'byung nas du//nor bu mu tig gser dngul dang //
9a3(1): gos rnams su yis rab tu byung //sems can dngos po'i sna grangs dang //mngon par brjed pa su yis bskyed//rig pa'i gnas dang bzo yi rnams//gang gis ji ltar rab tu 'byung //tshigs su bcad pa rnam pa du//tshigs bcad rkyang pa ji lta bu//rigs pa rnam
9a4(1): pa du zhig mchis//bshad pa dag kyang ji ltar 'gyur//skom dang kha zas sna tshogs dang //ji ltar 'khrig pa 'byung bar 'gyur//rgyal po 'khor los sgyur ba dang //khams kyi bdag po ji ltar lags//ji ltar rgyal srid srung bar 'gyur//lha yi ri la rnam pa du//
9a5(1): sa dang skar tshogs ci las gyur//nyi zla gnyis kyang ji lta bu//thar pa rnam pa du zhig mchis//rnal 'byor ldan pa rnam pa du//slob ma rnam pa du zhig mchis/slob dpon du yang ji ltar 'gyur//sangs rgyas rnam pa du zhig lags//skyes pa'i rabs la
9a6(1): rnam pa du//bdud la rnam pa du zhig mchis/ya mtshan can yang rnam pa du//khyod kyi rang bzhin rnam pa du//sems la rnam pa du zhig mchis//btags pa tsam yang ji lta bu//smra ba'i mchog gis bdag la gsungs//nam mkha'i sprin rlung ci las gyur//
9a7(1): dran dang yid gzhungs ji ltar 'byung //shing gi 'khri shing ji ltar mchis//srid gsum dbang po bdag la gsungs//glang po ri dgas rta blun rnams//gzung du rung ba ci slad du//ngan pa'i mi rnams ci las gyur//bdag la sems lung sgyur bas gsungs//
9a8(1): khams drug 'dzin pa ci slad du//'dod chen par yang ji ltar 'gyur//skyes pa bud med ma ning rnams//ji ltar skye 'gyur bdag la gsungs//rnal 'byor las ni ji ltar ldog/rnal 'byor la yang ji ltar 'jug/ci 'dra'i skyes bu ji lta bur//rnal 'byor gzhag pa
9a9(1): bdag la gsungs//'gro bar phyin pa'i sems can gyi//mtshan nyid mtshan ma ji lta bu//gang gis ji ltar phyug por 'gyur//mkha' 'dras bdag la bka' stsol cig/ji ltar ci yi shAkya'i rigs//bu ram shing skyes ji ltar byung //yun rings
9b1(1): drang srong dka' thub gang //khyod ky-is des ni ji ltar bslabs//khyod nyid zhing rnams thams cad du//mtshan dang de bzhin gzugs sna tshogs//rgyal ba'i sras kyis bskor ba yang //ci yi slad du kun na gda'//ji ltar sha rnams bzar mi rung //ji ltar
9b2(1): sha ni bkag pa lags//sha za'i rigs su skyes pa'i rnams//sha 'tshal bar yang ji ltar 'gyur//ji ltar zhing rnams nyi zla'i dbyibs//ri rab pad+mo 'dra ba dang //dpal be'u seng ge'i dbyibs 'dra ba//ci yi slad du bdag la gsungs//go 'dun
9b3(1): dag dang sbyi tshugs dang //mig 'phrul 'dra ba ji lta bu//rin chen kun gyi zhing rnams kyang //ji ltar ci slad bdag la gsungs//pi bang rnga yi dbyibs 'dra dang //'bras bu me tog sna tshogs 'dra//nyi zla dag dang rnam bral ba//ji ltar ci slad bdag
9b4(1): la gsungs//la la sprul pa'i sangs rgyas dang //sangs rgyas la la rnam smin 'byung //yang dag ye shes sangs rgyas rnams//ji ltar ci slad bdag gsungs//ci slad ji ltar 'dod khams su//rnam sangs ma rgyas bdag la gsungs//ci yi don du
9b5(1): 'og min du//chags bral khrod du mngon sangs rgyas//bde gshegs mya ngan 'das nas go/bstan pa su zhig 'dzin par 'gyur//ston pa ji srid bar du bzhugs//tshul yang ji srid gnas par 'gyur//khyod kyi grub mtha' rnam pa du//lta ba dag kyang
9b6(1): rnam pa du//dge slong dngos dang 'dul ba yang //ci slad ji ltar bdag la gsungs//rang rgyal rgyal sras nyan thos kyi//ci slad 'gyur ba rgyal po dang //snang ba med pa rgyal po yang //ci'i slad pa bdag la gsungs//'jig rten mngon shes gang gis
9b7(1): 'byung //'jig rten 'das kyi'ang ji lta bur//sems dang sa rnams bdun po yang //ji ltar ci slad bdag la gsungs//khyod kyi dge 'dun rnam pa du//ji ltar dge 'dun dbyen du 'gyur//sems can gso ba'i gtsug lag khang //ji lta ci
9b8(1): slad bdag la gsungs//'od srungs log par dad sel dang //gser thub kyang ni nga yin zhes//rgyal ba'i sras la gsung ba de//thub pa chen po ci slad gsung //bdag dang sems can cis ming brjod//rtag dang 'jig gtam ji lta bur//
9b9(1): ci slad sems tsam yang dag nyid//thams cad du ni bshad mi mdzad//ji srid skyes bu na chung tshal//a ru ra dang skyu ru tshal//ti se'i gangs dang khor yug ri//rdo rje sra ba'ang ji lta bu//de yi nang na du ma'i ri//rin chen sna tshogs rab
10a1(1): @#//brgyan cing //drang srong dri zas kun gang ba//ji ltar ci slad bdag la gsungs//theg pa che tshul sna tshogs pa//sangs rgyas rnams kyi thugs mchog 'di//sangs rgyas 'jig rten mkhyen pa'i phul//lha chen gyis ni
10a2(1): gsan pa dang //shes rab chen po legs so legs//khyod kyis yongs su gang dris pa//de ni rim gyis ngas bshad kyis//blo chen nga la shes par byos//skye dang skye ba med pa dang //ji ltar mya ngan 'das dang stong //'pho dang rang bzhin med pa
10a3(1): dang ||sangs rgyas pha rol phyin dang sras//nyan thos rgyal ba'i sras rnams dang //gzugs med spyod pa'i mu stegs dang //ri rab rgya mtsho ri rnams dang //gling dang sa dang zhing rnams dang //skar ma nyi ma zla ba dang //mu stegs lha dang lha
10a4(1): ma yin||rnam thar dbang po mngon shes dang //stobs dang bsam gtan ting nge 'dzin//'gog dang rdzu 'phrul rkang pa dang //byang chub yan lag lam rnams dang //tshad med rnams dang bsam gtan dang //phung po rnams dang 
10a5(1): 'gro 'ong dang //snyoms par 'jug dang 'gog pa dang //ldang dang sna tshogs bshad pa dang //sems dang yid dang rnam shes dang //bdag med pa dang chos lnga dang //rang bzhin rtog dang brtags pa dang //snang dang lha gnyis
10a6(1): ji lta bu//theg pa rnams kyi 'byung gnas dang //gser nor mu tig 'byung ba'i rigs//'dod chen pa dang 'byung chen dang //sangs rgyas gcig dang dkrugs ma dang //shes dang shes bya 'byung 'thob dang //sems can srid dang mi srid dang /
10a7(1): ci phyir glang    po ri dgas rta//ji ltar gzung rung bdag la gsungs//dpe dang rgyu rnams ldan pa yi//grub pa'i mtha' bshad ji lta bur//rgyu dang 'bras bu ci yi phyir//tshul dang nor pa sna tshogs dang //sems tsam de ni snang ba
10a8(1): med||sa rnams la yang rim pa med//snang ba med dang 'gyur ba rnams//brgya pa ci phyir bdag la gsungs//gso ba'i gtsug lag bzo rnams dang //sgyu rtsal rig lung de bzhin te//ri bo de bzhin ri rab dang //sa yi tshad kyang ji lta bur//
10a9(1): nyi ma zla ba rgya mtsho yi//tshad ni ji tsam bdag la gsungs//sems can lus la rdul gyi rnams//rab dang 'bring dang tha ma du//zhing dang zhing la rdul rnams su//'dom dang 'dom la ji snyed mchis//khru 'dom gom pa rgyang grags dang //dpag
10b1(1): tshad phyed dang dpag tshad la//ri bong nyi zer sro ma dang //lug gi dang ni nas kyang du//bre gang la ni nas 'bru du//bre phyed la yang nas 'bru du//de bzhin blo bo khal gcig la//'bum dang bya ba shu rdog du//yungs kar la ni rdul phran du//'ol ma se la
10b2(1): yungs kar du//ri gcig la ni 'ol se du//ma gin la ni ri du yod//zho gcig la ni nam du yod//srang gcig la yang zho du yod//rtsis kyi rtsis mgo 'di lta bur//ri rab la ni srang du yod//de skad bu yis nga la dris//rnam pa gzhan du ci phyir
10b3(1): 'dri||rang rgyal dag dang nyan thos dang //sangs rgyas rnams dang rgyal sras kyi//spu la rdul phran du yod ces//de skad ci phyir 'dri mi byed//me lce la ni rdul phran du//rlung la yang ni rdul phran du//dbang po gcig cing rdul phran du//smin ma'i
10b4(1): spu yi khung bu du//phyug po'i mi rnams ci las 'gyur//'khor lo sgyur ba'i rgyal po yang //rgyal srid cis bsrung de dag gis//de dag ji ltar thar bar 'gyur//tshigs bcad rkyang pa ji ltar smra//'khrig pa 'gro ba sna tshogs dang //zas
10b5(1): skom rnam pa mang po dang //ji ltar skyes bu na chung tshul//ji ltar ci slad ri bo rnams//rdo rje ltar sra bdag la smros//sgyu ma rmi 'dra ci phyir na//smig rgyu mtshungs pa ji lta bur//gang du sprin rnams 'byung bar 'gyur//dus kyi rnams kyang ci las
10b6(1): 'byung||ro yi bro ba ci las gyur//ci las pho mos ma ning rnams//rgyal ba'i sras kyi mdzes pa dag/bu khyod nga la ci phyir 'dri//ji ltar lha yi ri bo rnams//drang srong dri zas brgyan pa dag/grol ba gang du 'gro bar 'gyur//bcings pa su zhig
10b7(1): gang gis 'grol//bsam gtan pa yi yul de gang //sprul pa dang ni mu stegs kyang //yod med byed med ci yi phyir//snang ba las ni ji ltar sbyin//rtog ge dag kyang ji ltar bdag/rtog ge ci phyir 'byung bar 'gyur//byed pa ci yis 'byung bar
10b8(1): 'gyur||'gro ba'ang ci phyir bdag la gsungs//gang gis 'du shes gcad par bgyi//tshims ting 'dzin rtogs par 'gyur//srid gsum choms gnas pa de su//gnas ni gang lags lus kyang ci//bdag dang sems can med cing brjod//
10b9(1): kun rdzob bstan pa ji lta bur//bdag dang mtshan nyid 'dri ci yi phyir//bdag med 'dri ba ji lta bu//snying po sde pa ci yi phyir//nga la 'drir byed rgyal ba'i bu//rtag dang chad par lta ba dang //gang gis sems kyang mnyam par 'jog/brjod dang de bzhin yais dang //tshul khrim+sa rigs
11a1(1): //dang rgyal ba'i bu //bla ma slob ma rigs pa stond //sems can sna tshogs ci lta bu //zas skom nam mkha' yid gzhungs dang //bdud rnams dang ni gtags pa tsam /shing dang khri shing 
11a2(1): ji phyir zhes //ci phyir rgyal sras bdag la 'dri //zhing rnams sna tshogs ci'i phyir //yun ring drang srong dka' thub dang //khyod kyi rigs dang bla ma dang //ci'i phyir nga la rgyal sras 'dri //ci'i phyir na myi dag ngan //'dod pa 
11a3(1): 'i khams su 'tshang myi rgya //'og myin du ni grub pa'i mtha' //ci ltar rigs pa nga la 'dri //ci phyir 'jig rten mngon shes pa //ci ltar dge slong dngos su 'gyur //yuld pa dang ni rnam smin gnas pa'i //sangs rgyas 
11a4(1): nga la ci ltar 'dri //yang dag ye shes sangs rgyas dang //dge 'dun rnams kyang ci ltar 'gyur //pi bang me tog rnga 'dra dang //snang ba spong pa'i zhing rnams rnam dang //sems dang sa rnams 
11a5(1): bdun po dag //nga la rgyal ba'i bu yang 'dri //'di dang gzhan yang rab mang pa'i //re rer mtshan dang rab ldan zhing //lta ba'i nyes pa rnam spangs te //bshad pa phyogs kyi grub pa'i 
11a6(1): mtha' //ngas bshad nga la nyan par gyis //tshig rnams 'god pa ngas bya 'is //tshig ni brgya'i rtsa brgyad po //ji ltar sangs rgyas bshad pa'i rnams //da la bu yis mnyan par 
11a7(1): gyis //de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po blo gros chen pos //bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to //bcom ldan 'das tshig brgya' rtsa brgyad po gang lags //
11a8(1): bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsald pa //skye ba'i tshig dang /myi skye ba'i tshig dang /rtag nga'i tshig dang /myi rtag pa'i tshig dang /mtshan nyid kyi tshig dang /mtshan nyid myed pa'i tshig dang /gnas 
11a9(1): gzhan du 'gyur ba'i tshig dang /gnas gzhan du 'gyur ba ma yin pa'i tshig dang /skad cig pa'i tshig dang /skad cigs myed pa'i tshig dang /rang bzhin yod pa'i tshig dang /rang bzhan myed pa'i tshig dang /
11a10(1): stong pa nyid kyi tshig dang /stong pa nyid ma yin pa'i tshig dang /chad pa'i tshig dang /chad ma yin pa'i tshig dang /sems kyi tshig dang /sems myed pa'i tshig dang /dbu' ma'i tshig dang /dbu' ma'i
11b1(1): ma yin pa'i tshig dang /ther zug pa'i tshig dang /ther zugam yin pa'i tshig dang /rkyen kyi tshig dang /rkyend ma yin pa'i tshig dang /rgyu'i tshig dang /rgyu myed pa'i tshig dang /nyon mongs pa'i tshig dang
11b2(1): nyon mongs pa myed pa'i tshig dang /sred pa'i tshig dang /sred pa myed pa'i tshig dang /thabs kyi tshig dang /thabs ma yin pa'i tshig dang /mkhas pa'i tshig dang /mkhas pa ma yin pa'i
11b3(1): tshig dang /dag pa'i tshig dang /dag pa'i ma yin pa'i tshig dang /rigs pa'i tshig dang /rigs pa ma yin pa'i tshig dang /dpe'i tshig dang /dpe' myed pa'i tshig dang /slob pa'i tshig
11b4(1): dang /slob ma ma yin pa'i tshig dang bla ma'i tshig dang /bla ma'i tshig dang /bla ma ma yin pa'i tshig dang/rigs kyi tshig dang /rigs ma yin pa'i tshig dang theg pa gsum gyi
11b5(1): tshig dang /theg pa gsum ma yin pa'i tshig dang snang pa'i tshig dang /snang ba myed pa'i tshig dang /smond pa'i tshig dang /smond myed pa'i tshig dang /'khor gsum gyi tshig dang
11b6(1): 'khor gsum myed pa'i tshig dang /mtshan ma'i tshig dang /mtshan ma myed pa'i tshig dang /yod myed kyi phyogs kyi tshig dang /yod myed kyi phyigs ma yin pa'i tshig dang/gnyis ka'i tshig drang /
11b7(1): gnyis ka ma yin pa'i tshig dang so so rang gi 'phags pa'i ye shes kyi tshig dang /so so rang gi 'phags pa'i yi shes myed pa'i tshig dang mthong ba'i tshig la bde ba'i tshig dang /mthong ba'i chos la bde ba
11b8(1): myed pa'i tshig dang zhing gi tshig dang /zhing myed pa'i tshig dang rdul gyi tshig dang /rdul myed pa'i tshig dang chu'i tshig dang /chu myed pa'i tshig dang /rmong pa'i tshig dang /rmong ba myed pa'i tshig dang 
11b9(1): yang dag pa'i tshig dang /yang dag pa ma yin pa'i tshig dang /grangs dang rtsis kyi tshig dang /grangs dang /rtsis myed pa'i tshig dang /mngon par shes pa'i tshig dang /mngon par shes pa myed pa'i tshig dang /skyo ba'i tshig
11b10(1): dang /skyo ba myed pa'i tshig dang /stug po'i tshig dang /stug po ma yin pa'i tshig dang rgyu rtsal dang rig pa dang /bzo'i tshig dang /rgyu rtsal dang /rig pa dang /bzo' myed pa'i tshig dang /rlung gi tshig dang
12a1(1): /rlung myed pa'i tshig dang /sa'i tshig dang /sa myed pa'i tshig dang /sems kyi tshig dang /sems myed pa'i tshig dang /'das pa'i tshig dang /'das pa myed pa'i tshig dang /rang bzhin gi tshig dang /
12a2(1): rang bzhin myed pa'i tshig dang /phung po'i tshig dang /phung po myed pa'i tshig dang /semasacan gyi tshig dang /sems can ma yin pa'i tshig dang /blo'i tshig dang /blo myed pa'i tshig dang /mya ngan las 'das pa'i tshig dang /mya ngan
12a3(1): las 'das pa myed pa'i tshig dag /shes bya'i tshig dang /shes bya med pa'i tshig dang /mu stegs kyi tshig dang /mu stegs myed pa'i tshig dang /khrag khrug gi tshig dang /khrag khrug myed pa'i tshig dang /sgyu ma'i tshig dang /
12a4(1): sgyu ma myed pa'i tshig dang /rmyi lam gyi tshig dang /rmyi lam myed pa'i tshig dang /smig rgyu'i tshig dang /smig rgyu myed pa'i tshig dang /gzugs brnyan gi tshig dang /gzugs rnyan myed pa'i tshig dang /'khor lo'i tshig dang
12a5(1): 'khor lo myed pa'i tshig dang /dri za'i tshig dang /dri za myed pa'i tshig dang /lha'i tshig dang /lha ma yin gyi tshig dang /zas skom gyi tshig dang zas skom myed pa'i tshig dang /'khrig pa'i tshig dang /'khrig pa myed
12a6(1): pa'i tshig dang /lta ba'i tshig dang /lta ba myed pa'i tshig dang /pha rold tu phyind pa'i tshig dang /pha rol tu phyind pa myed pa'i tshig dang /tshul khrims kyi tshig dang /tshul khrims myed pa'i tshig dang /nyi zla dang skar ma'i tshig dang /
12a7(1): nyi zla dang skar ma myed pa'i tshig dang /bden pa'i tshig dang /bden ba ma yin pa'i tshig dang /'bras bu'i tshig dang /'bras bu myed pa'i tshig dang /'gog pa'i tshig dang /'gog pa myed pa'i tshig dang /'gogs pa las ldang
12a8(1): pa'i tshig dang /'gog pa las ldang pa myed pa'i tshig dang /gos pa'i tshig dang /gso' ba myed pa'i tshig dang /mtshan kyi tshig dang /mtshan ma myed pa'i tshig dang /yan lag gi tshig dang /yan lag myed pa'i tshig dang /
12a9(1): n+du rtsal gyi tshig dang /rgyu rtsal dang rig pa myed pa'i tshig dang bsam gtan gyi tshig dang /bsam gtan myed pa'i tshig dang /nor pa'i tshig dang /nor ba myed pa'i tshig dang snang ba'i tshig dang /snang ba myed pa'i tshig dang 
12a10(1): sdud pa'i tshig dang /srung ba myed pa'i tshig dang /rus pa'i tshig dang /rus ba myed pa'i tshag dang /drang srong gi tshig dang /dra srong ma yin pa'i tshig dang /rgyal po'i srid kyi tshig dang /rgyal po'i srid ma yin pa'i tshig dang
12b1(1): bzung ba'i tshig dang/bzung myed pa'i tshig dang/rin po che'i tshig dang/rin po che myed pa'i tshig dang/lung bstand pa'i tshig dang/lung bsand pa myed pa'i tshig dang/'dod chen ba chen po'i tshig dang/
12b2(1): 'dod chen pa myed pa'i tshig dang /pho smos dang ma ning gi tshig dang/pho smos ma ning myed pa'i tshig dang/bro ba'i tshig dang/bro ba myed pa'i tshig dang/byed pa'i tshig dang/byed pa myed pa'i tshig dang/lus kyi tshig dang/
12b3(1): lus myed pa'i tshig dang/rtog ge'i tshig dang/rtog ge myed pa'i tshig dang/g.yo' pa'i tshig dang/g.yo' ba myed pa'i tshig dang/dbang po'i tshig dang/dbang po myed pa'i tshig dang/'dus byas kyi tshig dang/'dus
12b4(1): ma byas kyi tshig dang/rgyu dang 'bras bu'i tshig dang/rgyu dang 'bras ba myed pa'i tshig dang/'og ma'i tshig dang/'og myin gyi tshig dang/dus kyi tshig dang/dus myed pa'i tshig dang/shing dang shing gel
12b5(1): pa dang/sdug ma gyes pa'i tshig dang/shing dang shang gel ba dang lcug ma gyes pa myed pa'i tshig dang/sna tshogs kyi tshig dang/sna tshogs myed pa'i tshig dang/bstand pas bzung pa'i tshig dang/bstand bas bzung
12b6(1): pa myed pa'i tshig dang/'dul ba'i tshig dang/'dul ba myed pa'i tshig dang//dge slong gi tshig dang/dge slong myed pa'i tshig dang/byin kyi rlabs kyi tshig dang/byin kyi rlabs myed pa'i tshig dang/yi ge'i tshig dang/
12b7(1): yi ge myed pa'i tshig dang/'ing dag ni blo gros chen po'i sngon gyi sangs rgyas kyis brjod pa'i tshig brgya rtsa brgyad do////'phags pa lang kar gshegs pa//bam po gnyis pa'o////
12b8(1): de nas yang byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po blo gros chen pos/bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsold to//bcom ldan 'das rnam par shes pa rnams kyi skye ba dang gnas pa dang 'gag pa rnam pa du
12b9(1): mchis/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsald pa/blo gros chen po rnam par shes pa rnams kya skye ba dang gnas pa da dang/'gag pa la rnam pa gnyis yod de/rtog pa pa rnams kyis khong du mi chud do//'di lta ste rgyun
12b10(1): 'gags pa dang/mtshan nyid 'gags pa'o//rnam par shes pa rnams kyis skye ba yang rnam pa gnyis ste/rgyu skye ba dang mtshan nyid skye ba'o//gnas pa rnams gnyis te/rgyu gnas pa dang mtshan nyid gnas pa'o//blo ces chen po rnam par shes
12b11(1): pa 
13a1(1): //ni rnam pa gsum ste /'jug pa'i mtshan nyid dang /las kyi mtshan nyid dang /rigs kyi mtshan nyid do //blo gros chen po mtshan nyid brgyad du gsungs pa'i rnam par shes pa ni mo dang rnam pa gnyis ste /snang ba
13a2(1): 'i rnam par shes pa dang/dngos po so sor rnam par rtog pa'i rnam par shes pa'o //blo gros chen po ci ltar me long la gzugs bzhin du snang ba la snang pa'i rnam par shes pa 'dzind te /blo gros chen po snang ba'i rnam par shes pa
13a3(1): dang /dngos po so sor rnam parartog pa'i rnam par shes pa de gnyis mtshan nyid tha myi dad cing gcig gi rgyu gcig go //blo gros chen po de la snang pa'i rnam par shesapa ni bsam gyis myi khyab pa'i bag chags yongs su 'gyur
13a4(1): pa'i rgyu las 'byung ba'o //blo gros chen po dngos po so sor rnam par rjog pa'i rnam par shes pa'i yul la rnam par rtog pa'i rgyu las byung ba dang /thog ma myed pa'i dus kyi spros pa'i bag chags kyi rgyu las 'byung ba'o //
13a5(1): blo gros chen po dbang po'i rnam par shes pa thams cad 'gag pa na 'di lta ste /kun gzhi'i rnam par shes pa yang dag pa ma yin par kun tu rtog pa'i bag chags sna tshogs 'gog pa ste //blo gros chen po 'di ni
13a6(1): mtshan nyid 'gag pa'o //blo gros chen po rgyun 'gag pa ni gang las byung ba yin te /blo gros chen po gang las shes bya ba ni gang la gnas pa dang /gang la dmigs pa'o //de la gang de la gnas pa zhes bya
13a7(1): ba 'di ni thog ma myed pa'i dus kyi sros pa gnas dang len gyi bag chags so //gang la dmigs pa zhes bya ba ni bdag gi sems snang pa rnam par shes pa'i yul du rnam par rtog pa'o //blo gros chen po'i de lta ste dper na /
13a8(1): 'jim pa la rdul phra rab dang 'jim pa'i gong bu ni gcig pa 'ang ma yin /tha dad pa 'ang ma yin pa dang /ci ltar gser dang brgyan bzhin te /blo gros chen po ci ste 'jim pa'i gong bu 'jim pa'i rdul phra rab dang tha dad pa zhig yin na ni //
13a9(1): de dag las tsams pa ma yin par gyurd na /de ni 'jim pa rdul phra rab de dag las kyang tsams pas na de'i phyir tha dad pa ma yin no //de ste tha dad pa ma yin na ni 'jim pa'i gong bu dang rdul phra rab bye brag myed par 'gyur ro //de bzhin du blo
13a10(1): gros chen po 'jug pa'i rnam par shes pa rnams kun gzhi'i rnam par shes pa'i rigs kyi mtshan nyid dang /gal te tha dad pa yin na ni kun gzhi'i rnam par shesapa'i rgyu las 'byung bar myi 'gyur ro //de ste tha dad pa ma yi na ni 'jug pa'i
13b1(1): rnam par shes pa 'gags na kun gzhi'i rnam par shes pa yang 'gags par 'gyur na /de ltar bdag gi rigs kyi mtshan nyid 'gags par yang myi 'gyur ro//de'i phyir blo gros chen po rnam par shes pa rnams kyi rang gi rigs kyi mtshan nyid
13b2(1): 'gag par myi 'gyur gyi /las kyi mtshan nyid na 'gag go //rang gi rigs kyi mtshan nying 'gags na kun gzhi'i rnams par shes pa yang 'gag par 'gyur te /blo gros chen po kun gzhi'i rnam par shes ba 'gag na ni smra ba 'di yang mu
13b3(1): stegs byed pa'i chad par lta ba dang /khyad par myed par 'gyur ro //blo gros chen po mu stegs byed pa rnams kyis smra ba ni 'di lta ste /'di ltar yul la 'dzind pa rgyun chad pas rnam par shes pa'i rgyun chad par 'gyur te /rnam par shes
13b4(1): pa'i rgyun chad pas thog ma myed pa'i dus kyi rgyun chad par 'gyur ba'o //blo gros chen po mu ste gas byed pa rnams kyi rgyu las rgyun byung par smra ba'i /rgyu ma gtogs po'i myig rnams par shes pas gzugs dang /snang ba 'dus pa las 'byung bar myi
13b5(1): smra ste /blo gros chen po rgyu ni gtso bo dang /skyes bu dang /dbang po dang /dus dang /rdul phra mor smra ba'o //gzhan yang blo gros chen po dngos po'i rang bzhin bdun yod de /ci ltar 'du ba dngos po'i rang bzhin dang /
13b6(1): dngos po rang bzhin gyi dngos dang /mtshan nyid kyi dngos dang /'byung pa chen po'i dngos po'i rang bzhin gyi dngos dang/rgyu'i dngos po dang /rkyend kyi dngos po dang /grub pa'i dngos po ste bdun no//blo gros chen po
13b7(1): gzhan yang don rnams pa bdun te /'di ltar sems kyi spyod yul dang /ye shes kyi spyod yul dang /lta ba gnyis kyi spyod yul dang /lta pa gnyis las 'das pa'i spyod yul dang /snang ba myed pa'i spyod yul
13b8(1): dang /sras kyi sa las 'das pa'i spyod yuladang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i so so'i rang gi rig pa'i spyod yul te /'di dag ni blo gros chen po 'das pa dang ma 'ongs pa dang /da ltar gyi de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang
13b9(1): dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyis dngos po'i rang bzhin dang/don dam pa'i snying po yin te /dngos po'i rang bzhin dang /don dam pa' snying po de dang ldan pas /de bzhin gshegs pa rnams 'jig rten dang
13b10(1): 'jig rten las 'das pa dang /'jig rten las 'das ba'i mchog gi chos rang dang spyi'i mtshan nyid du gtogs pa rnams dang /'phags pa'i shes rab kyi spyan gyis rnam par dgod de /ji ltar mu stegs byed pa'i smra ba dang /
14a1(1): @#//lta ba ngan pa dang thun mong du mi 'gyur ba de ltar rnam par 'god do//blo gros chen po dzaM ltar na mu stegs byed pa'i smra ba dang /lta ba ngan pa dang thun mang ma yin pa yin zhe na/'di lta ste/blo gros chen po rnam par
14a2(1): shes pa rnams kyi bdag gi yul la rnam par rtog pa'i lta ba khong du ma chud pa dang /bdag gi sems snang ba tsam du mi 'jug pas byis pa so so'i skye bo rnams dngos po dang dngos po med pa'i rang bzhin don dam par lta ba gnyis su smra
14a3(1): bar gyur to//blo gros chen po gzhan yang mi shes pa dang /sred pa dang /las kyi rkyen rnam par log pa/rnam par rtog pa/srid pa gsum gyi sdug bsngal rnam par zlog pa/rang gi sems snang ba'i yul sgyu ma rjes su mthong 
14a4(1): ba bshad par bya'o//blo gros chen po dge sbyong ngam/bram ze gang la la ma byung ba 'am/yod pa rgyu las 'bras bu mngon pa dang /dngos po yun du gnas pa dang /rkyen rnams las phung po dang /khams dang /skye mched
14a5(1): rnams skye ba dang /gnas pa dang /byung nas kyang 'jig par 'dod do /blo gros chen po de dag ni rgyun dang /bya ba dang /skye ba dang /srid pa dang /mya ngan las 'das pa dang /lam dang /las dang 'bras bu dang /
14a6(1): bden pa dang rnam par 'jig pa dang /chad par lta bar 'gyur te/de ci'i phyir zhe na/mngon sum dang mi dmigs pa dang /thog ma mthong ba med pa'i phyir ro//blo gros chen po 'di lta ste/gyo mo ni rdza ma med pas rdza ma'i
14a7(1): bya ba mi byed do//sa bon tshig pa'ang myu gu'i bya ba mi byed de/blo gros chen po de bzhin du phung po dang khams dang /skye mched kyi dngos po de dag 'gags pa dang /'gag pa dang /'gag par 'gyur ba ni bdag ni sems
14a8(1): snang ba rnam par rtog pa mthong ba'i rgyu las byung bas de dag ni rgyun mi chad par 'byung ba med do//blo gros chen po gal te ma byung ba 'am/yod pa las rnam par shes pa rnams gsum phrad pa'i rkyen gyi byed pa ldan pa las 'byung du
14a9(1): zin na ni blo gros chen po ru sbal gyi spu lta bu med pa rnams dang /bye ma las kyang 'bru mar 'byung bar 'gyur ro//blo gros chen po yod pa dang med pa las 'byung bar smra ba las dam bcas pa nyams pa dang /nges pa las
14b1(1): 'gal ba dang /bya ba dang las dang byed pa don med par yang 'gyur ro//blo gros chen po de dag la'ang gsum phrad pa'i rkyen gyi byed pa dang ldan pas rgyu dang 'bras bu dang /rang gi mtshan nyid du ston pa yod de/'das pa dang /
14b2(1): ma 'ongs pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i yod pa dang /med pa'i mtshan nyid yod par rig pa dang /gzhung gis brtags pa'i sa la gnas shing rang gi lta ba nyes pa'i bag chags kyis ston te/blo gros chen po de ltar byis pa so so'i
14b3(1): skye bo rnams nyes pa'i lta bas zin pa mi mnyam pa'i blo can rnams mi shes pas bstan pa la thams cad mkhyen pas bstan to zhes zer ro//blo gros chen po dge sbyong ngam bram ze gang la la rang bzhin med pa sprin dang 'gal me
14b4(1): bskor ba dang /dri za'i grong khyer dang /ma skyes pa dang /sgyu ma dang /smig rgyu ba dang /chu'i zla ba dang /rmi lam dang /phyi rol tu sems kyi snang ba rnam par rtog pa dang thog ma med pa'i dus kyi spros pa mthong bas rang gi
14b5(1): sems snang ba/rnam par rtog pa'i rkyen phyir ldog pa med pa/yongs su brtags pa'i brjod pa dang /mtshan dang mtshan gyi gzhi dang /brjod par bya ba dang bral ba/lus dang longs spyod dang /gnas su snang ba'i
14b6(1): kun gzhi rnam par shes pa dang /yul dang /gzung ba dang /'dzin pa dang rnam par bral ba/snang ba med pa'i spyod yul/skye ba dang gnas pa dang 'jig pa spangs pa/bdag gi sems skye ba dang ldan pa rnam par
14b7(1): sgom par 'gyur te/blo gros chen po byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po de dag ni ring po mi thogs par 'khor ba dang mya ngan las 'das pa mnyam pa nyid 'thob par 'gyur ro//blo gros chen po snying rje chen po dang thabs
14b8(1): mkhas pa lhun gyis grub par gyur pa'i sbyor ba dang /sems can gyi khams thams cad sgyu ma dang /gzugs brnyan dang mnyam pa nyid dang /rkyen gyis ma brtsams pa dang /phyi nang gi yul rnam par dben pa dang /
14b9(1): sems phyi rol tu lta bas mtshan ma med pa'i byin gyi rlabs dang ldan pa rnams rim gyis sa'i rim pa ting nge 'dzin gyi yul la rjes su 'jug pa dang /khams gsum bdag gi sems sgyu mar shin tu mos pas rab tu sgom pas
15a1(1): /sgyu ma lta bu'i ting nge 'dzin rab tu 'thob par 'gyur ro//bdag gi sems snang ba med pa tsam du la 'jug pas shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la gnas pa rjes su thob par skye ba dang /bya ba dang ldan pa'i
15a2(1): byang chub sems dpa' rnams ting nge 'dzin rdo rje'i gzugs lta bu de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku'i rjes su song ba de bzhin nyid kyi sprul pa dang ldan pa/stobs dang /mngon par shes pa dang /dbang dang /snying brtse ba dang /
15a3(1): snying rje dang thabs kyis brgyan pa/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi zhing gi mu stegs can gyi gnas su nye bar 'gro ba/sems dang yid dang /yid kyi rnam par shes pa dang bral ba/rim gyis gnas 'phos pa'i de bzhin gshegs
15a4(1): pa'i sku rab tu 'thob par 'gyur //blo gros chen po de bas na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku rjes su 'brang ba rab tu 'thob pa rnams kyis phung po dang /khams dang skye mched dang /
15a5(1): sems dang rgyu dang /rkyen dang bya ba dang ldan pa dang /skye bar gang gnas pa dang /'jig par rnam par rtog pa'i spros pa dang bral bar sems tsam gyis rjes su 'brang bar bya'o//thog ma med pa'i dus kyi sprul pa'i gnas ngan
15a6(1): len gyi bag chags byung ba'i sred pa gsum po rab tu mthong nas/sangs rgyas kyi snang ba med pa/mi skye ba dran pa'i phyir 'phags pa so so rang gi chos rtogs par khong du chud pas bdag gi sems la dbang sgyur zhing /lhun
15a7(1): gyis grub pa'i sbyor ba rtogs par khong du chud bas gzugs sna tshogs kyi nor bu dang 'dra ba shin tu phra zhing sems can gyi sems kyi rjes su 'jug pa'i sprul pa'i gzugs dang /sems tsam du 'jug pas sa'i rim pa'i
15a8(1): mtshams sbyor ba 'brel ba rab tu 'jog par 'gyur ro//blo gros chen po de bas na byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen pos bdag gi grub pa'i mtha' la mkhas par bya'o//de nas yang blo gros chen pos gsol pa/
15a9(1): bcom ldan 'das sems dang yid dang rnam par shes pa dang chos lnga dang /rang bzhin gyi mtshan nyid sangs rgyas dang /byang chub sems dpa' rnams rjes su dong ba bdag gi sems snang ba'i spyod yul dang rnam par
15b1(1): bral bar byed pa/brjod pa dang /rigs pa dang /de kho na nyid kyi mtshan nyid rnam par 'jig pa/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi gsung rab kyi snying po chos kyi rnam grangs kyi me tog bshad du gsol/lang+ka'i grong rdal dang /
15b2(1): ma la ya'i rgya mtsho la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi slad du kun gzhi rnam par shes pa'i spyod yul rgya mtsho'i rlabs lta bu chos ky-i sku de bzhin gshegs pas rjes su gsungs pa bshad du gsol/de nas
15b3(1): bcom ldan 'das kyis yang byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po blo gros chen po la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//blo gros chen po m-ig gi rnam par shes pa ni rgyu ba zhi dag gis 'byung ngo //bzhi gang zhe na/'di lta ste/
15b4(1): rang gi sems snang ba la 'dzin par khong du ma chud pa dang /thog ma med pa'i dus nas spros pa'i gnas ngan len gyi gzugs kyi bag chags la mngon par zhen pa dang /rnam par shes pa'i rang bzhin gyi ngo bo nyid dang /gzugs
15b5(1): rnam pa sna tshogs kyi mtshan nyid la ngo mtshar du 'dzin pa yin te/blo gros chen po rgyu bzhi po 'di dag gis kun gzhi rnam par shes pa chu'i klung lta bu las 'jug pa'i rnam par shes pa'i rlabs 'byung ngo //blo gros chen po mag gi
15b6(1): rnam par shes pa la dri lta ba bzhin du dbang po thams cad kyi rdul phra rab dang /spu'i khung bu rnam la'ang de bzhin te/me long la cig car dang /rim gyis yul gyi gzugs snang ba dang I/rgya mtsho la rlung gis btab
15b7(1): ba bzhin du blo gros chen po yul gyi rlung dang /sems gyi rgya mtsho'-i rlabs rgyu'i bya ba rgyun ma chad pa'i mtshan nyid gcig dang /tha dad pa las rnam par grol ba las dang rigs kyi mtshan nyid dang /shin tu 'brel pa'i rang bzhin
15b8(1): khong du mi chud pa rnam par shes pa'i tshogs lnga 'jug go/blo gros chen po rnam par shes pa'i tshogs lga po de dag dang lhan cig tu rgyu dang yul gyi bye brag 'byed pa'i mtshan nyid khong du chud par byed pa zhes bya ba'i
15b9(1): yid kyi rnam par shes pa de dag gi rgyu las byung ba'i bdag nyid rab tu 'jug ste/de dag dang de 'di snyam du bdag cag ni lhan cig tu phan tshun rgyur gyur cing /rang gi (sems)snang ba'i rnam par rtog pa la mngon par zhen pas
16a1(1): @#//zhugs pa'o snyam du mi sems so//'on kyang phan tshun tha mi dad pa'i mtshan nyid du lhan cig rnam par rig pa'i yul bye brag 'byed pa la 'jug ste/de ltar 'jug pa'i tshe mnyam par (mtha' ma )bzhag pa'i
16a2(1): rnal 'byor can gyis kyang ji ltar shin tu phra bar rgyu ba'i bag chags 'jug pa rnams mi shes par rgyu'o rnal 'byor can rnams ni 'di ltar bdag cag rnam par shes pa rnams bkag nas snyoms par 'jug go snyam du sems kyang /de dag
16a3(1): ni rnam par shes pa ma 'gags bzhin du snyoms par 'jog ste/bag chags kyi sa bon ma 'gags pa'i phyir ni ma 'gags pa'o//yul la 'jug cing 'dzin pa med pas ni 'gags so//blo gros chen po kun gzhi rnam par shes
16a4(1): pa rgyu zhing 'jug pa de ltar shin tu phra bas de bzhin gshegs pa dang sa la rab tu gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnams ma gtogs par gzhan nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas dang /mu stegs can gyi rnal 'byor gyi rnal 'byor pa rnam(sa)
16a5(1): kyis khong du chud pa 'am/ting nge 'dzin dang /shes rab kyi stobs kyis kyang rtogs par bla ba ma yin no//blo gros chen po gzhan du na sa'i mtshan nyid dang /shes rab dang /ye shes la mkhas shing /tshig rab tu phye ba
16a6(1): la rnam par gdon mi za ba rgyal ba rnams la mtha' yas pa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba bsags pa/rang gi sems snang ba la rnam par rtog pa'i spros pa dang bral ba I                nags tshal stug po dang /phug dang shing khrod na gnas
16a7(1): shing rnal 'byor rab dang 'bring dang tha ma la rnal 'byor pa rnams kyis rang gi sems kyi rnam par rtog pa snang ba'i rgyud mthong ba dang /zhing mtha' yas pa'i rgyal ba rnams kyis dbang bskur ba dang /dbang dang stobs dang /mngon
16a8(1): par shes pa dang /ting nge 'dzin rnams kyang thob par nus so//blo gros chen po dge ba'i bshes gnyen gyi skye bo la ri mor byas pa rnams kyis sems dang /yid dang yid kyi rnam par shes pa rang bzhin sems snang ba'i rang 
16a9(1): bzhin gyi spyod yul rnam par rtog pa'i 'khor ba la srid pa'i rgya mtsho las dang /sred pa dang /mi shes pa'i rgyu las byung ba las rgal bar nus so//blo gros chen pa de'i phyir rnal 'byor can gyis dge ba'i bshes gnyen gyi skya bo dang 
16b1(1): bsten pa'i nan tan brtsam par bya'o//de nas de'i tshe bcom ldan 'das kyis tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag bka' stsal to//ji ltar rgya mtsho'i dba' rlabs rnams//rlung gi rkyen gyis lhung ba dag/gar cing rab tu 'byung ba ni//rgyun yang rnam
16b2(1): par 'chad pa med//kun gzhi chu bo de bzhin du//rtag tu yul gyi rlung gis bskyod//sna tshogs rnam shes dba' rlabs rnams//gar cing rab tu 'byung par 'gyur//sngon po dmar po lan tshwa dang //mar dang 'o ma khar dang //bskaba'i
16b3(1): 'bras bu me tog dang //ji ltar nyi ma'i 'od zer dang //rgya mtsho yi ni dba' rlabs dag/mi gcig tha dad min par shes//de bzhin rnam shes bdun po yang //sems dang lhan cig ldan pa'o//rgya mtsho yongs su gyur pa ni//dba'
16b4(1): rlabs sna tshogs de dag ste//rnam par shes zhes bya ba ni//kun gzhi de bzhin sna tshogs 'byung //sems dang yid dang rnam shes pa//mtshan nyid don du rab tu brtag/brgyad po tha dad ma yin mtshan//mtshan gyi gzhi dang mtshan
16b5(1): nyid med//rgya mtsho dang ni dba' rlabs rnams//ji ltar khyad par med pa bzhin//sems dang rnam shes de bzhin du//yongs su gyur pa mi rnyed do//sems ni las rnams sogs par byed//yid kyang rnam par sogs pa
16b6(1): ste||rnam par shes pas rnam par rig/lnga po snang la rtog par byed//sngon po dmar po rnam par ni//mi yi rnam par shes pa gda'//dba' rlabs sems dang mthun pa'i chos//ci slad thub chen bshad du gsol//
16b7(1): sngon po dmar po'i rnam pa rnams//dba' rlabs dag la ma mchis te//mtshan nyid don du byis pa la//sems kyi 'jug pa bshad pa'o//de la 'jug pa med pa ste//rang gi sems ni gzung ba spangs//gzung ba
16b8(1): yod na 'dzin pa yang //dba' rlabs mthun par sgrub par 'gyur//lus dang longs spyod gnas lta bur//mi yi rnam par shes par snang //de phyir de yi 'jug pa ni//dba' rlabs dag dang 'dra bar snang //
16b9(1): dba' rlabs dngos por rgya mtsho ni//gar byed rnam par rig 'gyur na//kun gzhi 'jug pa de bzhin du//ci yi phyir na blos mi rig/byis pa blo dang mi ldan pas//kun gzhi rgya mtsho ji bzhin du//dba' rlabs 'byung ba'i
17a1(1): @#//chos mthun phyir//dpe yis nye bar sbyar ba yin//skye bo bzang ngan bar ma la//gdugs 'char 'gyur ba ji bzhin du//de bzhin 'jig rten sgron ma khyod//bus pa rnams la yang dag ston//chos pa
17a2(1): rab tu bzhugs mdzad cing //ci slad de bzhin nyid mi gsung //de ste de bzhin nyid gsung na//sems la de bzhin nyid ma mchis//ji ltar rgya mtsho'i dba' rlabs dang //mtshan nyid rnam pa ji bzhin du//cig cha'i dus su snang ba
17a3(1): bzhin||sems kyang rang gi spyod yul ba//yul dang rnam par mi ldan pas//rim gyis 'jug par rab tu 'byung //rnam par shes pas rnam par rig/yid kyis de bzhin snyems par byed//lnga po rnams la snang bar mngon//
17a4(1): mnyam par bzhag la rim pa med//ri mo'i mkhan po la la 'am//ri mo mkhan gyi slob ma'ang rung //ri mo'i don du tshon 'gyed pa//bshad pa 'di yang de dang 'dra//tshon la ri mo yod pa min//bir dang snod la'ang de bzhin med//sems can
17a5(1): drang ba'i don gyi phyir//tshon gyi ri mo rnam par brtag/yang dag la ni yi ge med//bshad pa dag ni 'khrul pa'o//chos rnams la ni gnas bcas shing //rnal 'byor can la yang dag bstan//so so rang rig yang dag ni//brtag dang rtog pas
17a6(1): yang dag spangs//rgyal ba'i sras la 'di bstan gyi//byis pa rnams la bshad ma yin//ji ltar sgyu ma sna tshogs dag//snang yang yod pa ma yin te//de bzhin bshad pa sna tshogs dag/bshad pa rnam par 'khrul pa yin//gzhan
17a7(1): la gang zhig bstan pa de//gzhan la bshad par mi rung ste//ji ltar nad pa so so la//sman pa sman rnams gtong ba ltar//sangs rgyas de bzhin sems can la//sems can dul bar rab tu gsung //mgon po rnams ni gang gsung
17a8(1): ba'i||so so rang rig spyod yul ni//rtog pa can gyi yul ma yin//nyan thos rnams kyi'ang de bzhin min//blo gros chen po gzhan yang byang chub sems dpa' rang gi sems snang ba'i gzung ba dang //'dzin pa la rnam par rtog pa'i
17a9(1): spyod yul yongs su shes par 'dod pas 'du ba dang //'du 'dzi dang /gnyid dang /spros pa med par bya'o//srod dang /tho rangs dang /nam gyi gung la mi nyal ba'i sbyor ba la brtson par bya'o//mu stegs can dang /
17b1(1): ngan pa'i gtsug lag dang /gtam dang /nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas (྾)kyi theg pa'i mtshan nyid dang bral bar bya'o//byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen pos rang gi sems snang ba la rnam par rtog pa'i mtshan nyid khong 
17b2(1): du chud par rtogs par bya'o//gzhan yang blo gros chen po byang chub sems dpa'i chen po sa sems dang shes rab dang /ye shes kyi mtshan nyid rnam par dgod pa la gnas te/gong du 'phags pa'i ye shes kyi mtshan nyid gsum la
17b3(1): brtson par bya'o//de la blo gros chen po gong du 'phags pa'i ye shesakyi mtshan nyid gsum la brtson par bya'o//de la blo gros chen po gong du 'phaDa pa'i ye shes kyi mtshan nyidagasum po gang zhe na/'di lta ste/snang ba med
17b4(1): pa'i mtshan nyid dang /sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rang gi smon lam gyi byin gyi rlabs kyi mtshan nyid dang /'phags pa so so rang gi     ye shes kyi mtshan nyid de/de dag khong du chud pas rnal 'byor can bong bu theng bo lta bur sems
17b5(1): dang /shes rab dang /ye shes kyimatshan nyid spangs te/rgyal bas sras kyi sa brgyad po thob nas de'i gong du mtshan nyid gsum la brtsod par byed do//de la blo gros chen po thams cad snang ba med pa'i mtshan nyid ni nyan thos
17b6(1): dang /rang sangs rgyas dang /mu stegs can gyi mtshan nyid yongs su 'dris pa las 'byung ngo //blo gros chen po byin gyi  rlabs kyi mtshan nyid ni sngon gyi sangs rgyas rnams kyi rang gi smon lam gyi byin gyi rlabs las 'byung ngo //blo gros
17b7(1): chen po so so               rang gis rig su 'phags pa'i ye shes kyi mtshan nyid ni sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad dang /mtshan nyid thams cad la mngon par zhen pa med pa dang /sgyu ma lta bu'i ting nge 'dzin gyi lus rab tu thob pas sangs
17b8(1): rgyas kyis khong du chud pas rtogs pa rgyu las 'byung ngo //blo grosachen po 'di ni 'phags pa rnams kyi mtshan nyid gsum ste/'phags pa'i mtshan nyid gsum dang ldan na 'phags pa rnams 'phags pa bdag gis so so rang gi ye shes

18a1(1): @#//dpa' chen po blo gros chen pos byang cub sems dpa'i 'khor de dag gi sems dang /bsam pa'i spyod pa rab tu shes nas 'phags pa'i ye shes kyi dngos po rab tu 'byed pa zhes bya ba'i chos kyi rnam grangs
18a2(1): bcom ldan 'das la zhus so//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams gang la gnas shing /byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams la rang dang spyi'i mtshan nyid du 
18a3(1): gtogs pa yongs su brtags pa'i rang bzhin rtogs pa rab tu phye ba'i tshul ston pa dang /yongs su brtags pa'i rang bzhin rtogs pa rab tu phyes ba'i tshul bye brag shin tu rtogs pa gang gis gang zag dang /chos la bdag med pa'i rab tu rgyu
18a4(1): ba shin tu sbyangs te/sa rnams la mkhas par byas pa rnams nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas dang /mu stegs byed pa thams cad kyi bsam gtan dang /ting nge 'dzin dang /snyoms par 'jug pa'i bde ba las 'das te/de bzhin
18a5(1): gshegs pas mkhyen pa'i yul la 'jug pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i chos lnga dang /rang bzhin rtogs pa las rnam par log pa/de bzhin gshegs pa'i chos kyi sku shes rab dang /ye shes shin tu 'brel ba'i chos can sgyu ma'i spyod
18a6(1): yul gyi mngon par grub pa/sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad dang /dga' ldan gyi pho brang dang /'og min gyi gnas su nye bar bzhud pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku rab tu thob par 'gyur ba/'phags pa'i ye shes kyi dngos po

18a8(1): pa la mngon par zhen pa dag rnam par rtogs pa'i blo rgyu zad pa'i rang bzhin med pa'i phyir ri bong la rwa med do snyam ste/ji ltar ri bong gi rwa med pa de bzhin du chos thams cad la'ang rnam par rtog go/blo gros chen po gzhan
18a9(1): yang 'byung ba dang /yon tan dang /rdul dang rdzas dang dbyibs dang /dpe tshul gyi khyad par mthong nas ri bong gi rwa med par mngon par chags shing zhen pa rnams ba lang gi rwa yod do snyam du rnam par rtog go/blo gros chen po mtha'

18b8(1): byung ba ma yin par 'bar ro//de ste tha dad pa ma yin na ni de'i rgyu las byung bas rdul phra rab kyi bar du rab tu brtags na ni dmigs pas ra dang tha dad pa ma yin pa'i phyir/de'ang med par 'gyur te/de gnyis ka'i dngos po
