72b9(1): ma byas gang yang myed do//sgyu ma'i skyes bu ni yod pa dang myed pa ci yang myi byed pas/skye ba 'am 'gag pa myed do//de bzhin du chos thams cad skye ba dang 'jig pa spangs pa ste/gzhan du na byis pa rnams log pa'i ltung pa'i 'du 
72b10(1): shes kyis skye ba dang 'gag pa rtogs ste/'phags pa rnams ni ma yin no//blo gros chen po log pa zhes bya ba ni ci ltar dngos po'i rang bzhin brtags pa de bzhin du yang ma yin/gzhan du yang ma yin no//gzhan du brtags 
73a1(1): //na ri dngos po thams cad kyi ngo bo nyid la chags pa nyid du 'gyur bas dben pa myi mthong ngo //dben pa ma mthong na rnam par rtog pa ldog par myi 'gyur ro //des na de'i phyir blo gros chen po mtshan ma myed pa mthong
73a2(1): pa ni dge' ba yin te /mtshan ma mthong ba ni ma yin no //mtshan ma ni skye ba'i rgyu yin pas myi dge' ba'o//blo gros chen po mtshan ma med pa ni rnam par rtog pa myi 'jug pa yin te /myi skye ba mya ngan las 'das par bshad do //
73a3(1): de la blo gros chen po mya ngan las 'das pa ni yang dag pa gnas pa ci lta bzhin du mthong bas rnam par rtog pa sems dang sems las 'byung ba mang po shind tu 'gyur bas /sngar drangs pa de bzhin gshegs pa 'phags pa bdag gis
73a4(1): so so rang gi ye shes kyi thugs su chud pa ni mya ngan las 'das pa zhes bshad do //de la 'di skad ces bya ste //skye ba rnam par bzlog pa'i phyir //myi skye ba ni rab tu sgrub //rgyu myed smra ba bshad pa ni //byis pa rnams kyis myi
73a5(1): rig go //'di dag thams cad ma skyes par //dngos po myed cing rgyun kyang myed //rmi lam sgyu ma 'dra dri za'i grong //rgyu myed pa'i dngos po yod //ma skyes rang bzhin myed pa dang //ci phyir stong
73a6(1): pa'ang bdag la gsungs //'dus pa dag ni ma gtogs par //blo yis dngos po myi rig go//de phyir ma skyes stong pa ste //rang bzhin myed par ngas bshad do //de bzhin 'dus pa re rer yang //snang ba
73a7(1): myed pas yod ma yin //'dus pa mu stegs lta ba ltar //'jig pas myed par 'gyur ba myin //rmi lam skra shad 'dzings pa dang //sgyu ma smig rgyu dri za' grong //rgyu myed pa dag snang ba ltar //'jig rten sna tshogs
73a8(1): de bzhin no //rgyu myed smra pas brtsad byas te //ma skyes pa yang rab tu sgrubs //skye ba myed par rab sgrubs na //da'i tshul ni myi 'jig go //rgyu myed smra ba bstand pa ni //ci ltar ci las ci dag gis //gang du rgyu
73a9(1): myed skyer 'gyur zhes //mu stegs can rnams 'jigs pa skye //rgyu las ma yin rgyu myed par //nam zhig 'dus byas mthong ba na //de la skye 'jigs smra ba'i //lta ba rnam par ldog par 'gyur //dngos po myed par skye
73a10(1): myed dam //de ste rkyend la brtag pa 'am //'on te dngos po'i mying lags sam //don myed pa 'ang bdag la gsungs //dngos po myed myi skye ma yin te //rkyen la brtag pa 'ang ma yin no//dngos po'i mying yang de myin te //
73b1(1): don myed pa 'ang mying ma yin //nyan thos dang ni rang rgyal dang /sa bdun du ni phyind rnams dang /mu stegs spyod yul myin pa'o//de ni myi skye mtshan nyid do//rgyu dang rkyend rnams bzlog pa dang /byed pa dag ni 'gag pa'o/
73b2(1): sems tsam du ni rnam bzhag pa/skye ba myed par ngas bshad do//dngos po rgyu las 'byung myed pa//rtog dang rtag pa sangs pa ste//yod myed phyogs las grol ba ste//skye ba myed par ngas bshad de//
73b3(1): sems ni snang las rnam grol ba//rang bzhin gnyis kyang sangs pa ste//gnas rnams rab tu gyurd pa ni//skye ba myed par ngas bzhad do//phyi rold dngos po dngos myed myin//sems kyang yongs su bzung pa 
73b4(1): myed//lta ba thams cad rab sangs pa//de ni skye mched mtshan nyid do//de bzhin stong pa rang bzhin myed//de la gsogs pa'i tshig kun brtag//nam yang stong pas stong ma yin//skye ba myed pI
73b5(1): stong pa'o//rkyen kyis rnam par 'dus pa las//rab tu 'jug cing ldog par 'gyur//'dus pa las ni logs shig na//skye ba myed cing 'gag pa 'ang myed//gcig pa dang ni tha dad du//ci ltar
73b6(1): mu stegs can rtogs bzhin//dngos po tha dad ma yin dang //'dus las gud na gang na'ang myed//yod myed dngos po myin ba ste//myed myin yod myed gang na'ang myed//de phyir gzhan du 'dus pa ni//rab tu 'jug cing
73b7(1): ldog par 'gyur//'di ni gtags pa tsam nyid de//phan tshun ltos pa 'breld pa'o//rkyend rnams//'breld pa las logs shig na//bskyed par bya ba'i don myed do//bskyed pa myed pas skye ba myed//
73b8(1): mu stegs can gyi skyon gyis spangs//'breld pa tsam du ngas bshad de//byis pa rnams kyis myi rig go//gang na 'breld las logs shig dang //dngos po bskyed pa yod ces pa//'breld pa rnam par 'jig pa 
73b9(1): ste//rgyu myed smra bar shes par bya//gang na 'breld las sogs shig na//dngos po su la yod na ni//mar me bzhin du rdzas rigs rnams//'breld pa snang ba byed par 'gyur//'breld pa dag las logs shig tu
73b10(1): chos gag myi mkhas rtog pa ni//ma skyes ngo bo nyid myed de//rang bzhin nam mkha' lta bu'o//'phags pa thob pa'i chos nyid ni//skye ba myed pa gzhan yin te//gang de'i skye ba myi 'byung ba//de ni myi skye
74a1(1): #//bzod pa yin//nam zhig 'jig rten 'di dag kun//'di ni 'breld pa tsam (ma)zhes//'breld pa nyid du mthong ba na//de na sems kyang mnyam par 'jog/myi shes sred pa las la stsogs//
74a2(1): 'breld pa dag ni nang gi yin//'khor lo chu shing 'jim la sogs//sa bon 'byung stsogs phyi rol lo//'breld pa tsam (pa)'di dag myin//gang na dngos po rkyend gzhan las kha gcig skye bar 'dod pa ni//rigs dang 
74a3(1): yul la de myi gnas//gal te bskyed bya ba'i dngos myed ni//blo yang gang gi rkyen las 'byung //'di dag phan tshun skyed pa ste//de phyir de dag rkyend pu shes//drod sher sra dang g.yo' pa'i//chos rnams byis pas 
74a4(1): rnam brtags ste//'dus pa 'di ni chos ma yin//de phyir ngo bo nyid myed do//nad pa'i dbang du sman pa dag//bya ba (ma)tha dag stond mod kyi//de la gtsug lag tha dad myed//nad kyis phye bas tha dad do//de bzhin ngas 
74a5(1): kyang sems can rgyud//nyon mongs nyes pa'i skyon chags pa///dpang bo dag gi stobs shes nas//byis pa dag la tshul bshad do//nyon mongs dpang po tha dad pas//pa'i bstand pa dbyer myi phyed//
74a6(1): yan lag brgyad ldan zhi ba'i//lam ni theg pa gcig yino///de nas yang byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po blo gros chen pos bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsold to//bcom ldan 'das myi rtag pa nyid de/
74a7(1): myi rtag pa nyid do zhes mu stegs byed thams cad kyis /rnam par brtags te khyod kyi kyang bstand pa brjod pa thams cad las /kye ma 'du byed myi rtag ste /skye zhing 'jig pa'i chos can no zhes gsungs na /de ci bcom ldan 'das
74a8(1): yang dag pa 'am log pa 'am /bcom ldan 'das myi rtag panyid la rnam pa du mchis /bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsald pa/blo gros chen po mu stegs byed thams cadakyis myi brtag pa nyid rnam pa brgyad du rnam par brtag+sa
74a9(1): te ngas ni ma yin no//rnam pa brgyad gang zhe na /de la blo gros chen po la la ni rab tu rtsom pa las rnam par ldog pa /myi rtag pa nyid ces zer te /blo gros chen po rab tu rtsom pa zhes bya ba ni skye ba yin te /myi skye ba ni myi rtag 
74a10(1): pa nyid do//kha gcig ni dbyibs rnam par ldog pa myi rtag pa nyid ces zer ro//kha gcig ni gzugs myi rtag pa zhe'o//kha gcig gi gzugs 'gyur ba'i bye brag gzhan ni myi rtag pa nyid yin te /chos thams cad
74b1(1): par chad myed par 'argyun tu rang gi rang gis 'jig pa dgral ba //'o ma la zhor 'gyur ba'i bye brag tha dad pa bzhin du dngos po thams cad la yang myi mthong ba dang zhig pa'i myi rtag pa nyid du 'jug ce'o//kha gcig ni dngos po myi rtag par
74b2(1): rtog go//kha gcig ni dngos po'i dngos po myed par myi rtag par rtog go//kha gcig ni myi rtag pa yang de'i nang du 'dus pa'i phyir chos thams cad skye ba myed pa myi rtag pa zhe'o//de la blo gros chen po dngos po'i dngos po myed phyi

74b4(1): myi rtag pa dang yod pa dang myed pa las myi 'byung ba dang /chos thams cad myi mthong ba dang /rdul phra mo'i bar tu brtags na//myi mthong ba 'di ni myi skye ba'i tshig bla dgas kyi skye ba ni ma yin no//blo gros chen po de ni myi skye ba

74b7(1): 'i 'bras bu'o//myi rtag pa myed pas/dngos po thams cad ngo bo nyid myed pa myi dmyigs/dbyug pa dang /rdo ba dang /tho' ba gang yang rung pas bcag par bya ba dang//gcog pa bzhin phan tshun bye brag myed par snang ba mthong

75a10(1): mo'i bar du rab tu 'byed cing yongs su rtog pa'o//'jig pa ni 'byung ba dang/'byung ba las gyurd pa'i dbyibs gzhan du mthong pa'i phyir/ring thung du myi dmyigs pa yin yin te/'byung ba rnams la

77a1(1): #//byang chub sems dpa' rnams ni gang gis yongs su mya ngan las myi 'da' bar ting nge 'dzind kyi sangs rgyas rnams ting nge 'dzind kyi bde ba la de las rnam par 'dzind to//gal te bzung du zind na de bzhin gshegs pa'i sa'i

99b1(1): bar shes pa dang //dbang dang ldan pa ra rab tu thob //thams cad gzugs su snang ba'i //sems ni nam zhig 'byung ba na //'di la sems myed gzugs myed kyi //nor sems thog ma myed ces pa//

99b7(1): la ni bag chags myed //rang gyi sems la mngon par chags pas da //sems ni shind tu 'byung bar 'gyur //phyi rold snang yod myed de //de'i phyir na sems tsam mo//sems ni kun gzhi rnam shes te

100a9(1): bshad do //yod dang myed las skye bar ni //khyad pa dang grangs can shes //lung du ma bstand thams cad kyang //de dag nyid kyis bshad pa'o //lung du bstand pa ni rnam bzhi ste //mgo' gcig dang ni dri ba dang //
100a10(1): rnam par dbye dang gzhag pa rnams //mu stegs smra ba zlog pa'o //kun rdzob tu ni thams cad yod //dam pa'i don tu yod ma yin //chos rnams rang bzhin myed pa la //dam pa'i don tu 'dod pa ni //dmyigs pa 
100b1(1): rang bzhin myed pa la //de phyi kun rdzob bstand pa yin //dngos po brjod pa'i rgyus 'byung ba //gal te rang bzhin yod na ni //brjod pa dag las 'byung pa'i //dngos po myed cing bya ba myed //brjod pa 'di ni dngos myed de //
100b2(1): kun brdzob tu yang yod ma yin //phyind log ni gzhi yin pas //dmyigs pa yod pa ma yin no //gal te phyind ci log yod na //ngo bo nyid myed myed par 'gyur //phyind ci log ni gzhi'i phyir //gang dang gang dag dmyigs
100b3(1): 'gyur ba //de ni rang bzhin myed par 'gyur //thams cad du yang myed pa yin//gang 'di sna tshogs snang pa'i //gnas ngan len kyis bsgos pa'i sems //gzugs su snang ba 'dzind pa ste //phyi rold dag ni sems
100b4(1): 'khruld pa //rtog pa dang ni myi rtog pas //rnam rtog rab tu spong 'gyur te //myi rtog rnam par rtog pa' yis //song pa nyid na yang dag mthong //sgyu ma'i glang po sna tshogs ltar //lo ma rnams dang gser bzhin du //
100b5(1): myi shes pas ni sems bsgoms pas //de bzhin myi la snang ba mngon 'phags pas nor pa myi mthong ste //yang dag nyid kyang skabs der yang gang phyir skabs der yang dag na //nor pa yang dag nyid du 'gyur //
100b6(1): nor pa thams cad spangs nas kyang //gal te mtshan ma skyer zind na //de nyid de'i nor pa ste //rab rib yongs su ma dag bzhin //rab rib can rnams bslad pa yis //skra shad mthong ba ci bzhin du //de bzhin
100b7(1): byis pa rnams kyi yang //'dzin pa yul la shind tu 'jug //srid gsum 'di ni skra shad 'dra //smyig rgyu'i chu ltar nor pa ste //rmi lam sgyu ma lta bu'i //rnam par bshig na grol bar 'gyur //rnam par rtog dang rnam brtag
100b8(1): dang //rnam par rtog pa 'jug pa dang //'ching dang 'ching bya bcings pa dang //drug po 'di dag thard pa'i rgyu //sa rnams myed cing bden pa myed //zhing rnams myed cing spruld pa myed //sangs rgyas rnams dang rang sangs
100b9(1): rgyas //nyan thos rnams kyang brtags pa'o//gang zag rgyud dang phung po rnams //de bzhin rkyend dang rdul phran dang //gtso bo dbang phyug byed pa rnams //sems tsam la ni rnam par rtog //sems ni thams cad thams
100b10(1): cad du //rnam pa drug gis sna tshogs 'dzind //sems kyi rigs ni mtshan nyid myed //phung po rnams la bdag myed de //bdag kyang phung po rnams l+che //de dag ci ltar brtags bzhin myed //de dag myed pa 
101a1(1): //'ang ma yin no//ci ltar byis pas brtags pa bzhin //dngos po thams cad yod pa ste //de dag mthong bzhin yod na ni //thams cad yang dag mthong bar 'gyur //dngos po thams cad myed pa
101a2(1): sna'i phyir //kun nas nyon mongs dag pa myed //ci ltar mthong bzhin de myed de //de dag myed pa 'ang ma yin no//kun rtog nor pa'i mtshan ma ste //gzhan gyi dpang gi mtshan nyid do//mtshan nyid de la mying btags pa //de ni

101a5(1): nas 'byung ba'o //gang yang sruld pa'as bshad pa dang //rnam smind skyes pas gang bshad pa//mdo' sde shin du rgyas pa'i tshul //do dgongs pa shes par gyis //gang yang rgyal ba'i bus bshad dang //
101a6(1): gang yang 'dren pa rnams kyis bshad //sruld pa dag gis gang bshad pa //rnam smind rgyal bas ma yin no //chos 'di rnams ni ma skyes pa //de dag myed pa 'ang ma yin no//dri za'i grong khyer sgyu ma dang

101b5(1): mu stegs lta can dag ni //stand las ring du gyurd par shes //dngos po kun kyis smra ba la //rtag tu tshe rabs gzhan song tshe //phan tshun rnam par rtog pa yis //goms shing goms par byed pa'o //
101b6(1): mying ni brjod par ma byas na //'jig rten thams cad rmongs par 'gyur //de bas rmongs pa gsal pa'i phyir //mying du gdag(sa) pa bya ba yin //mying dang nor pa'i rnam rtog dang //rkyend rnams

102a1(1): //skyes pa'i rang bzhin la //rtog pa gang gis rtog byed pa //phyi rold don mthong log pa ste //don myed sems ni 'ba' shig go//rigs pas rnam par bzhigs na ni //bzung dang ni
102a2(1): 'dzind pa bkag //ci ltar byis bas brtags pa ltar //phyi rold kyis don myed do //phag chags kyis ni dkrugs pa'i sems//don tu snang ba shind tu 'byung //rtog pa'i rnam pa gnyis 'gags na //
102a3(1): yang dag ye shes spyod yul te //'phags pa'i spyod yul bsam myi khyab //snang ba myed par 'byung bar 'gyur //mying dang mtshan ma kun rtog ni //rang bzhin gnyis kyi mtshan nyid do//yang dag ye shes de bzhin nyid //

102a5(1): 'byung ba chen po'i yis //shing rtog bzhin du sbyind par 'gyur //lnga dang lnga dang lnga dang lnga nyid dang //rma yang dgu po nyid rnams dang //so dang sen mo sdu g.yogs nas //'gul zhing rab tu skye bar 'gyur //myi gtsang srin bu skyes ma thag //

102a9(1): lho phyogs be da'i yul du ni //dge slong dpal ldan cher grags pa //de'i mying klu zhes bor ba ste //yod dang myed pa' phyogs 'jig pa //nga'i theg pa 'jig rten du //bla myed theg chen rab bshad nas //rab tu dga'

104a1(1): /gcig tu//gzugs brnyan chu bo'i chu 'dra ba //sems kyi sa bon dag las 'byung//gang tshe na sems dang yid//rnam par shes pa myi 'jug pa//de'i phyir na yid lus dang//
104a2(1): sangs rgyas sa dang thob bar 'gyur//rkyend dang khams dang phung po dang//chos rnams kyi mtshan nyid//btags dang sems dang gang zag rnams//rmi lam skra shad 'dzings pa 'dra//'jig rten rmi lam sgyu ma ltar//
104a3(1): mthong nas yang dag gnas par gyis//de bzhin nyid ni mtshan nyid bral//rig pa dang ni gtan tshigs spangs//'phags pa'i so so rang rig pa//spyod pa rtag tu dran par gyis//rigs pa dang ni rgyu rmongs pa'i//
104a4(1): 'jig rten de bzhin nyid la 'khor//spros pa thams cad zhi ba'i phyir//shes rab nor pa myi 'jug ste//ci srid nor par rnam rtog pa//de srid rmongs pas sems 'jug go//rang bzhin myed dang dngos po dang//stong pa 
104a5(1): rtag dang myi rtag rnams//skye bar smra ba lta ba ste//myi skye smra ba ma yin no//'gro gcig tha dad gnyis kar yang//dbang phyug dang ni 'dod rgyal dang//dus dang gtso dang rdul phran dang//rkyend rnams kyis ni rtag pa yin//
104a6(1): 'khor ba'i sa bon rnam par shes//sa bon yod na 'byung bar 'gyur cig pa yod na ri mo bzhin//yongs su shes nas 'gag par 'gyur//ci ltar sgyu ma'i skyes bu bzhin//myi dag skye dang 'chi bar 'gyur
104a7(1): de bzhin byis pa rmongs pa'i phyir//bcings dang tha dad par shind tu 'byung//phyi dang nang ni rnam gnyis te//chos rnams dang ni rkyen rnams so//rnal 'byord de la rnam bsgom zhing//snang ba myed la 
104a8(1): gnas par bya//bag chags dag la sems myi dbye'//sems dang bag chags gcig ma yin//sems ni tha dad ma yin mtshan//bag chags rnams kyis yongs su dkris//yid kyi shes las 'byung ba'i//
104a9(1): bag chags dri ma bzhin du ltos//sems ni ras yug dkar po 'dra//bag chags rnams kyis myi mdzes so//ci ltar ngas ni nam mkha' la//dngos myed dngos myed ma yin bshad//de bzhin lus la kun gzhi ni//
104a10(1): dngos dang dngos po myed pa spangs//rnyog pa spangs pa'i sems nyid ni//yid kyi rnam shes bzlog pa ste//chos rnams thams cad shes pa'i phyir//sems ni sangs rgyas zhes da 'chang//rgyud gsum rnam 
104b1(1): par 'chad pa ste //yod pa dang ni myed pa spangs //mtha' bzhi dag las rnam par grol //srid pa rtag tu sgyu ma bzhin //sems las byung ba'i sa bdun rnams //ngo bo nyid ni gnyis su 'gyur //sa gzhan dang ni sangs 
104b2(1): rgyas sa //de dag shind tu rdzogs pa yin //gzugs can dang ni gzugs myed khams //'dod pa'i khams dang mya ngan 'das //sems kyi spyod yul thams cad kyang //lus 'di nyid du bshad pa yin //gang tshe ci srid 
104b3(1): dmyigs gyurd pa //de srid nor pa 'jug par 'gyur //nor ba rang gi sems rtog na //'jug pa myed cing ldog pa myed //skye ba myed la rgyu yang myed //dngos pos 'khor ba kun 'dzind te //sgyu las gsogs dang 'drar mthong na 
104b4(1): mtshan nyid la ni myi rtog go //byis pa gzhan pa'i blo can la ////theg pa gcig dang theg gsum dang //theg pa myed par nga 'chad do////'phags pa rnams la dben pa nyid //mtshan nyid dang ni rtogs pa de //
104b5(1): nga'i skye ba rnam gnyis so /tshul bstand pa ni rnam bzhi ste //rigs pa sand dang grub pa'i mtha' //dbyibs dang byad dang khyad par gyis //nor pa mthong nas rnam par rtog //mying dang dbyibs dang bral gyurd 
104b6(1): nas //rang bzhin 'phags pa'i spyod yul gtsang //ci srid rnam par rtog pas rtog //de srid brtags pa'i mtshan nyid de //brtag bya rtog pa myed pa'i phyir //rang bzhin 'phags pa spyod yul lo //rtag pa ther 
104b7(1): zug yang dag nyid //rigs dang dngos po'i ngo bo nyid //de bzhin nyid ni sems las grol //rtog pa rnams kyang spangs pa'o //dngos po myed na dag myin 'gyur //nyon mongs pa 'ang su la myed //gang phyir sems ni dag 'gyur 
104b8(1): bas //kun nas nyon mongs snang bar 'gyur //de phyir rnam dag 'phags pa'i yul //yang dag nyid ni dngos por 'gyur //sems kyis rkyend kyis bskyed pa ste //rtog pa dag ni rnam par spel //
104b9(1): rmi lam sgyu ma la gsogs ltar //mthong na rnam par 'char rgyu 'gyur //bag chags sna tshogs gnas ngan len //sems dang lhan gcig rab tu ldan //rmi la phyi rold rnam snang ste //sems kyi chos nyid ma yin no //
104b10(1): sems kyi chos nyid dag pa ste //sems ni nor pas 'byung ba yin //nor pa gnas ngan len pa ste //de'i phyir na sems myi mthong //de bzhin nyid ni nor pa tsam //de bzhin nyid ni gud na myed //'du byed la myin gyur na 'dra 
105a1(1): //myen//'on kyang 'du byed mthong bas so//nam zhig 'dus byas rnams la ni//mtshan dang mtshan gzhi bral mthong na//'gro ba rang gi sems mthong bas//de'i phyir na spangs par 'gyur//sems tsam la ni
105a2(1): gnas nas ni//phyi rol don la myi brtags pa'o//yang dag dmyigs la gnas nas ni//sems tsam la ni 'da' bar bya//sems tsam las ni 'das nas ni//snang ba myed la 'da' bar bya//rnal 'byord snang ba myed la gnas na//
105a3(1): theg pa chen po myi mthong ngo//lhun kyis grub rtogs zhi ba ste//smon lam dag gis rnam par sbyangs//bdag myed ye shes mchog yin te//snang ba myed tshe myi mthong ngo//sems kyi spyod yul lta bar bya//ye shes kyi yang spyod yul
105a4(1): lta//shes rab spyod yul mthong na ni//mtshan nyid la ni rmongs myi 'gyur sdug bsngal bden pa sems kyis te//kun 'byung ye shes spyod yul lo//bden pa gnyis dang sangs rgyas sa//de la shes rab shind
105a5(1): tu 'jug//'bras bu thob dang mya ngan 'das//de bzhin yan lag brgyad pa'i lam//chos rnams thams cad rtogs pa'i phyir//sangs rgyas ye shes rnam dag gyurd//myig dang gzugs dang snang ba dang//
105a6(1): yid dang nam mkha' de bzhin te//de dag gis ni myi rnams kyis//kun gzhi las 'byung pa rnam shes te//bzung dang 'dzind dang 'dzind byed pa dang//mying yang dngos myin myed pa dang//rnam par rtog
105a7(1): pa rgyu myed dang//gang sems de dag mkhas ma yin//don la mying ni ma byung ste//mying la don kyang de bzhin no//mtshan nyid myed las byung ba'i//rnam par rtog pa ma rtog shig//dngos po thams cad rang bzhin myed//
105a8(1): tshig kyang de bzhin myed pa yin//stong pa dang ni stong pa'i don//byis pa ma mthong rnam par rgyug//rlom sems bden dgod mthong bas na//brtags pa'i ni bshad pa'o//gcig po rnam pa lnga grub pas//bden pa po 'di
105a9(1): spongs par 'gyur//spros pa bdud kyis bzung ba yin//yod dang myed las 'da' bar bya//log pa'i 'du shes bdag myed par//mthong bas srid las 'dun myi bya//rtag pa byed dang bcas pa'i//rtsod pa tsam zhig 'jug
105a10(1): pa ste//bden pa'i mchog 'di smrang byed pa//'gags na chos ni mthong ba yin//kun gzhi la ni rten bcas nas//yid kyang shind tu 'jug par 'gyur//sems dang yid la brten bcas nas//rnam par shes pa
105b1(1): 'jug par 'gyur //sgro btags pa dang sgro 'dogs dang //sems kyi chos nyid de bzhin nyid //rnal 'byord pas ni de bsgoms na //sems can shes pa thob par 'gyur //yid dang mtshan nyid dngos po la //rtag dang myi rtag rloms
105b2(1): sems myed //skye dang myi skye rnal 'byord la//rnal 'byord pa ni rloms sems myed //kun gzhi las 'byung rnam shes ni //don gnyis la ni rtog par byed //de las 'byung ba'i don gcig la //sems gnyis kyis ni shes myi 'gyur
105b3(1): smra ba myed cing tshig myed de //sems stong phyir ni stong ma yin //rang gi sems ni ma mthong phyir //lta ba'i dra ba 'jug par 'gyur //rkyend rnams 'ong ba myed pa ste //dbang po dag ni yang dag myed //khams rnams myed cing phung po myed //
105b4(1): 'dod chags myed cing 'dus byas myed //las dang mye myed sngon yang myed //byas ba myed cing 'dus byas myed//mtha' yang myed cing chags pa myed //thard pa myed cing bcings pa myed //lung ma bstand pa la dngos po myed //chos dang chos ma
105b5(1): yin ba myed //dus myed myed ngan 'das pa myed //chos nyid dag kyang myed pa yin sangs rgyas myed cing bden rnams myed //'bras bu myed cing rgyu rnams myed //log dang mya ngan 'das pa myed /'byung ba dang ni 'jig pa myed //
105b6(1): yan lag bcu gnyis yod ma yin //mtha' dang mtha' myed pa yang myed //lta ba thams cad sangs pa'i phyir //sems tsam du ni ngas bshad do //lus dang nyon mongs las lam dang //byed pa po dang 'bras bu rnams //
105b7(1): rmi lam dang ni smyigs rgyu 'dra //dri za'i ni grong khyer bzhin //sems tsam du ni rnam bzhag pas //dngos po'i mtshan las rnam par ldog//sems tsam la ni gnas pa'i phyir //rtag dang chad pa mthong bar 'gyur
105b8(1): mya ngan 'das pa phung po myed //bdag kyang myed cing mtshan nyid myed //sems tsam la ni 'jug pa yis //thard par 'dzind las ldog par 'gyur //sa snang rgyus 'byung nyes pa ni //mying rnams la ni phyi rold snang //sems ni
105b9(1): snang ba myed las 'byung //de'i phyir ni sems myi snang //myi rnams kyi ni phag chags rnams //lus dang longs spyod gnas ltar snang //sems ni dngos myin dngos myed myin //bag chags dag gis myi mdzes so//
105b10(1): dkar po la na dri ma snang //dri ma la ni dkar myi snang //nam mkha' la ni sprin bzhin du //de bzhin sems kyang myi snang ngo //sems ni las kyang satshogs par byed //ye shes kyis ni rnam par seld //shes rab kyis
106a1(1): //ni snang myed dang //mthu' 'ang rab tu thob par byed //sems ni yul ni dang 'breld pa ste //shes pa rtog ge dag la 'jug //snang ba myed dang khyad par la //shes rab dag ni shind tu 'jug //sems dang yid
106a2(1): dang rnam shes dang //'du shes stong pa spangs pa ste //rnam par myi rtog chos thob pas //nyan thos dag ni rgyal sras myin //zhi ba bzod pa'i khyad par la //de bzhin gshegs kyi ye shes dge //kun tu spyod pa spangs pa'i ni //
106a3(1): khyad par phyir ni skye bar 'gyur //kun tu btags pa'i rang bzhin yod //gzhan gyi dbang ni yod pa ste //nor ba dag gis brtags pa 'dzind//gzhan gyi dbang ni myi brtag go//thams cad 'byung 'gyur gzugs myed de //
106a4(1): 'byung las ma gyurd gzugs yod de //dri za'i grong khyer rmi lam dang //sgyu ma smig rgyu 'byung 'gyur myin //nga'i shes rab rnam gsum ste //gang gis 'phags pa rab phye dang //gang gis yang dag mtshan nyid rtog//
106a5(1): gang gis dngos po 'byed pa'o //nyan thos shes rab rnam spangs pa //theg pa gnyis dang bral ba ni //dngos por yod par chags pas na //shind tu 'jug par 'gyur ba yin //sems tsam la ni 'jug pa
106a6(1): 'is //bde gshegs shes rab dri ma myed //yod pa dang ni myed pa las rkyend kyis gal te skyer zin na //gcig dang tha dad lta ba la //gdon myi za bar de dag gnas //yan lag mang pos bsgrubs pa'i //
106a7(1): sgyu ma grub par myi 'gyur //de bzhin mtshan ma sna tshogs kyang //btags na grub par myi 'gyur ro //mtshan ma gnas ngan len pa'i //'ching ba' sems las byung ba ste //kun tu brtags pa myi shes pas //gzhan gyi
106a8(1): dbang zhes rtogs par 'gyur //gang brtags pa'i dngos po 'di //gzhan gyi dbang yang de nyid de //brtags pa sna tshogs snang ba ni //gzhan gyi dbang zhes rtog par byed //kun rdzob dang ni dam pa don //rgyu las
106a9(1): skyes pa gsum du myed //brtags pa kun rdzob ces bya ste //de chad 'phags pa'i spyod yul lo //rnal 'byord can gyi dngos po ltar //sna tshogs gcig tu rnam par mdzes //de la rnam pa sna tshogs myed //
106a10(1): brtags pa'i mtshan kyang de bzhin no //ci ltar rab rib can dag gis //gzugs mthong ba la sna tshogs rtog //rab rib gzugs myin //gzugs myed myin //mkhas pas gzhan dbang de bzhin no //cig ltar
106a11(1): gser ni dag pa dang //chu yang rnyog pa spangs pa dang //nam mkha la ni sprin myed ltar //rnam rtog dag pa de bzhin no //nga'i nyan thos rnam gsum ste //srul dang smon lam las skyes pa //'dod chags zhe sdang
106b1(1): spangs pa'i //chos byung ba'i nyan thos so//byang chub sems dpa' 'di rnam gsum ste //sems rgyas la ni mtshan nyid myed //sems can dag gis sems sems la //sangs rgyas gzugs ni rab tu snang //brtags pa'i 
106b2(1): ni dngos po myed //gzhan gyi dbang ni yod pa ste //sgro 'dogs pa dang skurd pa ni //rnam par brtags na 'jig par 'gyur //gal te brtags pa'i dngos myed la //gzhan gyi dbang gi dngos yod na //dngos po yod par 
106b3(1): dngos por 'gyur //dngos po dngos myed las 'byung 'gyur //kun tu brtags pa gnas bcas te //gzhan gyi dbang ni dmyigs par 'gyur //mying dang mtshan ma 'breld pa las //kun tu brtags pa'i 'byung bar 'gyur //
106b4(1): brtags pa shind tu ma grub ste //gzhan las 'byung ba ma yin no //dam pa'i chos kyi ngo bo nyid //dag pa de tshe shes par 'gyur //kun tu rtog pa rnam pa bcu //gzhan gyi dbang ni rnam pa drug //so so rang rig de bzhin nyid 
106b5(1): de'i phyir ni khyad myed do //chos lnga de bzhin nyid yin te //rang bzhin gsum yang de bzhin no //rnal 'byord can gyis de bsgoms na de bzhin nyid las myi 'da'o //skar ma dang ni srin lta bur //nyi ma 
106b6(1): zla ba 'dra ba dang //bag chags las 'byung snang 'dra bar //myi rnams kyis ni sems snang ngo //'byung ba 'di dag lus ma red //mtshan nyid myed cing mtshan gzhi myed //ci ste 'byung ba'i 'byung yin la //
106b7(1): gzugs ni 'byung ba las gyurd yin //'byung ba chen po yang dag myin//'byung la 'byung las gyurd pa myed //'byung ba chen po rgyu yin te //'bras bu sa dang chu la gsogs //rdzas dang brtags dang gzugs 
106b8(1): rnams dang //sgyu ma'i rnam byas de bzhin te //rmi lam dri za'i grong gi gzugs //smyig rgyu ba dang lnga yin no //'dod pa can gyi rnam pa lnga //rigs kyang rnam pa lnga yin te //theg pa lnga dang theg pa myed //mya ngan 'das
106b9(1): pa rnam pa drug yin //phung po tha dad nyi shu bzhi //gzugs kyang rnam pa brgyad du 'gyur //sangs rgyas nyi shu rtsa bzhi yin //rgyal ba'i sras ni rnam pa gnyis //tshul yang brgya po rtsa brgyad de //de bzhin nyan 
106b10(1): thos rnam gsum mo //sangs rgyas rnams kyi zhing gcig ste //de bzhin sangs rgyas gcig tu 'gyur //rnam par grol ba de bzhin gsum //sems kyi rgyud ni rnam pa bzhi //nga'i bdag myed rnam pa drug 
107a1(1): //shes par bya ba 'ang rnam pa bzhi//rgyu rnams dang ni 'bras 'gyur ba //lta ba'i nyes pa' rnam par spangs //so so rang rig myi g.yo' ba //theg pa chen po bla na myed //skye ba dang ni myi skye ba //rnam pa brgyad dang //
107a2(1): rnam dgur 'gyur //gcig dang rim gyis rtogs pa ste //grub pa'i mtha' yang gcig nyid do//gzugs myed khams ni rnam pa brgyad //bsam gtan tha dad rnam pa drug //rang rgyal sras po rnams kyi ni //'byung ba rnam pa 
107a3(1): bdun du 'gyur //dus gsum po yang yod ma yin //rtag pa dang ni myi rtag myed //byed dang las dang 'bras bu rnams //rmi lam gyi ni bya ba bzhin //sangs rgyas nyan thos rgyal ba'i sras //thog ma tha mar 'byung ba myed //
107a4(1): sems kyi snang dang bral ba ste //rtag tu sgyu ma'i chos dang 'dra //dum dang 'khor lo skyed de bzhin //'byung dang dga' ldan gyi gnas dang //zhing kun tu ni gshegs par yang //snang yang mngal na skyes pa myin //
107a5(1): 'pho dang rgyu dang sems can dang //de bzhin stond dang mya ngan 'das bden pa dang ni zhing rtog pa ///rkyend rnams kyis ni 'phangs pa yin //'jig rten dang ni nags 'tshal gling //mu stegs 'pho dang 
107a6(1): bdag myed dang //bsam gtan theg gnas thob pa dang //'bras bu'i spyod yul bsam myi khyab //zla ba skar ma'i rigs rnams dang //rgyal po'i ri dang lha'i gnas //gnod sbyin dri za'i rigs
107a7(1): rnams na //las las skyes shing sred las 'byung //bsam gyis myi khyab 'gyur ba'i //shi 'pho bag chags ldan pa ste //'chi 'phor snang ba chad na ni //nyon mongs dra ba 'gag par 'gyur //pa lang lug dang bran

107a9(1): pa dang //zangs kyi lhung gzed myi bcad do //snam bu sngon por btsos pa dang //ba lang lci ba hur smig dang //sa dang 'bras bu lo ma yis//rnal 'byord rtag tu dkar por 'asgyur //lugs dang do dang kham
107a10(1): pa dang //dung dang shel las byas pa'i //ma ga ta'i bre gang tshad ///lhung gzed rnal 'byord can gyis bcad //dgu po gos 'dra'i gri yang ni //sor bzhi pa'i tshad du bya //rnal 'byor da gzhol ba'i
107b1(1): rnal 'byord pas //bzo'i rig pa myi slab bo //rnal 'byord zhugs pa'i rnal 'byord pas //nyo dang tshong yang myi bya'o //kun dga'i khyim pas de byas te //de ni chos su ngas bshad de//dbang sdom zhing
107b2(1): don shes pa //mdo' dang 'dul la de bzhin/te//khyim pa dang ni ma 'dres par //rnal 'byord can la de ngas bshad //stong pa khyim 'am dur khrod dam //shing gi drung dang phug rnams dang //bla gab myed pa'i sog 
107b3(1): khrod du //rnal 'byord can gyis gnas pa ca'o//dur khrod du' 'am gzhan gang tu //rtag par gos gsum bgo' bar bya //gang yang bde bar byin pa dag //gos kyis phyir ni sbyin par bya //slong ma mo' zas
107b4(1): la gzhol byis ste //gnya shing gang tsam lta bar bya //me tog la ni bung ba ltar //de ltar bsod snyoms spyad par bya //mang po'i tshogs dang 'dres pa dang //dge slong ma dang gang 'dres pa //'dres par gyurd
107b5(1): ba'i 'tsho ba de //rnal 'byord can la rung ma yin //rnal 'byor nga g.yol ba'i rnal 'byord pas //rgyal po dang ni rgyal po'i bu //de bzhin blon po tshong dpon dang //zas kyi phyir ni bsen myi bya //shing dang bcas
107b6(1): pa'i khyim zas dang //dga' dang badza' dang ldan pa dang //dge slong dge slong ma dang 'dres//rnal 'byord can de myi rung //du ba gang thul gtsug lag khang //cho ga bzhin du rtag gtsed dang //
107b7(1): bdag gi phyir ni gang byas pa //rnal 'byord can la de myi rung //'jig rten mtshan gzhi mtshan ldan la //skye dang 'jig las grol ba dang //yod dang myed kyi phyogs spangs par //rnal 'byord
107b8(1): can gyis rnam par sgoms//gal te skye bar myi rtog na //ting nge 'dzind dang stobs rnams dang //mngon par shes dang dbang ldan gyis //rnal 'byord par ni myur du 'gyur //rdul phran dus dang gtso bo dang //
107b9(1): byed pa las kyang myi rtog go//'jig rten rgyu rkyend las 'byung bar //rnal 'byord pas ni myi brtag go //'jig rten rang gi rtog pas brtags //sna tshogs bag chags dag las byung //srid pa sgyu ma myi 'dra
107b10(1): bar //rnal 'byord can gyis rtag tu ltos //sgro 'dogs pa dang skur ba yis //spangs pa lta ba rtag tu ste//zhi ba'i lam ni dger song nas//rnal 'byord sna tshogs tshom bar byed //lus dang longs spyod
108a1(1): //dge ba'i las kyi 'bras bu yod yong zhing //gang gis bde bas bde bar gshegs pa bskyed //sngon gyi srid par spyad par spyod pa'i tshe //skye bo thams cad bde bas bde ldan byas /de'i legs pa'i lam de byas
108a2(1): bas na //bde' ba'i lam gyis mya ngan 'das reg 'gyur //ston ba'i gsung gis mdzes pa de dga' nas //de'i skad ci gas ster pus gtsugs te /gzhal myed dga' ba'i sems ni dbabs su //'jig rten sgron pa 
108a3(1): 'dren la phyag 'tshal byed //ston ba blo'is mkhas la phyag 'tshal lo //'gro ba kun las des 'byung phyag 'tshal //'jigs byed blo dag pa la phyag 'tshal lo //bde gshegs gzhan gyis gsung la phyag 'tshal
108a4(1): lo /de nas klu'i rgyal po lha mdzes kyis //bcom ldan 'das la tshigs bcad pa'i dbyang kyis zhus pa //'od mdzad spyan sdug legs pa'i spyan mnga' ba //blo dge nyon mongs tshogs las rnam grol zhing //
108a5(1): ye shes dri myed spyod yul khyod la ni //spyad pa spyod tshe rnam thara bla myed zhu //lha dang bcas pa'i 'jig rten sman slad du /ji ltar spyad na byang chub ci 'tshal zhing /myi mchog dam pa dag gis spyod sbyong ba 
108a6(1): yid 'das de dag bdag la bka' stsald gsol //rgyal ba de'i rdul bcom dbang po sdams //'dod pa'i 'ching las thugs ni rnam grol ba'i//dgongs pas de nyid sems kyi rjes 'jug pa 
108a7(1): thugs zhim bkrugs des ni lus stan pa /mkhas pas dge pa dag ni spyad pa'i phyir //srog gi phyir yang yid ni myi ltog cing /rtag tu spyod tshe nyon mongs myed kyis kyang /lus dang ngag gi dge ba mnyam spyod pa //
108a8(1): de yis legs pa'i las de byas pa nas /'khor pa'i 'gro ba spyod ba rnam dag ste //chu'i nang nas pad ma skyes pa ltar //gos pa myed pa'i byang chub yang dag grub //bde' gshegs gsung gis lha'i mdzes pa 
108a9(1): klu //de ni dga' zhing legs par dgu pas su //de yis skad cig sa la pus btsugs te //sangs rgyas rkang gnyis ma cig la phyag 'tshal byed ////thub pa 'jig rten kun la sman mdzad pa /blo gros zhis par 
108a10(1): zhi la phyag 'tshal lo //spyad pa spyod tshe khyod kyis rjes slob cing //byang chub myi g.yo' yid ni bskyed bar bgyid /sems can don du da spyad pa pa'i tshe //lus dang srog ni de bzhin gtang nas su //
108b1(1): sdug sngal mthar byed sangs rgyas 'gyur pa dang //byang chub zhi ba bla myed thob par bgyis //klu'i de'i bsam ba thugs su chud nas su //rgyal ba dgra las rgyal ba 'dzum mdzad de //de tshe ci ltar gang
108b2(1): 'ga'i byed snyed kyi //sangs rgyas mtha' yas zhing ni 'od kyis khyab //bde bar gshegs pas 'dzum pa stand pa la //nam mkha' lhas ris legs so zhes gsold te //e ma'o rgyal bas rdul btsa

112b7(1): brnyan dang //rab rib can la skra 'dzings pa //de bzhin bag chags kyis yog sems//byis ba rnams kyis mthong ba yin //bdag gi rtog pas brtags don la //rnam par rtog pa rab tu 'jug//
112b8(1): mu stegs can gyis brtags pa ltar //pha rold kyi(na) don myed de //ci ltar thag par ma shes nas //byis ba sbrul du 'dzind pa bzhin //rang gi sems don ma shes pas //phyi rold kyi ni don tu rtog //
112b9(1): de ltar thag pa thag nyid du //gcig dang tha dad spangs mod kyi//rang gis thag par (rnam)rtog par //de ni rang sems nyes pa yin //gang la gang gi dngos por yang //brtags na myi dmyigs ma yin pa //de ni myed
112b10(1): par shes myi bya //chos kyi chos nyid de yino //yod pa'i zlas drangs myed pa ste //myed pa'i zlas drangs yod pa yin//de phyir myed par shes myi bya //yod pa nyid 'ang myi rtog go //gang 'di kun tu brtags
112b11(1): pa la //brtags na de'i bdag nyid myed //de'i bdag nyid myed mthong zhing //ci ltar rnam par rtog pa 'jug //gzugs ni gzugs kyi bdag nyid myed //de bzhin bum pa gsar la gsogs //snang ba yod pa ma yin bas 
113a1(1): //khyod kyi rnam rtog myi 'byung ngo//thog ma myed ldan 'dus byas la//gal te khyod kyis rtog nor dang //dngos po'i dngos nyid su zhig gis//'khor byas thub cen bdag la gsungs//dngos po'i dngos nyid myed ste
113a2(1): sems tsam po ni snang ba yin//rang gi sems ni ma mthong pas//rnam par rtog pa rab tu 'jug/ci ltar byis pas brtags pa'i gal te yod med cing//de las rnam pa gzhan yod na//blos kyang rtogs par myi rung ngo//'phags pa gal te
113a3(1): de yod na//de ni byis pas brtags ma yin//gal te 'phags pa de log na 'phags pa byis pa mnyam par gyurd//gang phyir sems ni rnam sbyangs pa//'phags pa rnams la nor pa myed//sems kyi rgyud ni dag pa'i//byis pa brtags pa
113a4(1): la rtog par byed//ci ltar bu la ma zhig ni//kha la shing thog len byed cing//de re bu gcig ma rtsa bar//shing thog sna tshogs 'di yang long//de bzhin sems can kun la ngas//brtags pa'i 'bras bu sna tshogs kyis//brid nas yod dang myed pa'i//phyogs
113a5(1): spangs tshul ni rab tu bshad//gang gis ma gyur rkyend rnams kyis//'byung se 'breld las ma yin na//sngon ma skyes par da skyes te//bdag nyid du yang red pa myed//bdag nyid ma red skyes pa myed//rkyend myed par yang gang na 'ang myed//skyes
113a6(1): pa'i dngos po de dag kyang//rkyend myed par yang gar na 'ang myed//de lta bu'i mdor mthong na//gang na myed myin yod myed myin//dngos po rkyend las skyes par na mkhas pa rnams kyis myi brtag go//mu segs can rnams byis pa la//rkyend kyis
113a7(1): gcag gam tha dad dam//zer yang 'gro ba rmi lam dang //sgyu ma 'gro bar myi sheso brjod pa'i lus gyis theg pa ni/theg pa chen po bla na myed//ngas ni don kyang legs par bstand//byis pa rnams kyis myi shes so//gang dag 'jungs pa'i nyan
113a8(1): thos dang//mu stegs can gyis byas pa yin//gang phyir rto ges bshad pa na//de dag don la 'khrul pa'o//mtshan nyid dang ni dngos dbyibs dang//mying ni rnam pa bzhi po yang//de dag la ni dmyigs byas shing//rnam par rtog pa rab tu 'jug/gang rnams
113a9(1): gcig dang du ma dang//tshangs pa'i ris la dbang sgyur zhing//nyi ma zla ba'i 'od dag kyang//gang 'jig byed pa sras ma yin//'phags pa'i mthong ba phun gsum tshogs//yang dag ci bzhin rtogs byed cing//'du shes bzlog la mkhas pa rnams//rnam
113a10(1): par shes pa'i mchog phyind pa//de ni nga'i bstand pa la//sras po grol ba'i phyag rgya ste//dngos dang dngos myed rnam grol zhing//'on kyang 'gro ba rnam spangs pa'o//gzugs kyis rnam shes phyir log nas//gal te las ni 'jig 'gyur na//rtag dang
113a11(1): myi rtag par myi 'gyur//'khor ba dag kyang myed 'gyur ro//ldog pa'i tshe ni zhig 'gyur pa//gzugs kyi yul las phyir ldog ste//yod myed nyes dang bral ba'i las//kun gzhi la ni rab tu gnas//srid pa'i gnas su rnams shes gang//gzugs
113b1(1): lcang rab tu 'jig pa ste//gzugs dang rnam shes 'breld pa'i /las ni 'jig par myi 'gyur ro//gal te de dag 'breld pa'i //myi'i las ni 'jig 'gyur na //las kyi 'breld pa'i zhig pa'i phyir //'khor myed mya ngan 'da' ba 'byed //
113b2(1): zhig kyang de dag lhan gcig tu //gal te 'khor bar skyes bgyur na //gzugs ni de dang 'breld pa'i phyir //tha myi dad par 'byung bar 'gyur //sems ni gzugasu rnam rtog las //gcig pa ma yin tha dad myin //yod dang myed pa'i phyogs 
113b3(1): spangs shing //dngos po rnams la 'jig pa myed //kun tu brtags dang gzhan gyi dbang //phan tshun mtshan nyid tha myi dad //gzugs kyi myi rtag nyid bzhin te //gcig la gcig kyang skyed byed pa'o//gcig dang tha dad
113b4(1): rnam grol ba'i //brtags pa khong du myi chud na //yod dang myed par ci ltar 'gyur //gzugs kyi myi rtag nyidabazhin no//brtags pa shind tu mthong gyurd na //gzhan gi dbang ni myi skye ba'o//gzhan gi dbang ni rab mthong na
113b5(1): brtags pa de bzhin nyid du 'gyur //rtog pa rnam par 'jig pa ni //de'i tshul yang rnam 'jig byed //nga'i bstand pa 'di la ni //sgro 'dogs skurd pa 'debs pa yin //gang na gang tshe de 'dra ba //chos sun
113b6(1): 'byind pa yod pa rnams //nga'i tshul ni 'jig par byed //de dag kun dang 'gro myi bya //gang zhig sgro 'dogs skurd pa 'debs//brtags pa rnam par 'jig byed dang //mkhas pa dag tu gtam myi bya //
113b7(1): dge slong don la spang bar bya //skra shad 'dzings dang //sgyu 'dra dang //rmyi lam 'dra zhing dri za 'dra //rtog pa smig rgyu snang 'dra la//gang dag yod dang myed lta ba //de dag bsdud par su spyod pa //
113b8(1): sangs rgyas kyi la de myi slob //mtha' gnyis ltung ba ste //gzhan yang rnam par 'jig par byed //brtags pa'i dngos po rab dben zhing //dngos dang dngos myed rnam grol bar //rnal 'byord can
113b9(1): ni su mthong ba //de dag bsdu ba spyad par gyis //mu tig nor bu gser 'phyud pa'i //'byung khung 'jig rten na yod pa //sna tshogs las kyi rgyus ma byung //byis pa rnams kyis tshor ba ltar //
113b10(1): de bzhin sems can rigs rnams ni //rnam pa sna tshogs las kyis spangs //snang ba myed phyir las myed //las las byung pa'i 'gro ba 'ang myed //ci ltar 'phags pas rnam bsgom ltar //dngos po
113b11(1): 'i dngos nyid myed mod kyis //ci ltar byis pas brtags pa ltar //dngos po dag ni yod pa'o //ci ltar byis pas brtag pa ltar //gal te dngos po myed na ni //dngos pa thams cad myed pas //
114a1(1): //su la yang nyon mongs myed par 'gyur//dngos po sna tshogs nyon mongs las//'khor pa dpang bo nyer 'gro ba//myi shes pa dang rtend 'brel na//lus can rnaMs kyi 'byung bar 'gyur/ci ltar byis pa rtags pa ltar/
114a2(1): gang gi dngos po yod myin ba/de dag dbang po 'jug pa myed/rnal 'byord pa nyid kyang myed pa yin/bcom ldan gal te 'khor ba'i/rgyu'i dngos po ma mchis na/byis pa nan tan ma bgyis par/byed pa spangs ba'i thar par gyur
114a3(1): khyod kyi 'phags pa byis pa'i khyad/dngos po myed na ci ltar mchis/rnam par thard pa gsuM spyod pa'i/'phags pa dag la dngos po myed/phung po gang zag chos rnams dang/rang dang spyi'i mtshan nyid myed/dngos po rnams dang rkyen kyi rnaMs
114a4(1): nyan thos dag pas ngas bshado/rgyu myed pa dang sems tsam dang/'byord pa rnaMs dang sa rnams dang/so so rang gi de bzhin nyid/dag pa rgyal ba'i sras la bshad/nga'i stan la sun 'byin ba/dur smyig gi ni gos bgos shing/
114a5(1): 'bras bu yod dang myed smra ba/ma 'ongs pa'i dus na 'byung/dngos po myed/de rkyend rnams kyis/yod pa 'phags pa'i spyod yul lo//rtags pa'i dngos po shind tu myed/rtog ge ba rnams rtog par byed/
114a6(1): ma 'ongs pa'i dus na ni/byis pa'i rang bzhin gzegs zan 'byung/'bras bu myed smra'i lta ngan bas/skye po mang po brnyag par byed rdul phran las mi 'gro ba 'byung/rdul phran rnaMs ni rgyu myed de/rdzas dgu 
114a7(1): rnams kyang rtag pa'o zhes/lta ba ngan pas stond par 'gyur/rdzas rnaMs kyis ni rdzas bsaMs te/yon tan gis kyang yon tan no/dngos po dag gi dngos nyid gzhan/yod pa dag ni 'jig par byed/gal te ma byung 'byung 'gyur na/
114a8(1): 'jig rten thog ma yod par 'gyur/'khor pa sngon gi tha ma ni/ngas ni myed par bshad pa yin/srid gsuM tsaMs cad 'grang pa dang/de ste ma byung 'byung na ni/khyi dang rnga mo bong bu'i/rwa skyer 'gyur ba the tsom myed/gal te gzugs
114a9(1): dang rnaM shes dang/myig ni ma skyes skye 'gyur na/cod pan de lde ras lasogs/'dri ba bsgongs las 'byung par 'gyur/ngas dang de lde yod pa myin/rtsis kyang la yang ngas myed do/gcig pa las ni cig byung na/rkyen rnaMs kyis kyang
114a10(1): cir myi 'byung/'tsho ba de dang lus de dang/gang yang ma byung skye ba ni/de dag thaMs cad gzhan smra ba/ngas kyang de dag shin tu bshad/snga ma'i phyogs ni brjod bya ste/de dag blo ni zlog par bya/de dag blo ni zlog byas na/bdag gi phyogs nas
114a11(1): ngas bshad do/nga'i slob ma rmongs pa'i sde/yod myed phyogs la gnas 'od zhes/de phyir mu stegs smra ba dag/ngas ni brjod par byas ma yin/yan lag ser skya blo la 'ang/'gro ba gtso las skyes pa dang/
114b1(1): yon tan rnam par 'gyur ba yang /slob ma rnams la rab tu ston /rkyend kyis 'byung dang ma byung myin /rkyen rnaMs yod pa ma yin te/rkyend rnaMs dngos po myed pa'i phyir/ma byung ba ni myi 'byung ngo/yod dang myed pa'i phyogs bral zhing
114b2(1): rgyu dang rkyen rnaMs spangs pa dang/skye dang 'jig pa myed pas ni/rang gi phyogs de mtshan bzhi spangs/sgyu ma rmi laM 'dra pa'i/'jig rten rgyu dang rkyen spangs pa/rtag tu myed par mthong na/rnam par rtog par myi 'gyur ro/dri za'i grong 
114b3(1): khyer smyig sgyu 'dra/rtag par skra bshad 'dzings pa bzhin/yod dang myed pa'i phyog bral zhing/rgyu dang rkyen kyis sangs pa'i /srid pa rgyu myed par mthong na' sems kyi rgyud ni rnam par dag /gang gi dngos po myed pa dang /
114b4(1): sems tsam po yang yod gyurd na/dngos po myed par ci ltar sems/sems tsam du yang myi rung ngo/dngos po la ni dmyigs byas shing/myi rnams sems kyang skye par 'gyur/rgyu myed par ni ci ltar sems/sems tsa du ni myi
114b5(1): rung ngo/'phags pa'i dngos po'i tshul la ni/de bzhin nyid dang sems tsam yod/yod pa 'ang myin pa de dag ni/nga'i tshul la myi mkhas pa'o/bzung dang 'dzin pa'i dngos por ni/gal te sems ni 'jug gyurd na/de ni
114b6(1): 'jig rten pa'i sems/sems tsam du yangamyi rung ngo/lus dang long spyod gnas 'dra dang/gal te rmi lam 'dra skye na/seMs ni rnam pa gnyis su 'gyur/sems la gnyis kyi mtshan nyid myed
114b7(1): ci ltar ral gyi ngar so dang/ci ltar 'dzub mo rang rtse la/myi gcod reg par myi 'gyur ba/de bzhin sems kyi rang lta la'o/gzhan dang myed cing dbang yang myed/rtag pa'i mi dngos po myed/chos lnga rnaMs
114b8(1): dang sems gnyis dang/snang ba myed la myed pa'o/skye bar bya dang skye byed dang/dngos po'i mtshan nyid rnam gnyis ste/skye par byed las dgongs byas nas/rang bzhin myed par ngas bshad do/rnam pa sna tshogs
114b9(1): don dbyibs su /gal te rnam par rtog skyed na/ri bong rwa dang nam mkha' yang/don tu snang ba 'gyur bar 'gyur/gal te ma rtags don yod na/sems kyang don tu snang bar 'gyur/seMs dang ba dang rtag pa'i
114b10(1): don ni shind tu myi dmyigs so/thog ma myed pa'i khor ba na/don ni gang na 'ang myed pa ste/seMs ni rgyas par ma gyurd na/ci ltar don tu snang bar 'jug/gal te myed par rgyas gyurd na/ri bong rwa yang der 'gyur ro/dngos po myed pas rgyas gyur pa'i
114b11(1): rnaM par rtog pa myi 'byung ngo/ci ltar da ltar myed pa bzhin/sngon yang de ni yod pa myin/don myed pa ni don bral seMs/ci lta bur na 'jug par 'gyur/de bzhin nyid stong nyid mtha'/mya ngan 'das dang chos kyi dbyings/chos rnaMs skye ba myed pa ni/don dam dang ni
115a1(1): //rang bzhin no//rgyu dang rkyen las rtog pa yis/srid pa rgyu myed ma byung bar/rab tu shes par ma gyurd pas/byis pa yod dang myed par ltung/snang pa myed par sems mngon ste/bye brag thog ma myed rgyus byung/thog ma myed
115a2(1): nas don myed na/bye brag ci'is skye bar 'gyur/gal te myed pas rgyas 'gyur na/dbul po dag kyang nor ldan 'gyur/don myed ci ltar seMs skye ba/thub pas bdag la bshad du gsol/'di dag thaMs cad rgyu myed de/seMs kyang myed cing spyod yul
115a3(1): myed/sems kyang rgyas par myi 'gyur te/srid pa gsuM ni byed sngags pa'o/skye ba rnam par zlog pa'i phyir/myi skye ba ni rab tu sgrubs/rgyu myed smra ba bshad pa ni/byis pa rnaMs kyis myi rig go/'di dag thams cad ma skyes pas/
115a4(1): dngos po myed pa 'ang ma yin te/rmi laM sgyu 'dra dri za'i grong/sgyu myed pa'i dngos po yod/ma skyes rang bzhin myed pa dang/ci phyir stong pa 'ang bdag la gsungs/'dus pa dag ni ma rtogs par/nam zhig dngos po myi snang ba/
115a5(1): de tshe ma skyes stong pa ste/rang bzhin myed par ngas bshad de/rmi laM skra bshad sgyu ma dang/dri za'i grong khyer smyig sgyu rnaMs/sgyu myed par yang snang 'gyur ba/'jig rten sna tshogs de bzhin no/de bzhin 'dus pa de rang
115a6(1): yang/snang ba myed par yod ma yin/'dus pa mu stegs lta ba ltar/'jigs pas myed par 'gyur ba myin/rgyu myed pa la rtsad bya ste ma skyes pa yang rab tu sgrub/skye ba myed par rab sgrubs na/
115a7(1): nga'i tshul ni myi 'jig go/rgyu myed smra pa stand na ni/ci ltar ji las ji dag gis/gang du rgyu dang skye 'gyur zhes/mu stegs can rnams 'jigs pa skye/rgyu las ma yin rkyen myed par/nam zhig 'dus byas mthong ba na/
115a8(1): de na skye 'jig smra ba'i/lta pa rnam par ldog par 'gyur/dngos po myed pa'i skye myed dam/de ste rkyen la rtag pa 'am/'on te dngos po'i mying lags sam/don myed pa yang bdag la gsungs/dngos myed myi skye ma yin te
115a9(1): rkyen la rtag pa 'ang ma yin no/dngos po'i mying yang de myin te/don myed pa yang myed ma yin/nyan thos dang ni rang rgyal dang/sa bdun du ni phyind rnaMs dang/mu stegs spyod yul ma yin pa/de ni myi skye mtshan nyid do/rgyu dang rkyen rnaMs
115a10(1): zlog pa dang/byed pa dang ni 'gag pa dang/seMs tsam du ni rnaM bzhag pa/skye ba myed par ngas bshad do/dngos po rgyu las byung myed pa/stog dang rtag pa sangs ba ste/yod myed phyogs las grol ba ni/skye ba myed par ngas bshang do
115a11(1): seMs ni snang dang rnam bral ba/rang bzhin gnyis kyang rnam spangs te/gnas rnaMs rab tu gyurd pa ni/skye ba myed par ngas gshad do/phyi rol dngos po dngos myed myin/sems kyang yongs su bzung ba myed/lta ba thaMs cad rab spangs pa
115b1(1): de ni skye myed mtshan nyid do/de bzhin stong pa rang bzhin myed/de la sogs pa'i tshig kun rtag/nam yang stong pas stong ma yin/skye ba myed pa'i stong pa'o/rkyen kyi rnaM pa 'dus pa las /rab tu 'jug cing ldog par 'gyur /'dus pa las
115b2(1): ni logs shig na/skye ba myed cing 'gag pa 'ang myed/gcig pa dang ni tha dad du /ci ltar mu stegs can rtog bzhin/dngos po tha dad ma yin dang/'dus las gud na gang na 'ang myed/yod myed dngos po myi skye ste/myed myin yod myin
115b3(1): gang na 'ang myed/de phyir bzhan du 'dus pa ni/rab tu 'jug cing ldogs par 'gyur/'di ni rnga tsam nyid yin te/phan tshun ltos pa 'brel pa yin/rkyen rnaMs 'brel las log shig na/skyed par bya ba'i don myed do/skyed pa myed pas skye
115b4(1): ba myed/mu stegs can gi skyon kyis sangs/'brel pa tsam du ngas bshad de/byis pa rnaMs kyis myi rig go/gang na 'brel las log shig na/dngos po skyed pa yod ces pa/'brel ba rnam par 'jig pa ste/rgyu myed smra ba shes
115b5(1): bar bya /gang la 'brel las logs shig na/dngos po su las yod na ni mar me bzhin du rdzas rig rnaMs/'brel ba snang bar byed par 'gyur 'brel ba dag las logs shig tu/chos 'gag myi mkhas rtog pa ni/
115b6(1): ma skyes ngo bo nyid myed do/rang bzhin nam mkha' lta bu'o/'phags pas thob pa'i chos nyid ni/skye pa myed pa gzhan yin te/gang de'i skye ba myed pa ni/de ni myi skye bzod pa yin/nam zhig 'jigs pa
115b7(1): 'di dag kun/'di ni 'brel ba tsam mo zhes/'brel ba nyid du mthong ba na/de na sems kyang mnyam par 'jog/myi shes sred pa lasogs pa/'breld pa dag ni nang gi yin/'khor lo 'jigs shing slub las
115b8(1): sogs/sa bon 'byung sogs phyi rol lo/'di ni 'brel pa tsam yin te/gang gi dngos po rkyen gzhan las/gang yang skye bar 'dod pa ni/thad dang lung de myi gnas/gal te skyed bya'i dngos skyed na/blo yang gang gi rkyen las
115b9(1): byung/'di dag phan tshun rkyen gyurd pas/de phyir de dag rkyen du shes/drod sher sra dang g.yo' pa'i /chos rnaMs byis pa rnaM rtags pa/'di ni 'dus pa'i chos ma yin/de phyir ngo bo nyid myed do/nad pa'i dbang du sman pa dag/bya ba
115b10(1): tha dad byed mod kyi/de la btsug lag tha dad myed/nad kyis phye pas tha dad do/de bzhin ngas kyang sems can rgyud/nyon mongs nges pa'i skyon chags pa/dpang po dag gi stobs shes nas/byis pa dag la tshul bshad do/nyon mongs
116a1(1): //dbang po tha dad pas/nga'i stan par dbyed myi phyed/yan lag brgyad (ldan)zhi ba'i/laM ni theg pa gcig yin no/rdzas dang snam bu gcod pan rwa/ri bong rwa ni myed pa'i rgyu/gang rgyu tshogs las byung pa de/khyod kyis byed par shes myi bya/yod pa'i rgyus ni/
116a2(1): myed pa ste/myed pa myed par myi rung ngo/yod pa myed pa'i zla (2) las byung/phan tshun zla'i rgyu yin no/ci zhig la ni gnas bcas shing/ci zhig snang bar sus bsams pa/gang la rten pa ci zhig ni/rgyu myed pas na rgyu myed myin/gal te gzhan
116a3(1): la de rten cing/de yang gzhan la snang gyurd na/thub pa myed par rab 'gyur te/ci zhig ci yang myed par 'gyur /ci ltar lo ma shing sogs la/rten cing sgyu ma snang pa ltar/de bzhin dngos la rten byas shing /myi rnams la ni sna tshogs snang
116a4(1): sgyu ma'i 'dra la lo ma myin/gyo mo ma yin shing ma yin/byis pa rnaMs kyis sgyu mar mthong/sgyu ma byed pas gnas mthong ngo/de bzhin dngos la rtenapacas te/ci zhig bye brag tu gyurd na/snang ba'i tshe na gnyis myed par
116a5(1): ci zhig tu ni ci ltar rtag/rnam par rtog la rnams rtags myed/rnam par rtog pa 'ang myed pa ste/rnam par rtog la myed pas na/mya ngan 'das myed 'khor pa myed /rnam par rtog pa myed pas na/rnam par
116a6(1): rtog pa myi 'jug go/ma zhugs ni ci ltar sems/sems tsam du ni myi ru ngo/bsam pa tha dad du ma'i phyir/stan pa la ni snying po myed /snying po myed phyir thard pa myed/'jig rten rnam pa sna tshogs myed/
116a7(1): ci ltar byis pas rtags pa'i/phyi rol gi ni snang ba myed/sems ni gzugs brnyan bzhin du snang/bag chags kyis ni mgo' yur slad /yod myed ma byung myed pas na/dngos po thams cad ma skyes te/'di dag thams cad
116a8(1): sems tsam mo/rtog pa rnams kyis rnam par spangs/byis pa rkyen kyis dngos por chad /mkhas pa dag ni ma yin no/rang bzhin sems dang bral ba ste /sems ni 'phags pa nyer 'gro zhing/grangs can gcer bu khyad bar can
116a9(1): de mazhin braM ze phyugs bdag rnams/yod dang myed pa'i lta bar ltung/rnam par dben pa don kyis spangs /rang bzhin myed dang ma skyed dang/stong pa sgyu ma dri myed rnams/sangs rgyas rnams dang khyod kyis kyang/su'i phyir ni de
116a10(1): dag bshad /rnal 'byord can ni seMs gtsang la/lta ba rtog ge rnam spangs shing/sangs rgyas rnams kyi rnal 'byor bshad/ngas kyang rab tu bshad pa yin/gal te 'di kun sems yin na/'jig rten ci la rab tu gnas
116b1(1): ci phyir myi rnams 'od 'gro bar /sa la rab tu snang bar gyurd/ci ltar bya rnams nam mkha' la/rtog pas kun tu skyod bas na/gnas myed rten pa myed bzhin du/sa'i stang bzhin de dag spyod/de bzhin lus can thams cad kyang //
116b2(1): rnam par rtog pas skyod bas na/de dag bdag gi seMs la rgyu/lus dang long spyod gnas snang ba'i/sems ni rab tu 'jug bshad pa/snang dang 'jug pa ci ltar cis/sems tsam po ni bdag la gsungs/lus dang long spyod gnas
116b3(1): rnams dang/snang dang 'jug pa bag chags dang/snang dang 'jug ba rnam rtog pas/spyi brtson rnams la skye bar 'gyur/dngos po rnam bar rtag pa'i yul /sems ni yul la 'byung pa ste/snang ba'i sems ni yongs shes pas
116b4(1): rnam par rtog pa myi 'jug go/rtags pa'i mying ni mying bral dang/blo dang rig bya spang bar ni/'dus byas nam zhig mthong bar 'gyur/de'i tshe na grol par 'gyur/mying la mying ni rnam sgom pa/shes par bya ba de tsam ste/
116b5(1): gang dag gzhan du shes byed pa/de dag myi shes shes byed myin/chos lnga rnams dang rang bzhin dang/rnam par shes pa brgyad rnaMs dang/bdag myed gnyis kyi nang du ni/theg pa chen po thams cad 'dus /gang gi tshe na
116b6(1): 'gro ba dang/blo dang rig byas mthong pa/de tshe mying la myi rtog pa/mngon par 'jug par myi 'gyur ro/sems mthong phyir ni bya ba dang yi ge rtog pa'i phyir log go/rang gi sems ni ma mthong phyir/
116b7(1): rnam par rtog pa rab tu 'jug/bzugs myed pa'i phung po bzhi'/de dag la ni 'grang ba myed/'byung ba tha dad mtshan nyid gzugs/ci ltar bzugs rnaMs mang por 'gyur/mtshan nyid yongs su btang
116b8(1): pa'i phyir/'byung pa las gyurd myi 'byung myin/'on te mtshan nyid gzhan bzugs na/phung po rnams kyis cir myi skye/skye mched phung po rnam bral ba/gang tshe mtshan nyid myed mthong zhing/chos la bdag myed mthong pa'i phyir
116b9(1): de tshe sems ni ldog par 'gyur/yul dang dbang po tha dad phyir/rnam par shes pa brgyad du skye/mtshan nyid kyis ni gsuM yin te/snang ba myed la ldog par 'gyur/kun bzhi yin kyi bdag yin te/shes pa nyid ni bdag
116b10(1): gi phyir/gnyis su 'dzind pas rnam du 'jug/yongs su rtogs na ldog par 'gyur/gcig dang tha dad rnam 'grol zhing/rgyu pa myed par rnam mthong pa/de tshe bdag dang bdag gir yang/gnyis su rtogs par myi 'gyur
117a1(1): //ma zhugs par ni rgyas par 'gyur /rnam par shes pa'i rgyu yang myin/rgyu dang 'bras bu dngos po'i /'gag pa 'jug par myi 'gyur ro/ji'i phyir na 'jig rten rnaMs/rnam rtog seMs tsam bdag la gsungs /rgyu dang rnam 
117a2(1): par bral ba'i/mtshan dang mtshan bzhi rnam spangs pa'i/rang gyi seMs ni sna tshogs snang/mthong ba lta bur rnam par rtags /sems kyi snang ba shes pa dang/kun bzhi yongs su bzung pa'i phyir/blo'i gang tshe ma thong na /myed pa'i
117a3(1): lta bar gyur pa'i /seMs su 'dzind par 'gyur ba'i phyir/ci phyir de'i yod myi 'gyur/rnam rtog dngos myin dngos myed myin/de phyir yod pa myi skye ste/sems snang yongs su shes pa'i phyir /rnam rtog 'jug par myi 'gyur ro//rnam
117a4(1): par rtog pa myi 'jug pa /rab tu gyurd pa gnas myed do/bzhi'i phyogs rnams myi zlog cing /gal te dngos po rgyu can na/mying gi bye brag gzhan de byas/nges ni sgrub pa ma yin no/rgyu dang rkyen rnams 'dus pa
117a5(1): ba dang/rgyu ni 'gag par 'gyur ba'i phyir/de dag don kyis dpog par 'gyur/yang na rgyu las byung bar 'gyur/gal te rkyen rnams myi rtag na/rtag pa'i nges bzlog par 'gyur/byis ba rnams ni myi 
117a6(1): rtag phyir/'byung ba myed cing 'jig pa myed/gang yang 'jig par 'gyur bzhin du /rgyur 'gyur ba ni myi snang ngo/ma mthong ba ni ci ltar cis/myi rtag pa lam srid myi 'byung/sdu pa dag gis sems can
117a7(1): 'dul/tshul khrims kyis ni dbang du bya/shes rab dag gis lta ba bzhig/rnam par thard pa shind tu spel/'di kun 'jig rten rkyang phan te/mu stegs can gis gang bshad rdzun/rgyu dang 'bras bu yod lta bas/
117a8(1): rang gi grub ba'i mtha' yang myed/nga 'da' gcig tu rang grub mtha'/rgyu dang 'bras bu rnam par spangs/'jig rten rkyang phan rnam 'grol ba /slob ma'i sde las bshad par bya /sems tsam de ni snang ba myed /rtag
117a9(1): dang chad pa spangs pa yin/bzung dang 'dzin pa'i dngos po ru/seMs ni bsaM ba gnyis su snang/ci srid sems ni 'jug pa'i bar/de srid 'jig rten rkyang phan yin /'jig rten rang gi seMs mthong na /rnaM bar rtog pa myi 'jugo
117a10(1): 'du ba 'bras bu don grub pa/za ma 'bras bu ma mthong na/'du dang zad pa yongs shes na/rnaM par rtog pa myi 'jug go/rtag dang myi rtag byas ba dang/ma byas pa dang gzhan myin gzhan /de lasogs pa thaMs cad ni /'jig
117a11(1): rten rkyang phan tshul yin no/lha dang lha ma yin dang myi/byol songs yi dgas gshin rje'i gnas/gang tu lus can skye ba'i /'gro ba rnam pa drug du bshad/rab 'bring tha ma'i las rnams kyis /de dag tu ni skye bar 'gyur/
117b1(1): dge ba thams cad srungs na ni/khyad par du 'am thard par 'gyur/khyod kyis dge slong sde rnams dang/skad gcig skad cig dag la yang/tshe dang skye ba 'chi bshad pa/dgongs pa bdag la bshad du gsol/ci ltar zugs la gzugs
117b2(1): bzhan bzhin/sems kyang 'dus nas 'jig par 'gyur/de phyir slob ma rnaMs la ni/skad cig cig nas gcig bshad pa/gzugs dang bzugs las rnam rtog pa/'byung dang 'jig pa de bzhin no/rnaM par rtog pa skye pa yin
117b3(1): rnam par rtog pa bzhan myed do/skad gcig skad cig gang ngas bshad/'di ni rkyen du bshad pa yin/bzugs su 'dzind pa rnam spangs pa/skye pa myed cing 'jig pa myed/rkyen dang rkyen las skyes pa rnams/ma rig de bzhin nyid la
117b4(1): sogs/chos gnyis kyis ni 'jug par 'gyur/myi gnyis de bzhin nyid yin no/rkyen dang rkyen las skyes ba'i/chos rnams gal te khyad myed na/rtag la sogs pa 'bras bur 'gyur/rkyend rnaMs kyi ni rgyur 'gyur ro/rgyu dang
117b5(1): 'bras bur grub pa'i phyir/khyod dang sangs rgyas kyi gsung/mu stegs can dang khyad myed 'gyur/de phyir thub cen 'phags ma yin/'dom gang tsam gi yus la ni/'jig rten kun 'byung 'jig rten dang/
117b6(1): 'gog par 'gro ba'i lam gyi rnams/rgyal sras rnams la rab tu bshad rang bzhin gsuM la 'dzind pa yis/so so'i skye bo bzung 'dzind la/'jig rten 'das dang 'jig rten pa'i/chos rnams rnam par rtog
117b7(1): par byed/de phyir sngon la lta bya ste/rang bzhin rnams ni smos par bya lta ba rnams ni zlog pa'i phyir/rang bzhin rnam par myi rtag go nyams pa nges phyir sdom pa myed/sems kyang 'jug par myi 'gyur ro/
117b8(1): gnyis su 'dzind phyir 'jig par 'gyur/de bzhin nyid ni gnyis myed yin/myi shes pa dang sred dang las/rnam shes la sogs tshul ma skyes/mthard thug myed dang byas pa dang/srid pa byas la myi skye'o/dngos po rnams kyi
117b9(1): 'jig pa ni/myi mkhas rnams kyis rnam bzhir bshad/rnam par rtog pa gnyis 'jug phyir/dngos po yod dang dngos myed myed/mtha' bzhi dag las grol ba'i phyir/lta ba gnyis po rnam spangs pa/mthong nas rnam rtog gnyis 'jug ni
117b10(1): mngon par 'jug par myi 'gyur ro/ma skyes pa'i dngos po rnams/blo ni rnam par ldang pa'i phyir/skyes pa'i dngos po dag la yang/de dang 'dra phyir myi rtag go/ci ltar bdag dang gzhan rnams ni/rtag tu bsod myed myi 'dre par/lta ba rnam
118a1(1): //par lta ba rnam gnyis bzlog pa yis/rigs pas mgon po bdag la gsungs/mu stegs smra dang ma 'dres shing/rang rgyal nyan thos rnams spangs pa/rgyal sras rnams ni myi 'jig phyir/rgyal bas mngon rtogs spyod pa dag
118a2(1): rnam thar rgyu dang rgyu myed kyang/skye pa myed par mtshan nyid gcig/rnam grangs dag gis de dag slu/mkhas pa dag gis rtag tu spangs/sprin dang sprin gi phung po 'dra/smyig rgyu skra bshad 'dzings pa bsgyu ma 'dra/dngos po thams cad rang gi rtogs
118a3(1): byung ba/mu stegs 'gro ba rang gi rgyu yis rtog/skye ba myed de de bzhin nyid/yang dag mtha' dang stong pa nyid/de dag gzugs kyi mying yin te/dngos po myed par myi rtag go/lag dang byed pa 'jig rten na/dbang po rgya byin grong 'jig ltar
118a4(1): de bzhin dngos po thams cad kyi/rang bzhin rtag par myi bya'o/gzugs las stong pa bzhan ma yin/myi skye pa yang de bzhin ste/gzhan ma yin phyir myi rtag go/lta ba'i nges pa rab tu 'gyur/dngos po'i mtshan nyid bzung ba'i
118a5(1): phyir/kun rtog rnam par rtog pa'o/yongs su rtog pa bzung bas na/ring thung zlum lasogs pa'o/sems ni kun tu rtog pa yin/yid ni yongs su rtog pa'o/yid kyi rnam shes rnam rtog ni/mtshan dang mtshan
118a6(1): gi bzhi spangs pa/gang yang mu stegs myi skye ba/gang yang nga'i tshul lta bas/rtog pa de ni khyad par myed/lta ba'i nges pa rab tu gyur/dgos dang skye pa myed pa dang/skye pa myed pa'i don nyid de/rigs pa shes pa sus shes pa
118a7(1): de dag nga'i tshul shes so/dgos pa lta ba dgong pa dang/myi skye ba ni gnas myed pa/don gnyis yongs su shes pa ni/skye ba myed par ngas bshad do/dngos po ma skyes rnams mchis sam/'on tam ma lags thub cen gsungs/rgyu myed smra
118a8(1): dang myi mnyam dang/myed dang chos rnams 'dres par 'gyur/rgyu myed smra bar myi skye dang/myi mnyam mu stegs lta ba ste/yod dang myed las rnam grol pa/sems tsam du ni ngas bshad do/lta ba'i rgyu las phyung pa'i/skye dang myi skye spang par byos/rgyu myed
118a9(1): smra par myi skye dang/skye bas rgyu'i gnas su 'gyur/lhun kyis grub par bya ba myed/gal te bya yod lta 'dres 'gyur/thabs dang smon lam lasogs pa/lta ba nyid ni bdag la gsungs/chos rnaMs thams cad myed pa'i phyir/ci lta bur na dkyil 'khor skye
118a10(1): bzung dang 'dzind dang bral ba'i phyir/'jug pa myed cing ldog pa myed/dngos po las ni dngos gzhan mthor/sems kyang de las byung pa ste/chos rnams kyi ni skye myed pa/de ni ci ltar bdag la gsungs/gal te seMs can myi shes na/de'i phyir ni de bshad do/thub cen
118b1(1): bshad pa thams cad la/snga ma dang ni phyi mar 'gal/mu stegs lta las rnam grol ba/myi mnyam rgyu myed spangs pa'i/'jug pa dang ni ldog pa rnams/smra ba'i mchog gis bdag la gsungs/yod dang myed las rnam grol zhing/rgyu dang 'bras bu myi 'jig pa'i
118b2(1): sa'i rim pa'i mtshams sbyor ba/pad mo'i spyan gis bdag la gsungs/'jig rten mtha' gnyis lhung pa ni/lta bas rnam par dkrugs 'gyur bas/skye dang myi skye lasogs phyir/zhi ba'i ni rgyu myi rtogs/nga la dkyil 'khor gang yang myed/nga ni chos nyid myi
118b3(1): 'gyur te/gnyis yod na ni nges par 'gyur/gnyis ni sangs rgyas rnams kyis spangs/stong dang /skad gcig dngos po rnams/rang bzhin myed cing skye myed pa/lta ngan smra bas g.yogs pa rtog/de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyis myin/ci ltar rnam par gang dag gyis
118b4(1): yul la mngon gsum 'gyur ba dang/rnam par rtog pa rab 'jug dang/ldog pa bdag la bshad du gsol/kha dog rgyas dang ldan pa'i phyir/sros pa rnams kyis bsdul ba'i bzugs/mthong nas phyi rol dag tu yang/rnam par rtog pa rab tu 'jug/de nyid yongasu
118b5(1): shes pa dang/yang dag ci bzhin don mthong phyir/'phags pa'i rigs dang mthun pa'i sems ni mngon par myi 'jug go/'byung pa rnams ni ma rtogs par/dngos po skye ba myed pa ste/rtag par 'byung ba 'dra ba'i sems/ma skyes par
118b6(1): ni rnaM par sgom/rnam par rtog la ma rtog shig/rnam par rtog myed mkhas pa'o/rnam par rtog la rtags gyurd na/gnyis yin mya ngan 'das ma yin/myi skye bar ni dam bca' ba'i/tshul ni sgyu ma bzhin du snang/sgyu ma rgyu myed 'byung
118b7(1): pa ste/grub mtha' 'das shing nyams par 'gyur/thog ma myed nas sgoms pa'i sems ni bzugs brnyan bzhin du snang/don lta bu ste don yod myin yang dag ci bzhin rnam par sgom/me long la ni ci ltar bzugs/gcig
118b8(1): dang tha dad gnyis spangs pa/snang ste de dag myed pa 'ang myin/skye ba'i mtshan nyid de bzhin no/dri za sgyu ma lasogs dag/rgyu dang rkyen la ltos pa ltar/de bzhin dngos po thams cad kyi/'byung ba yin te myi 'byung myed/bdag dang chos su btags
118b9(1): pa yis/rnam par rtog pa skyes bu ltar/'jug pa rnam pa gnyis kyis 'jug/byis pa rnams kyis myi rtogs so/ma tshang ba dang rkyen kyi dbang/de bzhin nyan thos dgra bcom pa/bdag mthu' rag las rgyal ba la yang/rig lus nyan thos
118b10(1): lnga por bshad/dus gzhan du ni zhig pa dang/don dam du yang phan tshun dang/myi rtag pa ni rnam pa bzhi/byis pa myi mkhas rtog par byed/yon tan rdul phran dang bzhin rgyus/byis pa mtha' gnyis ltung pa dag/yod dang myed pa'i phyogs 'dzin phyir
119a1(1): //thard pa'i ni thabs myi shes/ci ltar byis pa sor mo'i rtser/zla bar 'dzind te bzhan tu myin/de bzhin yi ger chags pa rnams/nga'i de bzhin nyid myi rtogs/'byung ba mtshan nyid myi mthun pa/bzugs kyi dngos po skyed pa ste/de ni
119a2(1): 'byung par gnas pa yin/'byung 'gyur 'byung bas ma byas so/me ni bzugs la sreg par byed/sprul pa'i rang bzhin chu'i khams/rlung gi bzugs la 'thor byed na/'byung bas ci ltar skye par 'gyur/rnam par shes dang bzugs phung po/
119a3(1): de ni gnyis te lnga ma yin/phung po 'o'i rnam grangs tha dad pa/ngas ni rnam pa brgya yang bshad/sems dang sems byung bye brag kun/'jug pa rab tu 'jug pa yin/bzugs kyi rnam pa 'dres pa ste/'byung las gyurd myin seMs bzugaso
119a4(1): sngo' lasogs pa zla 'is dkar/dkar lasogs pa zla 'is sngo'/rgyu dang 'bras bus skyed pa dang/yod dang myed pa stong pa nyid/sgrub byed sgrub dang sgrub bya dang/grang dro mtshan dang mtshan bzhi dang/de la sogs
119a5(1): pa thams cad ni/rtog ge can gis ma sgrubs so/sems dang yid dang rnam shes drug/de dag bdag dang ldan pa ste/gcig dang tha dad bral pa'i/kun bzhi de ni rab tu 'jug/grangs can gcer bu khyad bar
119a6(1): can/dbang phyug smra ba'i rtog ge pa/yod dang myed pa'i phyogs su ltung/dben pa'i chos kyis rnam par spangs/dbyibs dang byad kyi bye brag rnams 'byung las skyes te 'byung pa myin/mu stegs can ni 'byung gyurd
119a7(1): dang/'byung ba rnams dang skye par smra/de las gang gzhan mu stegs can/rgyu las ma skyes rtog par byed/yod myed phyogs la rten pa rnams/rmongs pas khyod du myi chud do nas/sems dang lhan gcig ldan ba ste/yid la sogs
119a8(1): pa bral pa yin/rnam dag mtshan nyid sems can ni/ye shes dang ni lhan gcig gnas/gzugs dang las ni gang yod dang/phung po yul gi rgyu 'byung dang/sems can rnams kyang nyer len myed/bzugs myed pa na myi gnas so
119a9(1): sems can myed par gyurd pa'i phyir/sems can smra la bdag myed 'gyur/rnam bar shes pa 'ang myi 'byung phyir/bdag myed smra pa chad par 'gyur/de'i gnas pa bzhi bo rnams/bzugs myed par ni ci ltar yod/phyi nang dngos po myed
119a10(1): pa'i phyir/rnam par shes par myi 'jug go/srid pa bar ma'i phung po rnams/rtog ge can rnams 'dod pa ltar/de bzhin gzugs myed skyes pa'i/srid pa'i bzugs ni myed dam ci/sems can rnam la myed pa ni/nan tan ma byas thar
119b1(1): bar 'gyur/mu stegs smra ba the tsom myed/rtog ge can gis myi shes so/de na bzugs kyang yod pa ste/gzugs myed pa la lta ba myed/de myed pas na grub mtha' myed/theg pa myed cing 'gro ba myed/dbang po rnams dang ldan pa'i/bag 
119b2(1): chags las gyurd rnam shes pa/rnam pa brgyad las phyogs gcig ni/skad gcig dus su myi 'dzin to/ji ltar bzugs ni myi 'jug ltar/dbang po rnams dang dbang myi 'jug/de phyir bcom ldan 'das kyis kyang/dbang po las
119b3(1): sogs skad gcig bshad/gzugs ni rtog par ma gyurd par/rnam par shes pa ci ltar 'jug/rnam par shes pa ma zhugs na/ci ltar 'khor pa skyed par 'gyur/skyes ma thag tu 'jig par ni/rnam par 'dren par myi 'chad do/dngos po
119b4(1): rnams ni rnam rtog pa/'gro ba bar chad myed par g.yo'/dbang po rnams dang dpang po'i don/mkhas la ma yin blun po la/byis pa rnams ni mying gis 'dzind/'phags pa'i mying ni don la mkhas/drug po la ni nyer len myed/
119b5(1): nyer len bcas pa myi zind to/yod pa'i nges dang rnam bral pa/bye brag myi phyed 'phags pa smra/shes pa spangs pa'i rtog ge ba/rtag dang chad par 'jig par 'gyur/'dus byas 'dus ma byas la bdag/byis pa bye brag myi 'byedo
119b6(1): gcig pa la yang sbyin pa yod/tha dad pa la 'ang yod ma yin/sems dang lhan gcig gcig pa ste/yid la sogs dang tha dad do/sems dang sems las 'byung sgrubs pa'i/sbyin pa gang tshe nyer len las
119b7(1): dbye na de la ci ltar na/gcig par khong du chud par bya/nyer len bcas pa'i dmyigs pa ni/las dang tshe dang byed sogs dang/'dra dang myi 'dra tshul rnams kyis/myed bzhin du ni sgrub par byed/ci ltar sreg bya sreg
119b8(1): byed la/me ni cig char sreg pa bzhin/de bzhin nyer len bcas pa'i bdag/ci phyir rtog ge can myi stond/rtag tu skyes dang ma skyes kyang/sems ni shind tu 'od gsal na/bdag sgrub pa'i dpe' dag tu/ci phyir rtog ge
119b9(1): can myi byed/rtog ge can gi tshul spangs pa/rnam shes thibs por rmongs pa dag/bdag tu smra bar byed 'dod pas/phan tshun dag tu rab tu rgyug/so so rang rig rtog bya ba/bdag ni rnam dag mtshan nyid de/de bzhin gshegs
119b10(1): pa'i snying po de/rtog ge can gi spyod yul myin/nyer len nye bar len byed kyi/bye brag dang ni phung po'i/mtshan nyid gal te shes gyurd na/tshul gi ye shes skye par 'gyur/mu stegs can gi brjod pa ni/kun bzhi'i snying po lta bur sma
120a1(1): //bdag dang lhan gcig ldan pa'i chos /rab tu brjod pa ma byas so/de dag rab tu phye bas na/rnam par thard dang bden mthong na/sgom dang mthong bas spang ba'i /nyon mongs rnams kyang rnam byar yin/sems ni rang bzhin 'od gsal te
120a2(1): de bzhin gshegs pa'i snying po dge/sems can rnams kyi nyer len ni/mtha' dang mtha' myed spangs pa'o/byi dor byas pa'i gser mdog dang/sa le sbram dang kha ra dang/ci ltar mthong ba de bzhin du/phung po rnams la sems can no/
120a3(1): gang zag myed cing sems can myed/sangs rgyas ye shes zag myed pa/rtag tu zhi ba rnam sgom ste/de la bdag ni skyabs su mchi'/sems ni rang bzhin 'ong gsal ba/yid lasogs pa'i nyon mongs dngos/bdag dang lhan cig ldan bar ni
120a4(1): smra ba'i mchog gis rab tu lta na/sems ni rang bzhin 'do gsal ba/de las gzhan pa thog ma myed/de dag gis ni bsags pa'i lam/des ni gnyis nyon mongs 'gyur ba/thog ma myed pa'i blo bur gi/nyon mongs rnams dang
120a5(1): ldan bas na/rang bzhin 'od gsal bdag nyon mongs/ras dang 'dra par dag par 'gyur/ci ltar gos sam gser yang rung/dri ma myed cing nges spangs bzhin/gnas shing 'jigs par myi 'gyur te/nges
120a6(1): myed bdag nyid de bzhin no/rnga 'am dung 'am pi '+wang la/sgra brnyan phun gsum tshogs pa dag/myi mkhas la la 'tshol bar byed/de bzhin gang zag phung po la'o/sa 'og gter dang nor bu dang/de bzhin du ni
120a7(1): chu rnams dang/yod bzhin du 'ang myi snang ba/de bzhin gang zag phung pi la'o/sems dang sems las byung tshogs dang/bdag gi yon tan phung po dang/myi mkhas pas ni myi shes ste/de bzhin gang zag phungapo la'o//
120a8(1): ci ltar sbrum par gyurd pa'i bu/yod bzhin du yang myi snang ba/de bzhin bdag ni phung po la/rigs pa shes pas myi mthong ngo/ci ltar nan gis bsod pa dang/ci ltar me ni shing rnams la /rigs pa myi shes pas myi thod
120a9(1): de bzhin gang zag phung bo la/dngos po rnams la myi rtag dang/stong pa nyid kyang yod bzhin du /myi mkhas rnams kyis myi mthong pa/de bzhin gang zag phung pe la'o/sa dang dbang dang mngon shes dang/bla ma'i ni dbang skur dang/
120a10(1): ting nge 'dzind dang khyad par rnams/bdag myed na ni myed pa yin/gang tshe 'jig byed song nas ni/gal te yod na stond ces zer/rang gi rnam rtog stond cig ces/de la mkhas bas sgom par bya /sangs rgyas chos la gnod byed dang/
120b1(1): yod myed phyogs la lta ba'i/bdag myed smra dang smrar myi bya/dge' slong las la spang par bya/bdag myed nags 'tshal sreg byed cing/mu stegs nges las rnam bgrol ba/dus mtha'i me ni 'bar ba bzhin/bdag tu smra 'di rab tu 'bar/khan

120b4(1): myi 'jug par/rtog ge ba la nges par 'gyur/sems kyi rjes lta rnal 'byord pa/sems rnams kyis ni sems myi mthong/mthong byed snang las byung bas na/snang ba ci'i rgyu las byung/nga ni ka ta ya na'i rigs/gtsang ma'i gnas nas byung
120b5(1): ba te/mya ngan 'das pa'i grong 'gro ba/chos ni sems can rnams la stond/'di ni sngon gi lam yin te/nga dang de bzhin gshegs rnams kyis/stong phrag gsum gi mdo rnams kyis/mya ngan 'das pa shind tu bshad/
120b6(1): 'dod pa'i khams dang gzugs myed du/sangs rgyas rnam par 'tshang myi rgya/gzugs kyi khams kyi 'og myin du/'dod chags bral khyod 'tshang/rgya'o/'ching ba'i rgyu ni yul ma yin/rgyu ni lus la 'ching ba ste/ye shes
120b7(1): kyis ni nyon mongs bsad/myed sgyu ma lasogs pa/chos rnams ci ltar yod myed 'gyur/byis pa rnams la yang snang/bdag myed nyid ni ci ltar myed/byas pa dang ni ma byas phyir/rgyu ni 'jug par byed pa myed/
120b8(1): 'di dag thams cad ma skyes te/byis pa rnaMs kyis myi shes so/rgyu'i rnams ni ma skyes te/rkyen rnams de dag byas pa'o/de gnyis skyed pa ma yin na/rgyu rnaMs kyis ni ci ltar rtag/snga phyi dang ni cig car yang/rtog ge ba rnaMs rgyur stond te/snang dang slob ma
120b9(1): rdzas sogs kyis/dngos po rnaMs kyi skye ba 'chad/mngon par 'du byed mtshan rnaMs kyis/sangs rgyas mtshan ldan ma yin te/de dag 'khor los sgyur yon tan/des ni sangs rgyas myi brjod do/sangs rgyas mtshan ni ye shes te/lta ba'i nges pa rnaMs sangs pa

121a9(1): shag kya seng ge rtsod pa'i dus/don grub pa dang shag kya'i sras/'jug gsel rgyas dang dbang phyug dang/de lasogs pa'i mu stegs can/mya ngan nga 'das 'byung bar 'gyur/'di skad bdag gis thos las sogs/shag kya seng ge'i bshad pa yin/
121a10(1): sgrung dang ldan chad byung ba dag/de dag rgyas pa yin par 'gyur/'jug gsel dang ni dbang phyug dang/'byin par byed pa chad par 'gyur/mya ngan nga 'das phyind chad ni/de lta bu lasogs pa 'byung/nga'i sa ni nor ldan te/pha ni bram ze skye dgu'i bdag/
121b1(1): nga ni ka ta ya na'i rigs/mying ni rdul myed rgyal ba'o/yab dang mes dang nga bdag dang/yul ni tsam par skyes pa ste/mtshan ni zla ba skyabs zhes bya/zla ba'i rigs las de skyes so/tha dad par zhugs shing tul shugs spyad/tshul yang rna ba stong bshad nas/

121b7(1): pa byed pa zhing bzhin no/gal te gcig po mar 'gyur na/thams cad ma byas par 'gyur ro/byas na rnam par 'jig par 'gyur/'di ni rtog ge can gi yul de ni me 'bar sa bon 'dra/'dra phyir mang du ga la 'gyur/de lta bur na mar

121b10(1): srung byed pa/rgyal stobs chen sa gtong ba'o/grog ma'i khar dang sran chung myig/gya gyu can dang rta 'dzind du/khyu mchog dpal chen rnam dag kyang/ma 'ongs dus na 'byung bar 'gyur/don grub pa dang shag kya'i bu/yang dag mtha' dag phung bu lnga/tshig stobs

122b6(1): yin/mdal sgor drod shed la sogs pa/srid pa par mar 'byung pa dag/sems can mang po sna tshogs bzhin/'gro dang gro pa rnam par sgoms/lung dang rig pas bral gyurd pa/nyon mongs phyogs dang zang 
122b7(1): thob byed/mu stegs lta ba'i smra pa la/blo gros can gis myi spyad do/thog mar nye bar len pa las/bdag ni phye bas bye brag dbye'/ma phye bye brag 'byed pa ni/mo sham bu la bye brag 'byed/'phrul gis mthong pa sems

123a1(1): //de ni sangs rgyas zhes bya bar /rtog can rnams rab tu brjod/shes pa 'du byed rgyu byung ba'i/'du byed rnams kyis myi brjod do/'du byed sgrub ba'i shes pa ni/'du byed du ni rtogs par 'dzind/'di yod pas ni 'di
123a2(1): byung ste/rkyend rnams la ni rgyu byed pa/gsal bar byed pa rab tu ston/de myed pas na byed pa myed/rlung ni me dang srin dag las/skyod pa yin te 'byin pa myin/skyod nas de ni zhi gyurd nas/ci ltar sems can rab tu sgrubs/

123a8(1): myi brtag pa/blo ngan rnams kyis myi rtogs te/brjod du myed pa smra bar byed/ci ltar tsan dan byas pa'i rdza/byis ba dag gis gzhan du byed/de bzhin tsan dan a gar dri/shes dang rtog ge ngan pas so/langs nas 
123a9(1): kha zas phyir myi lend/lhung gzed gnas dang phyid pa can/gdong 'gyur la sogs nyes pa rnams/dag pas zas la spyad par bya/su zhig tshul 'dis rig pas dpog byed pa/dad ldan rnal 'byord rtson zhing myi rtog pa /

123b1(1): phyag gis dbang skur 'gyur/mu stegs rgyu'i phyogs kyis slad/gzhan yang rkyend kyis rnam par rmongs/gzhan dag rgyu myed de byung pas /don myed pa ni 'chang ba'o/yid dang rnam par shes pa dang/rnam par smyind par gyurd 
123b2(1): pa ste/yid ni kun bzhi las 'byung pa /rnam par shes pa yid las byung/kun bzhi las ni sems kun kyang /dpa' rlabs bzhin du 'byung bar 'gyur/rkyen bzhin du ni 'byung 'gyur ba/skad gcig tha dad 'brel

123b4(1): ste/mu stegs lta ba zlog pa'i /de'i rgyus 'byung gzhan nyid kyang /de las dmyigs nas 'jug par 'gyur/de las lta ba shes pa dang/'khor pa rab tu 'jug par 'gyur/dngos po'i rmi lam sgyu ma 'dra/dri za'i grong
123b5(1): khyer rnam bzhin te/smyig rgyu ba dang chu zla dang/rang gi rnam rtog rnam par sgoms/'jug pa'i rnam pas de bzhin nyid/yang dag ye shes de'i gnas/sgyu ma dpa' bar 'gro lasogs/ting nge 'dzind ni gzhan
123b6(1): rnams dang/mngon par shes dang dbang rnams dang/ye shes sgyu ma lta bu dang/de bzhin gshegs pas dbang skur pa/sa la zhugs nas thob par 'gyur /gang tshe 'gro ba rnam rtog mthong/de tshe 
123b7(1): sems ni ldog par 'gyur/sa ni rab tu dga' ba dang/sangs rgyas sa dang sa rnams thob/gnas ni rab tu gyurd pa'i phyir/ci ltar sna tshogs nor bu bzhin/sems can rnams kyi don byed do/chu la gzugs
123b8(1): brnyan ci bzhin no/yod dang myed pa'i phyogs rnams dang/gnyis dang gnyis myed pa dang bral/nyan thos rang rgyal 'das gyurd na/sa bdun por ni 'gyur ba yin/so so dang chos mthong pa ni/yang dag pa'i rab sbyangs te/phyi rold mu 
123b9(1): stegs rnams sangs pa'i /theg pa chen po bshad par bya /rnam par rtog par rab gyurd pa'i/shi 'pho 'jigs pa rnam spangs pa/ri bong spu ni nor bu bzhin/grol ba rnams la tshul mang bshad/ci ltar tshig la tshig rnams la /gal te
123b10(1): rigs la rigs de bzhin/de phyir rigs pa'i rigs pa yin/de las gzhan du ma rtogs shig/myig dang las dang sred pa dang/ma rig de bzhin tshul bzhin myin/myig dang bzugs dang yid rnams dang/brnyog pa'i yid kyang de bzhin no

124a4(1): tu myed pa la yang dag par zhugs pa /theg pa chen po tshad myed pa bla na myed pa la yang dag par bzhugs pa/theg pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa la yang dag par zhugs/pa/theg pa mtshungs pa myed pa la yang dag par
124a5(1): theg pa dpag tu myed pa la yang dag pa zhugs pa/theg pa/bla na myed pa la yang dag bar zhugs pa/'jig rten gyi khams dpag tu myed pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing tshad myed pa yongs su dag pa'i smon lam la
124a6(1): yang dag par zhugs pa/'jig rten gyi khyams grangs myed pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhang tshad myed pa'i yongs su dag pa'i smon la yang dag par zhugs ba/'jig rten gyi khams bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i sangs rgya
124a7(1): sa kyi zhing tshad myed pa yos su dag pa'i smon lam yang dag par zhugs pa/'dzig rten gyi khams mtshungs pa myed pa'i sangs rgyas kyi shing tshad myed pa bla na myed yongs su dag pa'i smon ma lam yang dag par zhugs ba'i/'jig rten gyi
