128a10(1): rigs byed dang /srid srungs gi rigs byed dang /sgra'i bstand chos rnams dang /gso' ba'i bstan chos rnams dang /nor sgyur dang /dug dang /sngags dang /sman gyi rgyu dang /dus khor ba dang /dus dang /bzang ba dang /rgyu skar shes pa yang rab tu
128b1(1): bstond to /sems can rnams la phan pa dang ldan pa'i bzo' po'i gnas thams cad kyang stond to /nam sangs rgyas 'byung ba de na de 'khor lo sgyur ba'i rgyal por 'gyur ro /rgyal po'i srid por nas 'khord dang bcas te /khyim nas
128b2(1): khyim myed par rab tu 'byung ngo /rab tu byung nas de bzhin gshegs pa de dag gis bstand pa yang kun 'dzind /yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa de dag ring sreld la yang mchod pa rnam pa mang pos yongs su sbyor bar 'gyur ro /skye bo
128b3(1): 'i tshogs dpag tu myed pa rnams sangs rgyas la mchod pa byed du 'jug par 'gyur /dam pa'i chos dang ldan pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi mchod pa dang ldan pa dang /sems can yongs su smind par byed pa dang /ldan pa'i bdag gis dge ba'i rtsa ba rnams
128b4(1): bla na myed pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sngo' zhing sems can thams cad dang mthun mong du byas nas /sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongs su dag pa'i phyir yongs su sngo'o /'jam dpal byang chub sems dpa' sprin gyi rgyal po des /byang chub
128b5(1): sems dpa' thams cad byang chub mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas pa la de lta bu'i tshul du bde' ba'i yo byad thams cad kyi rim 'gror byas /bzhugs pa dang /yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa'i de dag thams cad dang /de bzhin gshegs pa
128b6(1): tshad myed pa gzhan gyi yang dam pa'i chos dus thog ma dad par ma dang /tha ma gsum du bzung ste /'on kyang de la sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das de rnams kyis bla na myed pa yang dag pa rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu lung ma bstand to /
128b7(1): de ci'i phyir zhe na /de shes rab kyi pha rold tu phyind pa las nges par 'byung ba ma yin pa'i phyir ro /de bzhin gshegs pa mtshungs pa myed pa'i gzi brjid 'od de yang de'i tshe mya ngan las 'das te /de nas byang chub sems dpa' i sha gi rgyal po de /bcom lda
128b8(1): 'das de bzhin gshegs pa mtshung pa myed pa gzi brjid 'od kyis sangs rgyas kyi zhing du smon lam gyi dbang gis yang dang yang skyes nas /de'i dge ba'i rtsa ba yongs su smind pa'i stobs des de bzhin gshegs pa mthong ma thag tu shes rab kyi pha
128b9(1): rold tu phyind pa yongs su dag par byas ste /de'i tshe de bzhin gshegs pa des bla na myed pa yang dag bar rdzogs pa'i byang chub du lung bstand to /dge ba'i rtsa ba gang la rtend pas dge slong srin gyi rgyal po shes rab kyi pha rold du phyind
128b10(1): pa yongs su dag ces na /'di lta ste dus gsum mnyam pa nyid la 'jug pa dang /dus gsum gyi tshogs thams cad mnyam par mthong nas /dge slong srin gyi rgyal po shis rab kyi pha rold phyind pa yongs su dag go /'dus byas dang /'dus
129a1(1): //ma byas kyis chos rnams tha dad par myi mthong pas /deg slong sbrin gyi rgyal po shes rab kyi pha rold tu phyind pa yongs su dag go//chos thams cad ngo bo nyid dang 'bral par mthong nas /dge slong sprin gyi rgyal po shes rab
129a2(1): kyi pha rold tu phyind pa yodas su dag go /chos thams cad ma skyes ma 'gags par mthong pas dge slong srin gyi rgyal po shes rab kyi phra rold tu phyind pa yongs du dag go /chos thams cad 'ong ba 'ang ma yin/'gro ba yang ma yin par mthong bas
129a3(1): dge slong sprin gyi rgyal po shis rab kyi pha rold tu phyin pa yongs su dag go//chosathams cad gnas par mthong bas dge slong srin gyi rgyal bo shes rab kyi pha rold du phyind pa yongs su dag ga//chos thams cad mya ngan las 'das pa dang /mthu
129a4(1): ngas par mthong bas dge slongs srin gyi rgyal po shes rab kya pha rold tu phyind pa yos su dag go /chos thams chad brjod du myed par mthong bas dge slong sprin gyi rgyal bo yongs su shes rab kyi pha rold tu phyind pa yongs su dag//chos thams
129a5(1): cad bdag myed par mthong sba dge slong srin gyi rgyal po shis rab kya pha rol da tu phyind pa yongs su ga go /chos thams cad la sems myed par mthong pas dge slong sprin gyi rgyal po shes rab kyi pha rold tu pyind pa yongs su dag go //chos thams
129a6(1): chos thams cad srog myed par mthong bas dge bslong srin gyi rgyal po shes rab kyi phra rold tu phyind pa yongs gsu dag go /chos thams cad lagang zag myed par mthong pas dge bslong sprin gyi rgyal po shis rab kyi pha rold tu phyi nad
129a7(1): pa yongs su dag go//chos thams cad nam mkha' dang mtshungs pas mthong bas dge slong spring gyi rgyal po shes rab kyi phyind pa yongs su dag go//chosathams cad dbyings las 'byung pa thos pas dge slong sprin gyi rkyal po
129a8(1): shes rab kyi pha rold tu byind pa yongs su dag go/de ltar chos kyi dbyings las chos thams cad 'byung par mthong pas/dge slong sprin gyi rgyal bo shes rab kyi pha rold du phyind pa yongs su dag ga//de ltar de shes rab kyi pha rold
129a9(1): tu phyind pa yongs su dag pas 'di lta bu'i dge ba'i rtsa ba shugs rnyed de/de lta bu'i dge ba'i rtsa ba'i shugs rnyed pas/ta la bdun tsam du nam mkha' la 'phags te/de par snang la 'dug nas/phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams tshad
129a10(1): myed pa nas sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das mthong ba/la la ni bla na myed pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas/la la ni bla na myed pa'i chos kyi 'khor lo bskor/la la ni rdzu 'phrul gyi cho 'phrul
129b1(1): du mas sems can rnams 'dul /la la ni tshe'i 'du shes 'dor /la la ni phung bo'i lhag ma myed pa'i mya ngan las 'das pa'i dbyings su yongs su mya ngan las 'da' par mthong ste /mthong nas de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par
129b2(1): rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas mtshungs pa myed pa'i gzi brjid 'od de la shes rab kyi pha rold tu phyind pa dang ldan pa'i tshigs su bcad pas mngon par bstod do /bskal pa bye ba mang por 'khor ba na /spyin pa dag pa mtha' yas rgya cher stsald 
129b3(1): bde' bar gshegs pa'i bstand par 'dri zhing /sbyin bdag mang po dag la bdagi gis rnyed /'jig rten rig pa 'jig rten kun gras pa /s+yang lnga ces bya mkhyend pa ste /ri rab rtsegs 'dra bsod nams brgya'i mtshan /byang chub 'tshald
129b4(1): slad de la rim 'gror bgyis /bye ba khrag khrig drug bcu' mtha' yas pa /de'i dge 'dun bde shes sras la yang /lo bye ba khrag khrig bdun bcu'i bar du ni /rgyal pa nyan thos bcas la snyen bkur bgyis /dga' zhing mos pas mngon
129b5(1): par skyed nas su /zas skom rnam mang yid du 'ong ba dag /bde bar gshegs pa bdag gis dbul pa phul /'on kyang bde gshegs des bdag lung ma bstand /'das pa dus tshe sangs rgyas
129b6(1): 'byung ba ni /mkhyend pa 'jig rten mgon po mya ngan sel /myi rtsi srin ches bgyi ba'i de bzhin gshegs /nyan thos bcas pas rgyal pa de bsnyend bkur bgyis /'dren pa de la lo bye ba drug bcu' bzhir
129b7(1): bdag gi 'khord dang bcas pa bkur stir bgyis /bdag gis ye shes yangs pa ma sbyangs pas /'on kyang bde gshegs nges bdag lung ma bstand /'das pa'i dus na 'dren pa 'byung pa ni /ye shes rgya mtsho'
129b8(1): zhes kyang phyogs bcur grags /chos rgyal de'i 'khor du 'gyur ba ni bye pa khrag khrig bcur tshangs pa lags /bdag gis rgyal mchog nyan thos bcas pa de la /lo dgu bcu'i bar du snyend bkur bgyis byang chub dam
129b9(1): pa 'tshald pa'i slad du 'dir /bdag gis sbyin pa mtha' yas rgya cher 'tshald /mya ngan las 'das par gyurd nas lo lnga nas /bder gshegs nyan thos dag la bkur stir byas /bshos dang skyems kyi rnam par rab tu
129b10(1): sbyard /'on kyang bder gshegs des bdag kyang lung ma bstand /'das pa'i dus na 'khor lo 'byung ba mkhas pa ye shes ri bo de bzhin gshegs /de'i zhing rnams ni /la las btags nas phra rab rdul gyis la /de la myi gdal
130a1(1): /la byung ba'is /phra bar rdul du bgyis pa'i rdul de thogs /myi des shind tu rdul phra de thogs te /shar phyogs su mgyogs ldan de mchis la /gang 'ga'i klung gi bye ma snyed sangs rgyas zhing /de'i gom pa gcig gi de dag rgal
130a2(1): de bzhin myi des bskald par brgyar mchis nas /tha mar shind tu phra ba'i rdul gcig por /de bzhin rab tu phra ba'i rdul de kun /shar kyi phyogs su mchis nas thams cad por /de bzhin ma lus phyogs bcu' thams cad du /ci snyed kyis ni
130a3(1): rdul phran kun por te /de dag zhing rnams gang tu por ba dang /gang tu rdul phran bor ba de dag rnams /grong khyer gcig tu de kun bgyis nas su /de'i nyungs dkar dag gis gang bar bgyis /gal te 'jig rten khams rnams thams cad kyang /
130a4(1): yungs 'brus gang ba'i la la su spro ba /btags nas shind tu phra ba'i rdul du bgyis /thams cad kyang ni ci ltar snga ma bzhin /de bzhin thams cad phyogs kyi zhing rnams su /stong gsum bsgyur cing 'gre ba bgyis pa yang /de'i tha
130a5(1): ma 'tshald bar nus kyang gda' /skyob ba de'i zhing ni tshad ma mchis /bder gshe gas de la dgra bcom mang ni /nyan thos khrag khrig bsam myi khyab pa/rgyal ba de la nyan thos bcas pas gcod /de bzhin gshegs pa des
130a6(1): bdag lung ma bstand /'das pa'i dus na 'drend pa 'byung ba ni /'jig rten mchod rten zhes ni phyogs su grags /gang zag mchog de bdag gi mchod pas na yang /de bzhin gshegs pa nges bdag lung ma stand /ma smad pa'i bde
130a7(1): gshegs shes rab brgyad /rgyal grags 'changs 'jigs mdzad des pa dang /sa la'i rgyal po nor bzangs 'od chen dang /rtog 'i rgyal po bsod nams brgya'i mtshan /bdag gis 'drend pa'i de dag nyan thos bcas /byang chub mchog rab 'tshald tu bkur 
130a8(1): sting bgyis /'on kyang shes rab mthard phyind ma spyangs pas /bde' gshegs des bdag lung ma stand /bskald pa gcig la mthu cen bde bar gshegs /thams cad gzigs pa stong phrag drug byung ste /bdag gis gang zag mchog de kun
130a9(1): mchod kyang /bde gshegs de dag gis kyang lung ma bstand /'das ba'i dus na 'drend pa byung ba ni /zlas byin zhes kyang phyogs bcur grags pa ste /rgyal ba nyan thos bcas pa bkur stir bgyis /de bzhin gshegs pa des bdag lung ma stand/
130a10(1): 'das pa'i dus na 'drend pa 'byung ba'i /tsan dan dam pa bde gshegs phrul chen te /myi 'i dam pa de la 'ang bsnyen bkur bgyis /gang zag mchog des bdag la lung ma stand /'drend pa 'jig rtend mkhyend pa sngon 'byung ba /glog sbyin zhes
130b1(1): bgyi phyogs bcur grags pa ste /rgyal de yang nyan thos bcas pas mchod /de bzhin gshegs pa des bdag lung ma bstand /sngon gi sangs rgyas rkang gnyis mchog 'byung ba /the tsom kun kyis bcings pa'i gcod mdzad pa /sprin sbyin rnam 'dren
130b2(1): bde bar gshegs pa ste /de la rim 'gro bgyis kyang lung ma stand /sngon sangs rgyas rnams des sdig myi mnga' /bdagra bcom zhes kyang phyogs su grags pa ste /'jig rten khams pa de la mchod bgyis kyang /de bzhin gshegs des bdag lung
130b3(1): ma bstand /sngon gyi sangs rgyas khrag khrig mtha' yas pa /'jig rten mgon po mtshungs myed nyan thos bcas /byang chub mchog 'tshal /bkur stir rab bgyis nas /bde gshegs de dag gis kyang lung ma bstand /mtshungs myed 'drend
130b4(1): pa rnams pa la bkur stir bgyis /shes rab dam pa mchog gi bdag gis sbyangs /chos kyi tshul mtshan nyid de rtogs nas /des ni dri myed mchog gis bdag lung bstand /'di ltar bdag gis de ring chos nyid mthong /the tsom ma lus par ni thams cad
130b5(1): bcad /sgyu ma skye bu chu zla 'dra ba ru /chos rnams thams cad skye ba bdag gis mthong /pa /ci ltar myi rnams ri'i rab na yang /yid du 'ong ba'i glu blangs skad 'byind te /skad cig tu ni brag cha gzhan yang 'byung /de'i sgra la byed pa
130b6(1): bo mchis /ri rab ni ci ltar brag ca bzhin bdag chos rnams yang dag mthong /de la bdag ni the tsom mchis te /des na gang zag mchog des bdag lung bstand nam mkha' bar snang ci ltar bya'i brjes /de dag ma mchis ma byung
130b7(1): byung myi 'gyur /rgyal bas bstand pa /de bzhin mthong ba na /des na gang zag mchog gis bdag lung bsand /ci ltar rmi lam la ni skom ba myis /chu grang ba dang ba de'is 'tshal na yang /skom ba chang cing zhi bar myi 'gyur ba /
130b8(1): de bzhin bdag gis 'dod pa'i yon tan bdag gis rtogs /rnga rdung de las sgra rab 'byung ste /skye bo byis rnams myi dga' bar 'gyur /de yang ko ba dang ni shing las byas /sgra myi rnyed bzhin chos nyid do /ci ltar bying bar lend
130b9(1): ba bsgrengs pa'i /rgyud nas snyand pa'i sgra yang shind tu gtong /shing dang rgyud rnams las kyang ma lags te /sgra de myi rnyed de bzhin chos nyid do /gzugs dang /sgra dang /ro dang dri dang /reg kyang /bi waM dag gi sgra 'drar mthong bas na /the tsom
130b10(1): dge ba thams cad kun tu bcom /de bas 'jig rten 'dren pas bdag lung bstand /sa dang shing dang mun dza bal ba dza /de bzhin 'o ma rtswa dang chung brtend te /bar snang la ni de dag kun bskord nas /'jig rten kun rdzob khyim du gyurd ces brjod /
131a1(1): //de bzhin rus pa sen mo rgyus lpags dang /sdu dang skra dang khrag rnag pags pa yis /nam mkha' dag kyang kun nas bskord nas ni /de bzhin kun rdzob lus su gyurd ces brjod /gzugs ni dbu ba 'dra ste snying po myed
131a2(1): tshor ba rnams ni chu'i chu bur 'dra /'du shes rnams ni smyigs rgyu 'dra bar mthong /de la byis pa'i rang bzhin skyes pos nord /sems dag ni chu shing snying po 'dra /rnam shes rnam pa kun tu sgyu ma bzhin /gang gis 'gro ba ltar bcas
131a3(1): khyab byas /de ltar mthong pas /de nas de ga slong sprin gyi rgyal po des de bzhin gshegs pa mtshungs pa myed pa'i gzi brjid 'od de Sha/shes rab kyi pha rold tu phyind pa dang ldan pa'i tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag gis mngon par bsod
131a4(1): nas /dge ba'i rtsa ba des bskald pa brjod du myed par nams kyang chos la rmongs par gyurd /nams kyang tshul khrims la snying por 'du shes par ma gyurd /ting nge 'dzind rnams la snying por 'du shes bar ma gyurd /rtag tu rgyund
131a5(1): myi chad par shes rab kyi pha rold tu phyi nad pa la gzhol bar gyurd te /nams kyang shes rab kyi pha rold du phyind pa dngos por ma dmyigs te /'di lta ste dper na myi zhig zings la rgya mtsho chen por rgal nas /bzings por
131a6(1): nas gang dga' bar 'gro ba de bzhin du /dge slong sbri gyi rgyal po de yang shes rab kyi pha rold tu pyind pa shes par byed mod kyi /shes rab kyi pha rold tu phyend pa la mngon par zhend pa myi byed do /'di lta ste dper
131a7(1): na myi zhig mye 'dod mye dgos pa byed mod kyi /tshig pa'i nyes pa yang srung ba de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' srin gyi rgyal po de yang shes rib kyi pha rold tu phyind pas chos thams cad mthong mod kyi /mngon par
131a8(1): zhend pa'i nyes pa 'ang srung ngo /shes rab kyi pha rold du pyind pa yang gzhan stand mod kyi /mngon par zhend pa'i nyes pa las kyang zlog par byed do /shes rab kyi pha rold ta phyind pas sangs rgyas kyi zhing chad myed par sangs rgyas pa byed
131a9(1): mod kyi /de sems can de dag dang sangs rgyas de dag tu 'dzind pa yang myed de /'jam dpal de ci snyam du sems /de'i tshe de'i dus na /dge slong sprin gyi rgyal po zhes bya bar gyurd pa de gzhan zhig yin snyam du 'dzind na /'jam dpal
131a10(1): khyod kyi sa de ltar myi lta'o /de ci'i phyir zhe na /de bzhin gshegs pa don thams cad grub de'i tshe de'i dus na /dge sbyong sprin gyi rgyal po zhes pa bar gyurd to /'jam dpal de lta bas na /thabs la khasam ba gang dang ldan pas dge
131b1(1): slong sprin gyi rgyal po shes rab kyi pha rald tu phyind ba la spyod ba'i thabs khams pa de dang ldan pas /byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen pos shes rab kyi pha rold tu phyind pa la spyad par bya'o/de lta bu'i thabs dang ldan ba'i byang chub sems
131b2(1): dpa' ni sangs rgyas kyi zhing dpag tu myed pa yongs su sbyong ngo /sangs rgyas kyi grangs myed pa bsam gyi myi khyab pa /tshungs pa myed pa /gzhal du myed pa /brjod du myed pa yongs su sbyang ngo /de nas yang bcom ldan 'das kyis 'jam
131b3(1): dpal gzhon nur gyurd pa la bka' stsald pa /'jam dpal de bzhin gshegs pa don thams cad grub pa de sngon byang chub sems dpa'i spyad pa spyod pa na /byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzind zhi ba zhes bya ba sgoms te/
131b4(1): rab tu goms par byas lam du byas /rten du byas /nan tan du byas /rab tu bris par byas /legs par brtsams shing rab tu rdzogs par byas te /des sbyin pa'i pha rold tu phyind pa zhi ba rab tu zhi bar rtogs so /tshul khrims kyi pha rold
131b5(1): tu phyind pa dang /bzod pa'i pha rold tu phyind ba dang /brtson 'grus kyi pha rold tu phyind pa dang /bsam gtan gyi pha rold tu phyind pa dang/shes rab kyi pha rold tu phyind pa yang zhi ba rab tu zhi bar rtog so /'di lta
131b6(1): ste dper na /'dus ma byas kyi khams zhi ba dang rab tu zhi ba de bzhin du pha rold tu phyind pa drug kyang zhi dang rab tu zhi bar rtogs so/'di lta ste dper na /
131b7(1): bdag thams cad kyi thams cad du myi dmyigs pa de bzhin du pha rold du phyind pa drug kyang thams cad kyi thams cad tu myi dmyigs te /pha rold tu phyind pa drug ci lta bzhin du chos thams cad kyang myi skye myi gAg go /chos thams cad ni
131b8(1): zhi ba ste /zhi ba de yang myi dmyigs /gang gis zhi ba de yang myi dmyigs so /'jam dpal sngon 'byung ba 'das ba'i dus na /bskald pa grangs myed pa /bsam gyi myi khyab /mtshungs pa myed pa /gzhal du myed pa /brjod du myed pa'i
131b9(1): pha rold kyi yang ches pha rold na /nyi ma snang ba zhes bya ba'i bskald pa la de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rig dang zhabs su ldan pa /bde' bar gshegs pa /'jig rten mkhyend pa
131b10(1): skye bu 'dul ba'i kha lo bsgyurd pa /bla na myed pa /lha dang myi rnams kyi bstond pa /sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das thams cad zild kyis nond pa zhes bya ba 'jig rten 'byung ste /de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad zild kyis non pa /de'i rim
132a1(1): //'gro byed pa dge slong zhi ba'i blo gros bye ba zhig yod de /des de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad zild kyi non pa la lo bye ba phrag brgya bcur bla na myed pa'i yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub 'dod pa'i phyir yid du 'ong bas rim 'gro 
132a2(1): byas te /yid du myi 'ong bas ma yin no /de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa tham cad zild kyis gnod pa de'i gang du song nas 'di skad ces gsold to /bcom ldan 'das zhi ba sangs rgyas kyi sa la slob par 'tshal ba'i
132a3(1): byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po chos gang la brtson par bgyi/de skad ces gsold pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad zild kyis non pas /dge slong zhi ba'i blo gros la 'di skad ces bka' stsald to /dge slong zhi
132a4(1): ba sangs rgyas kyi sa la slong bar 'dod pa byang chub sems dpa' semasad pa' chen pos sbyin pa'i pha rold tu phyind pa la brtson par bya'o /dge slong ci ltar spyad pas bdag sbyin pa'i pha rold tu phyi nad pa yongs su rdzogs par
132a5(1): gyurd zhes na /byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen pos sbyin pa'i ngo bo nyid myi dmyigs par bya'i /sbyin pa myi sbyin par myi bya'o /sbyin gnas kyi ngo bo nyid myi dmyigs bar bya'i /sbyin pa'i gnas myi btsal ba ni myi
132a6(1): bya'o /sbyin bdag gi ngo bo nyid dmyigs par myi bya'i /sbyin pa bdag tu myi 'gyur par ni myi bya'o /de las kyi rang bzhin myi dmyigs par bya'i /las bdag gir bya ba dang 'gal bar myi bya'o /slab pa'i ngo bo nyid myi dmyig par bya'i /
132a7(1): sangs rgyas kyi ye shes la ni myi slab par myi bya'o /sbyin pa de yang byang chub tu yongs su bsngo' bar byas te /byang chub kyi ngo bo nyid dmyigs par mi bya' /bsngo' ba'i ngo bo nyid kyang dmyigs par myi bya //dge ba'i rtsa ba rnams kyi bsngo' bar

197a3(1): yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyis kun nas mngon tu gyur pa gang yin/sangs rgyas pa bcom ldan 'das rnams kye zhing yongs su sbyong ba la myi 'jigs pa gang yin/sangs rgyas ma bcom ldan 'das rnams gyi sku mtha' yas pa gang yin/
197a4(1): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as kyi lhun kyis grub pa'i ye shes gang yin/sangs rgyas bcomaldan 'das kyi bsam gyis myi khyab pa gang yin/sangs rgyas rgyas bcom ldan 'das ranams kyi kun tu gzigs pa gang yin/sangs rgya bcom ldan

197a6(1): kyi spyi tshug ltar spyi mthong pa gang yin/kye rgyal ba'i sras sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis sems can yongs su smyind par mdzad par mdzad pa'i spyod pa'i gnas rnams gang yin/sangs rgyas ba com ldan 'das rnams
197a7(1): kyi sems can yongs su sbyind par mdzad pa'i spyi 'jigs pa rnams gang yin/de skad ces smras pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po kun tu bzang pos/'jam dpal gzhon nur gyurd pas la 'di skad ces smras so/'jam dpal
197a8(1): chos kyi rnams grangs 'di ni mthong bar dka'/rig par bya dka' ba ste/brtag par bya ba ma yin/rtog ge spyod yul ma yin par mos par dka' ma ste/sangs rgyas bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong mang po la bsnyen bkur byas pa'i sems can de dag chos kyi
197a9(1): rnams grangs 'di thos par 'gyur zhing/thos nas kyang mos par 'gyur ro/'jam dpal gyis smras pa/kye rgyal ba'i sras dmu long la mar me myi gzugs kyi dmyig dang ldan pa rnams la mar me gzugs te/kye rgyal ba'i sras de bzhin du byang chub
197a10(1): sems dpa' sems dpa' chon po gang dag bsod nams dang /ye shes kyi tshogs dpag tu myed pa bsags pa de dag gis phyir kho bo myi 'dri'o/gang dag bskald pa grangs myed par sems can dmyal pa dang /dud 'gro dang/yid dgas dang/shind
197b1(1): rje'i 'jig rten du gnas par gyurd pa'i las tshe 'di nyid la myong bar 'gyur ba de dag gi phyir kho bos dri'o/gang bdag mthong ba dang thos pa dang /bsnyen bkur byas pa don yod pa de dag gi phyir kho bos dri yis stond cig/kye rgyal ba'i sras chos
197b2(1): kyi rnam gras 'di la ma dris pa la bla na myed pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub ma smras/kye rgyal ba'i sras skye bo mang po la 'phan pa dang/skye bo mang po la bde ba dang/'jig rten la thugs rtse ba dang/skye bo phal po chen po dang/lha dang/
197b3(1): myi rnams kyi don dang/phan pa dang bde bar bya bar 'i phyir bstond cig/de skad ces smras pa dang/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po kun tu bzang pos/'jam dpal gzhon nur gyurd pa la 'di skad ces smras so/'jam dpal
197b4(1): de la stond pa thogs pa myed pa ni de bzhin gshegs pa phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams so so'i red yang/gser gi kha dog gis 'dul ba'i lha dang/myi rnams dang/byang chub sems dpa'i rnams la de bzhin gshegs
197b5(1): pa gser gyi kha dog tu bstond pa la thogs pa myed do/nor bu rin po/che'i rgyal po skar 'od kyi kha dog gis 'dul pa rnams la/de bzhin gshegs pa rnams la nor bu rin po che'i rgyal po skar 'od kyi khadog tu stond pa la
197b6(1): thogs pa myed do/nor bu ron po che'i rgyal po'i glo ga gi sgron ma'i kha dog gis 'dul ba rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa nor bu'i rin po 'i rgyal po glog gi sgron ma kha dog tu bstond pa las thogs pa myed pa'o/nor bu rin po'i
197b7(1): rgyal po gnyi zla mar mye'i kha dog gis 'dul pa rnam sa la de bzhin gshegs pas nor bu rin po che'i rgyal bo nyi zla'i mar mye'i kha dog tu bstond pa lasogs pa myed do/nor bu rnon po che'i rgyal po snang ba thams cad bsdus pa kha dog gis 'dul
197b8(1): pa la rnams la de bzhin gsheg pa nor bu rin po che snang ba thams cad bsdud pa'i kha dog tu stond pa'i la thogs pa myed do//nor shun po che'i dbang gyi rgyal po'i kha dog gis 'dul ba rams la de bzhin de bzhin gshegs pa nor pu rin po'i rgyal po kha dog
197b9(1): tu stond pa la thogs pa myed do//nor bu rin po che'i rgyal po ze pa can gyi kha dog gis 'dul ba rnam sa la/de bzhin gshegs pa ni bu rin po che'i rgyal po the pa can gyis kha dog tu bstond pa la thogs pa myed de/nor bu rin po che'i rgya la po
197b10(1): seng ge ral pa can gyi kha dog gis bdul pa rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa nor bu rin po che'i rgyal pho seng ge ral pa can gyis kha dog tu pa stond pa thogs pa myed do/nor pu chen po'i seng ge ral pa can rgyal mtshan gyi rtse mo'i kha dog tu 'dul ba rnams la
198a1(1): //de bzhin gshegs pa nor bu rin po che'i rgyal po seng ge'i rgyal mtshan gyi rtse mo'i kha dog tu bstond pa thogs pa myed do/nor bu rin po che'i rgyal bo rgya byin thogs pa'i 'dul ba rnams la/de bzhin gshegs pa norabu rin po'i rgyal
198a2(1): po brgya byin thogs pa'i kha dog tu bstond pa la thogs pa myed do /nor bu rin po che'i rgyal pa lha thams cad 'od kyi kha dog gis 'dul ba rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa nor ba rin po'i rgyal po lha thams cad kyi 'od kyis kha dog tu bstond pa
198a3(1): la thogs pa myed do/nor ba rin po'i rgyal po chu dang bar byed pa'i kha dog gis 'dul ba rnam sa la de bzhin gshegs pa nador bu rin po'i rgyal po chu dngabar byed pa'i kha dog tu bstond ba la thogs pa myed do//nor bu rin po che'i rgyalabo rdo rje'i 'od kyis

198a7(1): nor pu rin po che'i rgyal po mthon ka chen po'i kha dog gis 'dul ba rnam sa la de bzhin gshegs pa nor bu ring po'i rgyal po mthon ka chen pi'i kha dog tu bstond pa la thogs par myed donor bu rin po che rgyal po rdo rje'i snying po'i kha dog gis 'dul

198a9(1): rgyal po dge ba bkod pa'i kha dog tu bstond pa las thogs pa myed donor bu rin po'i 'tsho'i rgyal po ma chags pa dkod pa'i snying po'i kha dog gis 'dal ba rnams la de bzhin bshegs pa nor bu rin po che'i rgyal po ma chags bkod pa'i
198a10(1): snying po'i kha dog tu bstond pa ma thogs pa myed donor bu ni rin po che'i rgyal po mu tig dkar posa'i kha dog gis 'dul pa nams la de bzhin gshegs par nor bu rin po che'i rgyal pa mu tig dkar po'i kha dog tu bstond ba la thogs myed
198b1(1): /nor bu rin po che'i rgyal bo mu tig mthon ka'i mdog gis mngon pa rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa nor bu rin po che'i rgyal po mu tig mthon ka'i kha dog tu bstond pa la thogs pa myed do /nor bu rin po che'i rgyal po mu tig
198b2(1): gser lta bu'i kha dog gis 'dul ba rnams la /de bzhin gshegs pa nor ba rin po che'i rgyal bo mu tagi gser lta bu'i kha dog tu bstond pa la thogs myed do //nor bu ran po che'i rgyal po gser gis kha dog gis 'dul ba rnams la /

198b4(1): de bzhin gsheg sa nor bu rin po che'i rgyal pa bsod nams kyi tshogs kyi rgya mtsho' grangs myed pa rjes su sgrogs pa'i kha dog tu bstond pa lasogs pa myed do /nor bu rin po che'i rgyal po ye shes kyis tshogs rgya mtsho' grangs med pa rjes su

200a3(1): shing thams cad kyi rdul bsod nams kyi tshogs rgya 'tsho grangs myed pa'i bye brag bstod pa /gzhan dang gzhan gcig la zhi gnas thams cad kyi rdul snyed kyi pha rold tu phyind pa rgya mtsho grangs myed pa'i bye brags bstand pa la gzhan dag mkhyend pa la thogs pa med

201a3(1): pa'i mtshan de re yang /phyogs bcu'i 'jig rtend gyi khams rab 'byam thamasacad du 'jig rten gyi khamsre re dang /gling re rer yab /thogs pa myed pa /sgrib pa myed pa /dbyed myed pa /nam mkha' khyad par 'gengs pa

201a5(1): thams cad dang /'jig rten gi khams re re dang /glid re re yang thogs pa myed pa /sgrib pa med pa /dbyer myed pa /namam mkha' khyab par 'gengs pa'i /sems can yongs su smyinang par mdzad pa /

201a8(1): thams cad du /'jig rten gyi khamam re re dang /gling re re yang thogs pa myed /sgrib pa med pa /nam mkha' khyab pa 'gengs par /sems can yongs su smrind par mdzad pa/khyu mchog tu gyurd pas/phyi ma'i mtha'i mu
201a9(1): 'i bskald par byind kyis rlob pa'o /de ci'i phyir zhe na /ma dres pa'i chos dang ldan pa'i phyir te /ma 'dres pa'i chos dang ldan pa' ni /smon lam gyi bstobs kyis byind kyis rlabs pa'i phyir ro//de bzhin gshegs pa'i
201a10(1): sems can yongs su smyind par mdzad pa khyu mchog tu gyurd pa /mdor bstand pa yin te /rgyas par ni phyi ma'i mtha'i mu'i bskald par byind kyi rlabs par bstand par myi nus so /'jam dpal de bzhin gshegs pa'i kun nas
201b1(1): mngon tu 'gyurd pa'i /phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gi khams rab 'byam thams cad du 'jig rten gyi khams re re dang /gling re re dang /rdul phra rab re re la ste /rdul phra rab kyi rdul gyi phyogs re re la /byang chub sems dpa' rgya mtsho rab tu
201b2(1): 'khod pa ni mang gi /'jig rten gyi khams lnga bcu'i rdul phra rab kyi rdul snyed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho grangs myed pa dag la rdul phra rab kyi ched du ste /byang chub sems dpa'ade dag ni /sems can dang 'jig rten gyi kham+sa
201b3(1): dang /gling dang /ri rab dang /ri rab chen po dang /khor yug dang /khor yug chen po dang /grong dang /grong khyer dang /grong rdal dang /ljongs dang /yul khor dang /rgyal po'i pho brang 'khor dang /'bab chu dang /tshe 'u dang /
201b4(1): mtsho bran dag gang la yang gnod pa dang /sgrib pa myed de /sdon ci lta bar phyis kyang de bzhin du bya ba dag 'jug ste /de bzhin gshegs pa ma 'dres pa'i chos dang ldan pa'i phyir ro /bsam pa ci lta ba dang /
201b5(1): 'dul ba ci lta ba'i lha dang /gnod sbyin dang /dri za dang /lha ma yin dang /nam mkha' lding dang /myi 'am ci dang /lto phye chen po dang /brgya byin dang /tshangs pa dang /'jig rten skyong ba dang /
201b6(1): myi 'am myi ma yin dang /byang chub sems dpa' de dag gis kyang 'di ltar kun na /de bzhin gshegs pa gnya' shing gang btsam na bzhugs par mngon gsum du mthong ngo /de ci'i phyir zhe na /ma 'dres pa dang 
201b7(1): ldan pa'i phyir ro /'jam dpal 'di lta ste dper na /zla ba yar ngo bzhin no /rgyad phyed kyi dus na /mdzam bu'i gling gi skyes pa dang /bud myed dang /khye 'u dang /bu mo thams cad kyis /zla ba'i dkyil 'khor 'dun na
201b8(1): 'dug par mthong yang /zla ba'i dkyil 'khor ni myi rtog rnam par myi rtog /mod kyis /'on kyang ma 'dres pa'i chos dang ldan pa'i phyir myi rtog cing /lhun kyi grub par bya ba'i phyir lta bu 'di dag 'byung ngo /'jam dpal de bzhin du de 
201b9(1): bzhin gshegs pa yang phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams rab 'byam thams cad kyi sems can gyi bsam pa ci lta ba dang /'dul pa ci lta ba de dag gis de bzhin gshegs pa mdun na bzhugs pa mthong yang /de bzhin gshegs
201b10(1): pa yang myi rtog rnam par myi rtog mod kyis /'on kyang ma 'dris pa'i chos dang ldan pa'i phir myi rtog cing /lhund kyi grub par mdzad pa 'di lta bu 'di dag 'byung ngo/'jam dpal de bzhin gshegs pa'i zhing yongs su sbyong ba myi
202a1(1): /'jig pa'i /de bzhin gshegs pa phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams rab 'byam thams cad kyi 'jig rten gyi khams re re dang /rdul phra rab kyi rdul re re la 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi rdul snyed kyis 'jig
202a2(1): rten gyi khams rgya mtsho la 'jug go /de la 'jig rten gyi khams la la ni bsod nams kyi tshogs rgya mtsho grangs myed pa'i bye brag pas nad pa' ye shes kyi rnam par spyod do /'jig rten gyi khams la lani ye shes kyi tshogs rgya mtsho grangar
202a3(1): myed pa'i bye brag pas nadapa'i ye shes kyis rnam par spyod do /'jig rtan gyi khams rgya mtsho la la ni pha rold tu phyind pa rgya mtsho grangs myed pa'i byed brag bstand pa'i ye shes kyis rnam par sbyod do /'jig rten gyi khamasargya mtsho'
202a4(1): la la ni sa rgya mtsho grangs myed pa'ibye brag bstand pa'i ye shes kis sbyor ro /'jig rten gyi khams la ni gzungs rgya mtsho grangs myed pa'i bya brag bstand pa'i ye shesakyi rnam par sbyor ro /'jig rten gyi thams rgya mtsho la la ni
202a5(1): ting nge 'dzind rgya mtsho grangs myed pa'i phye brag bstand pa'i ye shes  kyis sbyor ro /'jig rten gyis khams rgya mtsho la la ni rnam par pa thard pa rgya mtsho grangs myed ba'i bye brag bstand pa'i ye shesal sbyor ro /'jig
202a6(1): rten gyi khams rgya mtsho la lani mngon par shes pa rgya mtsho' mngon par shes pa'i ye shes kyis rnam pa sbyor ro /'jig rten gyi kha mas rgya mtsho la la ni smon lam rgya mtsho grangs myed pa'i be brag bstand pa'i ye shes
202a7(1): par sbyor ro /'jig rten gi khams rgya mtsho la la ni brtson 'grus brtsom pa rgya mtsho grangs myed pa'i bye brag bstand pa'i yeshes kyis rnam par sbyor ro /'jig rtan gyi khams rgya mtsho la la nidabugs 'byind pa rnyed brgya mtsho grangs
202a8(1): myed pa'i ye shes kyis rnam par sbyor do /'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho la ni byang chub sems dpa' la 'jug pa rgya mtsho grangs myed pa' bye brag bstand pa'i ye shas kyis rnam par sbyor ro/'jig rten gyi khams rgya mo la la
202a9(1): ni de bzhin gshegs pa la 'jug pa rgya mtsho grang myed pa'i bye brag bstanang pa'i ye shes kyis rnam parasbyor ro /'jig rten gyi khams rgyamatsho la la ni sems can gyi bsam pa dang spyod pa la 'jug pa rgya mtsho gangs myed pa'i bye brag
202a10(1): bstand pa'i ye shes kyis rnam bar spyod do /'jig rten gyi khams mtsho la la ni 'jig rten gyi khams la 'jug pa rgya mtsho grangar myed pa'i bye brag bstanadapa'i ye shes kyis rnam paraspyor ro /'jig rten gyi kham rgya mtsho
202b1(1): la la ni bskald pa la 'jug pa rgya mtsho grangs myed pa'i bye brag bstand pa'i ye shes kyis rnam par sbyong ngo /'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho la la ni dus gsum du bstand pa rgya mtsho grangs myed pa bye brag bstand pa'i ye shas kyis
202b2(1): rnam par sbyor ro /'jig rten gyi khams la la ni dus gsum la 'jug pa rgya mtsho grang myed pa'i bye brag bstanadapa'i ye shes kyis rnam par spyod do/'jig rten gyi khams rgyamatsho la la ni myi skyob sems bskyed de rgya mtsho grangs
202b3(1): myed pa'i ye shes kyis rnam bar sbyong ngo /'jig rten gi khams rgya mtsho' la la ni dbyer myed pa'i rgya mtsho grangs myed pa'i ye shes kyis rnamapar sbyong ngo /de bzhin gshegs pa ni gnas gang nas myi g.yo'o /de ci'i phyir zhe na//
202b4(1): myi g.yo' ba'i ye shes brnyed pa'i phyir ro /'jam dpal de bzhin gshegs pa 'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongs su sbyor ba la myi 'jigs pa mdor bstand pa yin te/rgyas par ni phyi ma'i mtha'i mu'i bskald par byind kyis rlabs kyang bstand par myi
202b5(1): nus so /'jam dpal de bzhin bshegs pa'i sku mtha' yas pa'i phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams rab 'byam thams cad kyi 'jig rten gyi khams re re dang /gling re re la ste/'jig rten gyi khams lnga bcu'i rdul
202b6(1): phra rab kyi rdul snyed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho stong rgyang grangs myed pa'i rdul phra rab kyi rdul rnams ni rab tu nyung gis/de bzhin gshegs pa byang chub sems dpa'i 'khord kyi dkyil 'khor rgya mtsho ni ches
202b7(1): mang ngo /'jig rten gyi khams dang bcu'i rdul snyed kyis 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mo rdul phra rab kyi rdul rnalas ni rab tu nyung gis /byang chub sems dpa'i 'khaurd kyis dkyil 'khor re re 'i byang chub sems dpa' bsod nams kyi
202b8(1): tshogs rgya mtsho grangs myed pa'i gzhan dang gzhan gyis /sangs rgyas la lnga pa mngon gsum du gyurd pa thob pa dag ni ches mang ngo /'jig rten gyi mkhas lnga bcu'i rdul rnyed kyis 'jig rten gyi khamasang rgya mtsho'i rdul phra rab kyi rdularnams
202b9(1): ni rab tu nyung gi/byang chub sems dpa'i khord kyi dkyil 'khor re re'i byang chub sems dpa' rgya mtsho ye shes kyi tshogs rgya mtsho grang myed pa gzhan dang gzhan gyis sangs rgyas la lta ba mngon tu gyurd pa thob pa dag niches mang ngo /
202b10(1): 'jig rten gyi khams lnga bcu'i rdul rnyed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho' grangs myed pa'i rdul phra rab kyi rdul ni rab tu nyung gi byang chub sems dpa'i kho re re re'i byang chub sems pa'i rgya mtsho' grangs myed pa gzhan dang gzhanagyis sangs rgyas lta ba mngon
203a1(1): /tu gyurd pa thob pa dag ni ches mang ngo /'jig rten gyi khams lnga bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho' grangs myed pa'i rdul phra rab gyi rdul rnams ni rab tu nyung gi/byang chub sems dpa'i 'khord kyi dkyil 'khor re re'i byang chub sems dpa' 
203a2(1): rgya mtsho grangs byed pa gzhan dang gzhan gyis sangs rgyas la lta ba mngon tu gyurd pa thob pa dag na ches mang ngo /'jig rtenagyi khams lnga bcu'i rdul rnyed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams rgya cho grangs myed pa'i rdul phrarab kyi rdul rnams ni rab tub gyis/byang chub sems dpa'i
203a3(1): 'khord kyi dkyil 'khor rere'i byang chub sems dpa'i rgya mtsho'i gzungs rgya mtsho' grangs myed gzhan dang gzhan gis sangs rgyas la lta ba mdon tu gyurd pa thob pa dang ni ches mang ngo /'jig rtan gyi khams lnga bcu'i rdul snyed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams kyi rgya mtsho grangs myed
203a4(1): pa'i rdul phra rab kyi rdul rnams ni rab1tu gi/byang chub sems dpa'i khord kyi dkyil 'khor re de'i byang chub sems dpa' rgya mtsho'i ting nge 'dzind rgya mtsho grangs myed pa gzhan dang gzhan gyi sangs rgyas la lta ba mngon1 gyurd pa thob pa dag ni ches
203a5(1): mang ngo 'jig rten gyi khams lnga bcu'i rdus rnyed kyi 'jig rten gyi kha mas rgya mtsho 'grangs myed pa'i rdul phra rab kyi rdus rnams ni rab tu 'ur gi/byang chub sems dpra 'khord kyi dkyil 'khor re re'i byang chub sems dpa'argya mtsho' rnam par
203a6(1): mthad pa rgya mtsho grangs myed pa gzhad dang gzhan dag gis sangs rgyas la lta ba mon tu gyurd pa thob pa dag ni chos mang ngo /'jig rten gyi khams lnga btsu'i rdul rnyed kyi 'jig rten gyi khyams rgya mtsho' grangs myed pa'i rdul phra dang 
203a7(1): kyi rdul rnams ni rab tu nyung gi/byang chub sems dpa' 'khord kyi dkyil 'khor de re'i byang chub sems dpa'i rgya mtsho' so so yang dag par rig pa rgya mtsho grangs myed pagazhan dang gzhan dag gyis sangs rgyas la lta ba mngon tu gyurd pa thob pa
203a8(1): dag ni ches mang ngo /'jig rten kyi khams lnga bcu'i rdul rnyed kyi 'jig rtan gyi khams rgya mtsho grangs myed pa'i rdul bra rab kyi rdul rnams ni rab tub gi/byang chub sems dpa'i 'khord kyi dkyil 'khor rere'i byang chub sems dpa'irgya mtsho
203a9(1): mngon par zhend pa rgya mtsho grangs myed gzhan dang gzhan gyis sangs rgyas la rnams la lta ba mngon tu gyur pa thob pa dag ni ches mang ngo //'jig rten gyi khams lngas brgya'i rdul rnyed kyi 'jig stan gyi khams rgya mtsho grangs med pa rdul phra rab kyi rdul
203a10(1): rnams ni rab tu nyung gi/byang chub sems dpa'i khord kyi dkyil 'khor de ra'i byang chub sems dpa'i rgyal mtsho'i smon lam gyi phye brag rgya mtsho grangs med pa gzhan dang gzhan gyis sang rgyas la lta ba mngon du gyurd pa thob pa dag ni ches 
203b1(1): 'jig rten gyi khams lnga bcu'i chu rnyed kyis 'jig gi rten gyi khams rgya mtsho grangs myed pa'i rdul phra rab kyi rdul rnams ni rab tu nyung gi/byang chub sems dpa'i 'khord kyi dkyil 'khor de re re'i byang chub sems dpa' rgya mtsho'i bsod nams kyi tshogs kyis bye brag
203b2(1): gzhan dang gzhan thob pa de dag/bsod nams kyi tshogs kyi bye brag gzhan dang gzhan thob pa ni/stobs skyed pa'i byind kyi rlabs kyis ca/de bzhin gshegs pa gzhan dang gzhan mthong ba dag ni ches mang ngo/'jig rten gyi khams lnga ba bcu'i rdul rnyed
203b3(1): kyi khams rgya mtsho grangs myedapa'i rdul phra rab kyi rdus rnams ni rab tu nyung ga/byang chub sems dpa'i 'khaurd kyi dkyil 'khor re re'i byang chub sems pa'i rgya mtsho'i ye shes kyi tshogs kyi bye brag gzhang dang gzhan dag la thob pa de dag ye shes kyi
203b4(1): tshogs kyi bye brag gzhan dag gzhan thob pa ni stobs skyed pa'i byind kyi blabs kyis de bzhin gshegs pa gzhand pa gzhan mthong ba dag ni chos mang ngo/'jig rten gya khams lnga ba bcu'i rdus rnyed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho grangs myed pa rdul
203b5(1): phra rab kyi rdul rnams ni rab tu nyung gi/byang chub sems dpa'i 'khord kyi/dkyil 'khor re re'i byang cub sems dpa' rgya mtsho pha rold du phyind pa'i bye brag gzhan dang gzhan thob pa de dag pha rold tu phyind
203b6(1): ba'i bye brag gzhan dang gzhanathob pa'i stobs skyed pa'i byind kyis rlabs kyis de bzhin gsheg gas pa gzhan dang gzhan mthong ba dag ni ches mang ngo/'jig rten gyi khams lnga ba rdula'i rdul rnyed kyi 'jig rten kyi khams rgya mtsho grangs myedapa'i rdul
203b7(1): phra rab kyi rdus rnams ni rab tu nyung bi/byang chub sems dpa'i 'khaurd kyi dkyil 'khor re re'i/byang chub sems dpa' rgya mtsho sa'i bye brag gzhan dang gzhand thob pa de dag sa'i bye brag gzhan dang ga zhan thob pa'i bstobs pa skyed pa'i byind kyis
203b8(1): rlabs kyis de bzhin gshegs pa gzhan dang gzhan 'thong pa dag ni ches mang ngo/'jigs rten gyi khams lnga bcu'i rdul rnyed kyi 'jig rtan gyi khams rgya mtsho grangs med pa'i rdul phra rab kyi rdul rnams ni rab tu nyung gi/byang chub sems
203b9(1): dpa'i 'khord kyi dkyil 'khor re re' byang chub sems dpa' rgya 'tsho gzugs kyi bye brag gzhan dang gzhan mthong pa de dag stobs skyed pa'i byind kyis rlabs kyis/de bzhin gshegs pa gzhand dang gzhan mthong pa dag ni ches mang do/
203b10(1): 'jig rten gyi khams lnga bcu'i rdzus rnyed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho grangs myed pa'i rdul phra rab kyi rngul rnams ni rab tu nyud gi/byang chub sems dpa'i 'khord kyi dkyil 'khaurd re re'i/byang chub sems dpa'i rgya mtsho'i
204a1(1): //ting nge 'dzind kyi bye brag gzhan dang gzhan thob pa de dag ting nge 'dzind gyi bye brag gzhan dang thob pa'i so pas bskyed pa'i byind kyis rlabs kyis /de bzhin gzhan gshegs pa gzhan dang gzhan mthong pa dag ni ches mang ngo /'jig rten
204a2(1): gyi khams lnga bcu'i rdul rnyed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho grangs myed pa'i rdul phra rab kyi rdul rnams ni rab tu nyung gi/phyang chub sems dpa'i 'khord kyi dkyil 'khor re re'i dbyang chub sems dpa'i rgya mtsho rnam par mthard pa'i bye brag gzhan dang gzhan
204a3(1): dgod pa de dag rnam par thard pa'i bye brag gzhan rang gzhan thob pa'i stobs skyed pa'i byind kyi rlabs kyis de bzhin gshegs pa gzhan dang /gzhan mthong ba dag ni ches mang ngo /'jug rten gyi khams lnga bcu'i rdul gyi 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mo grangs myed
204a4(1): 'i rdul phra rab kyi rdul ni rab tu nyung gi/byang chub sems dpa'i 'khod kyi dkyil 'khor re re'i byang chub sems dpa' so so yang dag par rig pa'i bye brag gzhan dang /gzhan thob pa de dag so so yang dag par rigs pa'i bye brag
204a5(1): gzhan dang gzhan thob pa'i stobs bskyed pa'i byind la rlabs kyis de bzhin gshes pa mthong pa dag ni ches mang ngo /'jig rten gyi khams lnga bcu'irdul snyed kyi/'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho grangs myed pa'i rdul phra rab kyi rdul rnams
204a6(1): ni rab tu nyung gi/byang cub sems dpa'i 'khord kyi dkyil 'khor re re'i dbyang chub sems dpa'i rgya mtsho mngon par shes pa'i bye brag gzhan dang gzhan thob pa de dag mngon par shes pa'i bye ba gzhan dang gzhan thob pa'istobs skyed pa'i byind kyi
204a7(1): rlabs kyis de bzhin gshegs pa gzhan dang gzhan mthong pa dag ni ches mad ngo /'jig rten gyi khams snga bcu'i rdu snyed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams rgya ma tsho grangs myed pa'i rdul pha rab kyi rdul rnams ni rab tu nyung gi/byang chub sems dpa'i 'khord kyis dkyil
204a8(1): 'khor re re'i byang chub sems dpa' rgya mtsho smon lam gyi bye brag gzhan dang gzhanathob pa de dag smon lam gyi bye brag gzhanadang gzhan thob pa'i stobs skyed pa'i byind kyi rlabs kyis de bzhin gsheg pa gzhan dang gzhan thob dag ni ches mang ngo /
204a9(1): 'jam dpal de bzhin du sbyar te/rten pa rnams dang /do mtshar gyi 'du shes rnams dang /spyod pa rnams dang /dge ba'i bshes gnyen rnams dang /brtson 'grus brtsom pa rnams dang /dbugs 'byind pa rnyed pa rnams dang /
204a10(1): sems can yongs su smyind par mdzad pa rnams dang /tshul khrimam rnams dang /lung bstand pa rnams dang /byang chub sems dpa' dag 'jug pa rnams dang /de bzhin gshegs pa la 'jug pa rnams dang /sems can gyi bsam pa dang /
204b1(1): bskyod pa la 'jug pa rnams dang /'jig rten gyi khams la 'jug pa rnams dang/bskald pa la 'jug pa rnams dang /dus gsum bstand pa rnams dang /myi skyo ba'i sems skyed pa rnams dang /ye shes dbyer myed pa rnams dang /gzugs
204b2(1): rnams dang /sangs rgyas bstond pa dang /kun tu bzang po'i sems skyed pa rnams dang /kun tu bzang po'i spyod pa'i chos rnams dang /snying rje chen po thob pa rnams dang /byang chub kyi sems kyi rkyen rnams dang /dge ba'i bshes gnyen la gus pa'i
204b3(1): bya ba'i skyed pa rnams pa rnams dang /yongs su dag pa rnams dang /pha rold tu phyind pa rnams dang /ye shes rjes su rtogs pa rnams dang /so sor rtogs pa rnams dang /stobs skyed pa rnams dang /mnyam pa nyid kyi tshul gyis lam
204b4(1): rnams dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos 'chad pa rnams dang /kun tu bzung pa rnams dang /spobs pa rnams dang /ye shes kyi dbang rnams dang /gnas myed pa rnams dang /sems mnyam pa nyid dang /ye shes mngon par bsgrub pa rnams
204b5(1): dang /sbruld pa rnams dang /byind kyi rlabs pa rnams dang /dbugs 'byind pa chen po rnyed pa rnams dang /zab mo la 'jug pa rnams dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos la rtend rnams dang /sems skyed pa la brtan pa rnams

208b4(1): 'das pa dang /ma 'ongs pa dang /da ltar 'byung ba'i 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho gang dag yod pa de dag de bzhin gshegs pa'i spu'i zhud pu re rer 'jig cing chags so /de ci'i phyir zhe na/mtha' dang dbus myed pa'i chos nyid rnyes
208b5(1): pa'i phyir ro//'jam dpal de bzhin gshegs pa'i mdor bstand pa yin te rgyas par phyi ma'i mtha'i pa skald par byind kyis brlab kyang bstan da par myi nus so /'jam dpal de la de bzhin gshegs pa'i kun tu gzigs
208b6(1): pa ni phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten kyi khams rab 'byam thams can na /sems can 'chi 'pho ba dang /skye ba dang /bde 'gror bla dang /ngan 'gror 'gro pa rnams la de bzhin gshegs pas gzigs te /de bzhin du gtsug
208b7(1): tor gyis kyang gzigs so /de bzhin du mdzod spus kyang gzigs so /de ba zhin du mtshan re rer kyang gzigaso /de bzhin du dpa' byad bzang po re rer kyang gzigs so /de bzhin
208b8(1): de bzhin du phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams rab 'byam thams cad du sems can 'chi 'pho ba pa dang /skye ba dang /ba de 'gror 'gro ba dang /ngan 'gror 'gro ba dang /dus gcig tu gzigs so /'jam dpal de bzhin gshegs pa'i
208b9(1): ta kun tu gzigs so mdor bshad pa yin te /rgyas par ni phyi ma'i mtha'i mu'i bskald par byind kyis rlabs kyang bstand par myi nus so /'jam dpal de la de bzhin gshegs pa'i grags pa ni /'jam dpal de bzhin bshegs pa'i
208b10(1): phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyis khams rab 'byam thams can dang /'jig rten gyi khams re rer dang /gling re rer dang /rdul phra rab de la /'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi rdul rnyed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho dag 'jug cing /

209a6(1): dang la de la de bzhin gshegs pa'i bye brag myed cing /myi gnas pa ni 'jam dal de bzhin gshegs pa 'jig rten gyi khams 'di na tha dad pa /'jig rten gyi khams gzhan dag tu gnas pa yin no /de bzhin ga
209a7(1): gshegs pa gling 'di na tha dad pa gling gzhan du gnas pa ma yin no /de bzhin gshegs pa khyim 'di na tha dad la /khyim gzhan du gnas pa ma yino /de bzhin gshegs pa lha'i rigs gzhan na /lha'i rigs gzhan du gnas pa ma
209a8(1): yin no /de bzhin du dud 'gro'i skye gnas su skyes pa 'di na tha dad pa la /dud 'gro bo skye gnas su skyes pa gzhan dag tu gnas pa ma yin no /de pa zhin gshegs pa yid dgas kyi 'gro ba 'di dag la tha dad la /yi dgas 'gro ba
209a9(1): gzhan du gnas pa ma yin pa /de bzhin gshegs pa sems can dmyal ba'i 'gro ba 'di dag tha dad la /sems can dmyal ba'i 'gro pa gzhan du gnas pa ma yinano /de bzhin gshegs pa 'dod pa'i khams na tha dad pa /gzugs
209a10(1): kyi khams su gnas pa ma yin no /'jam dpal de bzhin gshegs pa'i dbyer myed cing /myi gnas pa mdor bstand pa yin te /rgyas par ni phyi ma'i mtha'i mu'i bstand par byind kyis rlabs kyang bstand par myi nus so /'jam dpal
209b1(1): de la de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku thogs pa myed pa ni phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams ram 'byam dag na /sangs rgyas kyi zhing gar dag yod pa de dag ni nam mkha'i khams ci 'dra ba'i mtha' ni yod srid kyis /dus gsum gyi phyogs mtha' yam 
209b2(1): pa dag na rgyal ba'i gtsug tor 'byung ba /'jig rten snang ba'i rgyal ba'i gtsug tor gyi mtha ni phyogs rnams na myi rnyed do //de dag ni nam mkha' la ci 'dra ba'i mtha' ni yong srid kyis /de bzhin (de nyid yid min pa'i brtan mig mthas)rgyal ba'i mdzod spu dang /de bzhin du mtshan re de 
209b3(1): dang /de bzhin du dpe' phyad bzang po brgya bcu' dang /de bzhin du spu'i khung bu re re'i mtha' ni phyogs rnams na myi rnyer do /lha dang myi dang /byang chub sems dpa' gang dag chos kyi tshul bstand pa ci lta bus 'dul ba de dag gis /chos kyi tshul bstand 
209b4(1): ci lta bus 'dul de dag gis /chos gyi tshul bstand pa de 'dra ba dag 'byung bar shes ste /de la lha dang /myi dang /byang chub semasadapa' kha cig gis ni gtsug tor las bsod nams kyi tshogs rgya mtsho grangs myed pa'i bye brag bstand

210a3(1): 'i sems skyed pa rgya mtsho grangs med pa bye brag bstand pa dag byung par shes so /lha dang /spyi dang /byang chub sems dpa' kha gcig gis ni gtsug tor nas dbyer med pa'i ye shes rgya mtsho grangs myed pa'i phye brag 
210a4(1): bstand pa dag 'byung par shes so/de bzhin du lha dang /myi dang /byang chub sems dpa' bsam ba ci lta ba dang /'dul pa ci lta ba bzhin thams /bcad dag ni rdul rnyed dag gis sems can gyi skad gcig la de bzhin du mdzod
210a5(1): spu dang /de bzhin du mtshan re re dang /de bzhin du dpa' byad bzang po re re dang ba spu'i khur bu re re nas chos tha dad pa rgya mtsho grangs myed pa'i bye brag bstand pa dag 'byung bar shes so /'jam dpal de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku 
210a6(1): thob pa'i myed pa mdor bstand pa yin te/rgyas par ni phyi ma'i mtha'i mu' bskald par byind kyis rlabs kyang bstand par myi nus so /'jam dpal del de bzhin gshegs pa'i spyi tshug ltar myi mthong ba ni /'jam dpal 'jig rten
210a7(1): gyi khams lnga bcu'i rdul rnyed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho dag na /rdul phra rab kyi rdul yod pa dag ni rab tu nyung gi /de bzhin gshegs pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i 'khord kyi dkyil 'khor rgya mtsho dag ni ches mang ngo /
210a8(1): de la byang chub sems dpa' kha cig gis gi phun ni dpag tshad bcu dang /nyi shu dang /gsum bcu dang /bzhi bcu'o/byang chub sems dpa' kha gcig gis dpag tshad brgya'o /byang chub sems dpa' kha gcig gis khrunani
210a9(1): dpag tshad brgya bcu dang /nyi shu dang/sum bcu dang /bzhi bcu 'o //'khor de nyid ni byang chub sems dpa' kha cig gis khrun ni dpag tshad bcu'o /'khor de nyid na /byang chub sems dpa' tha cig gi khrun ni dpag tshad
210b1(1): stong phrag bcu dang /nyi shu dang /gsum bcu dang /bzhi bcu dang /lnga bcu dang /byang chub sems dpa' kha cig gis ni khrun dpag tshad phyed pa'o /byang chub sems dpa'-i kha cig gi khrun ni dpag tshad bye ba phrag bcu dang /
210b2(1): nyi shu dang /sum bcu dang /bzhi bcu bcu dang /lnga bcu'o /byang chub sems dpa'i kha cig gi khrun ni dpag chad bye ba phrag brgya'o /byang chub sems dpas kha cig gi khrun ni dpag thad bye ba phrag stong ngo /byang chub sems dpa'
210b3(1): kha gcig gi khrun ni dpag tshad bya ba stong phrag bcu dang /nyi sha dang /sum bcu dang /bzhi bcu dang /lnga bcu yod do /de dag thams cad kyis phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams rab 'byam thams cad dang /gling thams cad
210b4(1): na dus cig tu de bzhin gshegs pa gnyis 'gyur du 'ong ngo /'jam dpal gal te byang chub sems dpa' rnam par mthard pa bsam gyis myi khyab pa la gnas pa gang la la zhig sems kyi skad cig sems kyi skad cig la /'jig
210b5(1): rten gyi khams lnga bcu'i rdul rnyed kyi gom pa dor bas sted gyi phyogs logs su song ste /gom pa re re kyang 'jig rten gyi khams lnga bcu'i rdus rnyed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtshor song la /de bzhin gshegs
210b6(1): pa'i spyi rgyug tshol zhing /de bzhin du 'jig rten gyi khams lnga' bcu'i rdul rnyed kyi bskald par rgya mtshor song yang /deng yang de bzhin gshegs pa gnyisa'agyur du mthong ngo /de ci'i phyir zhe na /ma 'dres pa'i
210b7(1): chos dang ldan pa'i phyir ro /'jam dpal de bzhin gshegs pa'i spyi rgyug 'di ltar myi mthong mdor bstand pa yin te /rgyas par ni phyi ma'i mtha'i mu'i bskald par byind kyis rlabs kyang bstand par myi nus so /'jam dpal de la de bzhin
210b8(1): gshegs pa'i sems can yongs su smyind par mdzad pa'i gnas pa rnams ni 'jam dpal phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams rab 'byam thams cad rdul phra rab tu rdul rnams ni rab tu nyung gi /'jig rten gyi khams re re dang /gling re re dang /rdul phra rab kyi rdul
210b9(1): re re dang /de bzhin gshegs pa sems can yongs su smyin par mdzad pa /spyod pa'i gnas rnams ni ches mang ngo /'jam dpal 'jig rten gi khams re re dang /gling rai re dang /rdul phra rab kye rdul re re la de bzhin gshegs pa sems chan yongs su smyind
210b10(1): par mdzad pa dang /spyod pa'i gnas ci snyed pa des ni /de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku las 'od zer gyi rgya mtshor 'gyur zhing /byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa'i glo glos 'asgrogs pa dag ches mang ngo /sems kyi skad cig sems kyi skad cig la de bzhin du gtsug tor dang
211a1(1): /mdzod spu dang /mtshan re re dang /dpe' byad bzang po re re dang /spu'i khung bu re re nas zhing thams cad kyi rdu gyi 'od zer rgya mtsho dag 'byung ste /de la 'od zer gyi rgya mtsho kha gcig ni bsod nams tshogs rgya mtsho grangs myed pa rjes su sgrogs so 
211a2(1): 'od zer gyi rgya mtsho kha cig ni ye shes kyi tshogs rgya mtsho grangs myed pa rjes su sgrog+sa go /'od zer gyi rgya mtsho kha cigani pha rold tu phyind pa rgya mtsho grangs myed pa rjes
211a3(1): ting de 'dzind rgya mtsho grangs myed pa'i rjes su sgrog+sa go /'od zer rgya mtsho kha cig ni ting nge 'dzind rgya mtsho grangs med pa rjes su sgrog+sa go //'od zer rgya mtsho kha gcig ni rnam par mthard pa rgya mtsho grangs med pa rje su sgrog+sa go /'od zer rgyu mtsho kha gcig ni so so
211a4(1): yang dag par rig pa rgya mtsho grags med pa rjes su sgrogs go /'od zer rgya mtsho kha gcig ni ting nge 'dzind Ipa rgya mtsho grangs med pa rjes su sgrog+sa go //'od zer rgya mtsho kha gcig ni smon lam rgya mtsho grangs myed pa rje su sgrog go//
211a5(1): 'od zer kha gcig ni brtson 'grus brtsom pa rgya mtsho grangs myed pa rjes su sgrog/go /'od zer kha gcig ni dbugs 'byind pa rnyed pa rgya mtsho grangs /myed pa rjes su sgrag go//'od zer rgya mtsho kha ga cig ni byang chub sems dpa' 
211a6(1): la 'jug pa rgya mtsho grangs med ba rjes su sgrog go//'od zer kha cig ni de bzhin gshegs pa la 'jug pa rgya mtsho grangs myed pa rjes su sgrog go //'od zer kha gcig ni sems can gyi bsam pa dang /spyod pa la 'jug pa rgya mtsho grangs myed pa 
211a7(1): rjes su sgrog go//'od zeng rgya mtsho kha gcig ni 'jig rten gyi khams la 'jug pa rgya mtsho grang myed pa rjes su sgrog go//'od zer rgya mtsho kha gcig ni /bskald pa la 'jug pa rgya mtsho gangs myed pa rjes su sgrog go /'od zer kha gcig ni dus gsum la 'jug
211a8(1): pa rgya mtsho grangs med pa rjes su sgrog go /'od zer rgya mtsho mtha' yas dus gsum bstand pa rgya mtsho grangs med pa rjes su sgrog go//'od zer rgya mtsho kha gcig ni spyi skyo ba'i sems skyed pa rgya mtsho grangs myed pa rjes su sgrog go//'od zer rgya mtsho kha gcig
211a9(1): ni ye shes mtha' yas pa rgya mtsho grangs myed parjes su sgrog go//thogs pa myed pa dang /sgrib pa myed pa dang /dper myed pa dang /nam mkha'i khams rgyas par 'gengs pa /phyi ma'i mtha'i mu'i bskald pa byind kyis rlob po /'jam dpal de bzhin gsheg+sa
211a10(1): pa'i sems can yongs su smind par mdzad pa /spyod pa'i gnas rnams mdor bstand pa yin te /rgyas par ni phyi ma'i mtha'i mu'i bskaladapar byind kyis rlabs kyang bstand par spyi nus so /'jam dpal de la de bzhin gshegs pa la sems can
211b1(1): yongs su smind par mdzad pa la myi 'jigs pa la ni 'jam dpal de bzhin gshegs pa skad cig skad cig la/phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams rab 'byam thams cad na/zhing thams cad kyi rdu rnyed kyi sems can rgya mtsho dag tu gcig tu yongs su smind par
211b2(1): mdzad do//de la sems can rgya mtsho kha gcig ni bsod nams kyi tshogs tha dad pa rgya mtsho grangs myed pa'i bye brag bstand pa'i ye shes kyis yongs su smind par mdzad/sems can rgya mtsho kha gcigani ye shes kyi tshogs tha dad pa rgya mo grangs myed pa'i bye brag pa stand
211b3(1): pa'i ye shes kyis yongs su smyand par mdzad do//sems can kha gcig ni pha rold tu phyind pa tha dad pa rgya mtsho grangs med pa'i bye brag bstand pa ye shes yongs su smind par mdzod do//sems can rgya mtsho kha cig ni sa tha dad pa rgya mtsho grangs
211b4(1): bstand pa'i ye shes kyis yogs su smind par mdzad do//sems chan rgya mtsho kha gcig ni gzungs tha dad pa rgya mtsho grang med pa'i bye brag bstanadapa'i ye shes kyis yongs su samind par mdzad do//sems rgya mtsho kha gcig ni ting nge 'dzind tha dad pa
211b5(1): rgya mtsho grangs med pa'i byed brag pas nad pa'i ye shes kyis yong su smind par mdzad do //sems can rgya mtsho kha gcag ni rnam par mthard pa tha dad pa rgya mtsho gangs med pa'i bye brag pas nad pa ni ye shes kyis yongs su smind par bshad do//
211b6(1): de sems can rgya mtsho kha gcig ni so so yang dag par rig pa tha dad pa rgya mtsho grags myed pa'i bye brag bstand pa'i ye shes kyis yangs su smind par mdzad de/sems can rgya mtsho kha gci ni mngon par shes pa tha dad pa rgya mtsho grang
211b7(1): myed pa'i ye shes kyis yos su smind par mdzad dosems can rgya mtsho kha gcis ni smon lam tha dad pa rgya mtsho grangs med pa'i bye brag bstand pa' ye shes kyis yongs su smyind par mdzad do//sems can rgya mtsho kha cig ni brtson 'grus brtsom pa tha dad pa
211b8(1): pa rgya mtsho grangs med pa'i bya brag bstand pa'i ye shes kis yongs su smind par mdzad//sems can rgya mtsho kha cag ni dbugs 'byind pa rnyed pa tha dad pa rgya mtsho gras med pa'i bye brag bstan pa'i ye shes kyis yongs su smyind par mdzad do
211b9(1): sems can rgya mtsho kha gcag ni byang chub sems dpa' la 'jug pa tha dad pa rgya mtsho gangs myed pa'i bye brag bstanang ba'i ye shes kyis yongs su sbyind par mdzad dosems cang rgya mtsho kha gcig ni de bzhin gshegs pa la 'jug pa tha dad pa ra rgya mtsho grangs
211b10(1): myed pa'i bye brag bstand pa'i ye shes kyis yongs du smyind par mdzad dosems chan rgya mtsho kha gcig ni sems can gyi bsam pa dang /spyod pa la 'jug pa tha dad rgya mtsho grangs med pa'i bye brag bstand pa' ye shes kyis yongs su smyind par
212a1(1): //mdzad do //sems can rgya mtsho kha gcig 'jig rten gyi khams la 'jug pa tha dad pa rgya mtsho la grangs med pa'i bye brag bstand pa'i ye shes kyis yongs su smyind par mdzad do //sems can rgya mo kha gcig ni bskald pa la 'jug pa tha
212a2(1): dad pa rgya mtsho grangs med pa'i bye brag pa stand pa'i ye shes kyis yong su smyind par mdzad dode bzhin du dus gsum la 'jug pa dang /rus gsum bstand pa dang /myi skyob 'i sems skyed pa dbyer myed pa'i ye shes kyis yongs du smyind par mdzad do//
212a3(1): de bzhin sdems can rnam par spruld pa dang /chos rnam par spruld pa dang/lus rnam par spruld pa dngasmon lam rnam par spruld pa dngaye shes rnam par spruld pa dngamangon par shes pa rnam par spruld pa
212a4(1): dngardzu 'phrul rnam par spruld pa dngastobs rnam par spruld pasarnam par rold pa'i ye shes rgya mtsho grangs myed pa'i bye brag bstand pa ye shes kyis/shand du gaskad gcig skad cig la sems can rgya mtsho' zhing thams cad kyi
212a5(1): rdul rnyed kyi dag rdul gcig la yongs su smyind par mdzad do'ajam dpal de bzhin gshegs pa'i sems can yongs su smyind par mdzas pa la myi 'jigs pa mdor bstand pa yin te/rgyas par ni phyi ma'i mtha'i mu'i
212a6(1): bskald par byind kyis rlabs kyang bstand par myi nus so'ajam dpal rigs kyi bu 'am rigs bu mo gang la la zhig gis bskald par brgya mtsho gang 'ga'i klung gi bye ma snyed kyis sems can dag la nyin gcig bzhin du
212a7(1): lha'i zas ro brgya dang ldan pa'i byin pa dang /de bzhin du lha'i gos rnams sbyin pa byin nas /rigs kyis bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo gzhan gzhan gang zhig gis/dog bstend pa gzhan la myi rtend dge ba bcu'i las kyi lam la ldan pa'i sangs rgyas
212a8(1): bcom ldan 'das kyis bslab pa la slabs sosnyam du bsam nas/nyin cig zan cig byin na /'di ni des ches bsod nams grangs myed pa skyed do'ajam dpal rigs kyis bu 'am rigs gyi bu mo gang la la zhig gi bskald pa gang
